### [1] [Self-Indexing Attention for Compression-Compatible Sparse Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13205)
*Xu Yang,Jiapeng Zhang,Zhangke,Changjian Chen,Yuxin Chen,Feiqiang Sun,Chengguang Xu,Feng Jin,Zhuo Tang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 Self-Indexing Attention，一种免训练框架，基于共享变换域符号-幅值表示，用 1-bit 索引统一 prefill 与 decode 检索，并兼容低比特 KV-cache 压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏长上下文推理需在 prefill 与 decode 两阶段都高效检索 token，但现有方法对两阶段采用不同检索策略，导致检索表示无法在整段推理中复用。

Method: 构建共享的变换域符号-幅值表示；以关键符号作为可复用的 token 级索引，统一用于分组 prefill 选择与 decode 检索；该表示无需额外 indexer 元数据即可兼容外部 KV-cache 压缩；利用 1-bit 索引通过现代加速器广泛支持的位运算实现高效检索。

Result: 在 5% 注意力密度下，LongBench 与 RULER 上接近稠密注意力；prefill 注意力算子最高加速 6.1x，decode 最高加速 10.3x；与 TurboQuant 及 DeepSeekV4-Flash 的实验验证了其对低比特 KV-cache 压缩和预训练稀疏注意力 indexer 的兼容性。

Conclusion: Self-Indexing Attention 以单一 1-bit 共享索引统一 prefill 与 decode 检索，在保持精度的同时显著提升效率，并具备与低比特 KV-cache 压缩及已有稀疏注意力 indexer 的良好兼容性。

Abstract: Sparse long-context inference requires efficient token retrieval in both prefill and decode. Existing methods often use different retrieval strategies for the two stages, preventing one retrieval representation from being reused throughout inference. We propose Self-Indexing Attention, a training-free framework built on a shared transform-domain sign-magnitude representation. The key signs provide a reusable token-level index for grouped prefill selection and decode retrieval, while the same representation remains compatible with external KV-cache compression without separate indexer metadata. This 1-bit index enables efficient retrieval through bitwise operations widely supported by modern accelerators. At 5% attention density, Self-Indexing Attention remains close to dense attention on LongBench and RULER and achieves up to 6.1x prefill and 10.3x decode attention-operator speedups. Experiments with TurboQuant and DeepSeekV4-Flash further demonstrate compatibility with low-bit KV-cache compression and pretrained sparse-attention indexers.

</details>


### [2] [Decoupling Error Attribution in Cloud-Native Graph-RAG: A Data Integrity Diagnostic Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13324)
*Shuai Yan,Yuhang Wu,Xiaodong Huang,Ke Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出三层解耦诊断框架，将Graph-RAG系统错误归因于推理损失、知识图谱缺陷与Cypher生成错误，实验表明数据完整性而非算法推理是主要性能瓶颈，并揭示了参数化知识掩蔽效应（PKME）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Graph-RAG研究通常假设数据质量完好，忽视了云原生数据库扰动对系统的严重冲击，缺乏对错误来源的正交归因与量化审计手段。

Method: 构建三层解耦诊断框架，将系统误差正交拆解为推理损失、知识图谱缺陷和Cypher生成错误三类；在藏东南地区时空生态知识图谱上，针对八种缺陷类型进行评估。

Result: 数据完整性是主导性性能瓶颈：结构缺陷使系统准确率由0.93降至0.39；观察到类掩蔽现象PKME，LLM利用内部记忆补偿断裂的检索路径，使表观查询生成错误缩减逾70%，掩盖真实存储退化并提高自动监控漏报风险。

Conclusion: 该工作为云信息融合系统中数据完整性的审计与优化提供了量化基础，并提示需警惕LLM内部参数化知识对缺陷监测的掩蔽效应。

Abstract: Graph-RAG systems often assume pristine data quality, overlooking the severe impact of perturbations in cloud-native databases. This paper proposes a three-layer decoupled diagnostic framework to orthogonally attribute system errors to reasoning loss, Knowledge Graph (KG) defects, and Cypher generation errors. Evaluated on a spatio-temporal ecological KG of the Southeastern Tibet region with eight defect types, results reveal that data integrity, rather than algorithmic reasoning, is the dominant performance bottleneck, with structural defects degrading system accuracy from 0.93 to 0.39. Crucially, we observe a masking-like phenomenon termed the Parametric Knowledge Masking Effect (PKME), suggesting LLMs compensate for broken retrieval paths using internal memory. This shrinks apparent query generation errors by over 70 percent, obscuring actual storage deterioration and increasing the risk of false negatives for automated monitoring. This work provides a quantitative foundation for auditing and optimizing data integrity in cloud-based information fusion systems.

</details>


### [3] [PCGNet: Unifying Shared and Specific Information for Fashion Matching Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13339)
*Shuiying Liao,P. Y. Mok*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出PCGNet，一种多目标图学习框架，将服装互补推荐中的商品兼容性与用户个性化偏好有机统一建模，通过对比互信息最大化、相关性感知邻居采样与可学习全局图增强提升稀疏噪声数据下的推荐效果，在两个基准数据集上四项指标均显著超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时尚领域互补服装推荐需同时满足用户个性化偏好与服装间兼容性，但现有模型存在两方面不足：一是将商品兼容性与用户偏好视为过度简化的解耦关系，忽视二者天然的复杂交互；二是数据驱动方法未针对真实时尚数据的稀疏性与噪声交互进行优化。

Method: PCGNet为多目标图学习框架：采用对比互信息最大化提取并对齐共享与视角特有模式，以刻画兼容性与个人偏好的复杂交互；引入相关性感知邻居采样与可学习全局图增强，从图中挖掘自监督信号以增强表示的稳定性与信息量；最终通过BPR排序损失与多视角互信息损失的联合优化生成推荐分数。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上的实验验证表明，PCGNet在全部四项评价指标上均显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: PCGNet通过图学习与多目标、自监督信号融合，有效统一建模兼容性与个性化偏好，缓解稀疏与噪声数据问题，验证了该框架在互补服装推荐任务上的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: In fashion domain, recommending complementary clothing items that match selected pieces is a crucial cross-selling technique that improves customer satisfaction. Nevertheless, fashion matching presents significant challenges, as recommendations must not only align with individual user fashion preferences but also ensure compatibility between garments. These challenges are twofold. First, existing models often assume an overly simplified decoupled relationship between product compatibility and personalized user preferences, overlooking the natural complexity between the two. Second, existing data-driven approaches are not optimized for real-world fashion data, which is typically sparse and characterized by noisy interactions. To address these challenges, we propose Personalized Compatibility Graph Network, a multi-objective graph learning framework that organically unifies the modeling of product compatibility and personal preferences. PCGNet uses contrastive mutual information maximization to extract and align shared and view-specific patterns, thereby capturing the complex interplay between compatibility and personal preferences. Moreover, we introduce a correlation-aware neighbor sampling and a learnable global graph augmentation, which enhance the model by incorporating self-supervised signals mined directly from the graph, ensuring more stable and informative representations. Finally, PCGNet generates recommendation scores through the joint optimization of BPR ranking loss and multi-view mutual information losses. Experimental validation on two benchmark datasets demonstrates that PCGNet significantly outperforming state-of-the-art methods across all four evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [4] [Mixture-of-Experts Language Models Can Be Strong and Efficient Retrievers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13486)
*Anubhav Shrestha,Safal Shrestha,Minwu Kim,Torsten Suel,Keith Ross*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究将MoE LLM用作检索器，证明MoE检索器在相近激活参数量下优于稠密检索器，并具备更强效果与更低编码成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于decoder-only LLM微调的检索器效果随主干规模增大而提升，但每个query和document都要经过完整模型，编码成本随模型规模增加；MoE LLM每token仅激活部分参数，虽广泛用于生成模型扩展，但作为检索器仍未充分探索。

Method: 用统一流程训练多个系列的MoE与稠密LLM检索器，在多样化数据集上评估，并在相同serving配置下测量query编码时间；进一步分析减少query编码时使用专家数量、无需重训练或重建索引的可行性，并与reranker组合比较。

Result: 在BEIR上，MoE检索器在相近激活参数量下比稠密检索器最高提升3.0 nDCG@10；最强MoE检索器之一以少59%激活参数量、低18% query编码时间匹配8B稠密检索器；减少query编码专家数可保留超过99%检索效果，同时将query编码时间最多降低26%；近期reranker相对强MoE首阶段仅带来有限增益，后者常匹配或超过所评估的rerank配置。

Conclusion: MoE LLM可作为强且高效的首阶段检索器，在效果与编码效率之间提供更优权衡，并支持部署时灵活调节专家使用以降低query编码延迟。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that fine-tuning decoder-only large language models (LLMs) for retrieval yields strong first-stage retrievers, with effectiveness improving as backbones grow in size. However, every query and document must pass through the full model, so encoding cost increases with model size. Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) LLMs activate only a subset of parameters per token and are widely used to scale generative models, yet remain underexplored as retrievers. We systematically study MoE backbones for retrieval by training MoE and dense LLMs from several families using the same procedure, evaluating them across diverse datasets, and measuring query encoding time under the same serving configuration. We show that MoE retrievers outperform dense retrievers with comparable active parameter counts by up to 3.0 nDCG@10 points on BEIR. One of our strongest MoE retrievers matches an 8B dense retriever with 59% fewer active parameters and 18% lower query encoding time. We further show that the number of experts used for query encoding can be reduced without retraining or re-indexing, retaining more than 99% of retrieval effectiveness while reducing query encoding time by up to 26%. Recent rerankers provide only modest additional gains over strong MoE first stages, which often match or exceed the reranked configurations we evaluate. Together, these results show that MoE LLMs can be strong and efficient first-stage retrievers.

</details>


### [5] [Pre-retrieval Query Clustering for Adaptive Top-k Document Retrieval in RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13489)
*Ye Xia,Emre Yamangil,Haixun Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种查询自适应检索深度框架，通过离线估计查询难度与饱和度k*，聚类后为每类查询设定满足高覆盖的top-k，运行时以常数时间选择k，兼顾效果与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统通常使用固定的top-k检索，存在明显缺陷：简单查询过度检索引入噪声与成本，复杂查询检索不足导致召回失败，进而引发错误答案。核心问题是对于一个任意查询，究竟需要检索多少文档才能可靠地作答。

Method: 离线阶段：以默认检索器下的NDCG衡量每个查询的检索难度，并从NDCG-k曲线推导查询特定的“饱和度”点k*；由于在线计算这些信号成本高，故在嵌入空间中对大量查询聚类，用均值加方差规则为每个簇总结出一个面向高覆盖率（约95%）的推荐检索深度。运行时阶段：将输入查询分配到对应簇，并以常数时间选择相应的top-k。该方法属于检索前（pre-retrieval）、以查询为中心的策略，区别于依赖对已检索文档聚类的检索后置信度方法。

Result: 在全流量查询上测试，低复杂度簇上F1提升超过36%，同时token使用量减少14%，且无精度损失；该方法在异构、类案式语料上更鲁棒，可跨法律、医疗、金融、企业搜索等领域应用。

Conclusion: 固定top-k的静态检索策略存在过度检索与检索不足的双重弊端，采用基于查询难度估计与聚类映射的查询自适应检索深度框架，可在常数时间开销下同时提升回答质量并降低token成本，具有良好的通用性与跨域适用性。

Abstract: RAG systems commonly retrieve a fixed number of documents (top-k) to ground generation, but this static approach is brittle: simple queries suffer over-retrieval (adding noise and cost) while complex queries are under-retrieved, causing recall failures that cascade into incorrect answers. Motivated by the question of how many documents must be retrieved to answer an arbitrary query reliably, we propose a practical, general framework for query-adaptive retrieval depth. Offline, we estimate per-query retrieval difficulty by measuring NDCG under the default retriever and deriving a query-specific "saturation" point k* from the NDCG-k curve. Because computing these signals online is expensive, we cluster a large set of queries in embedding space and summarize each cluster with a recommended retrieval depth that targets high coverage (e.g., \textasciitilde{}95\%) using a mean-plus-variance rule. At runtime, the system assigns an incoming query to a cluster and selects the corresponding top-k in constant time. Compared with post-retrieval confidence methods that rely on clustering retrieved documents, our approach is pre-retrieval and query-centric, making it robust in heterogeneous, case-like corpora and applicable across domains such as legal, healthcare, finance, and enterprise search. Finally, this framework has been tested in full-traffic queries that improved $F_1$ by over 36\% while reducing token usage by 14\% on low-complexity clusters without accuracy loss.

</details>


### [6] [Addressing Cross-Stage Decoupling of Semantic and Collaborative Signals in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13678)
*Jiayi Dan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SCRec 提出一个通用框架，通过协同增强分词、语义引导生成与流形对齐三部分实现跨阶段双向信息补充，缓解生成式推荐中语义分词与生成阶段的信息解耦问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐采用两阶段流程：分词阶段以文本语义为主、协同信号与交互相似性融入不足，导致码本分配与下游生成目标错位；生成阶段基于交互数据重新嵌入码序列，又忽视原始语义信息。这种跨阶段信息解耦损害了语义一致性与推荐准确性。

Method: 提出 SCRec 通用框架，包含三个相互关联组件：(i) 协同增强分词，在不引入额外对齐任务的前提下，将文本化协同信号显式注入语义分词；(ii) 语义引导生成，在生成阶段以可学习码嵌入动态校准语义先验；(iii) 流形对齐，调和离散码本索引嵌入空间与稠密连续语义空间之间的几何失配。三者共同对齐语义与协同信号，且额外训练与推理开销极小。

Result: 大量实验验证了所提框架的有效性、鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: SCRec 通过双向信息补充提升跨阶段信息一致性，为生成式推荐中语义与协同信息的融合提供了一种低成本、通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative recommendation reformulates sequential recommendation as autoregressive generation by encoding items into semantic tokens, enabling improved scaling capability and cross-domain generalization. However, existing generative recommender systems typically follow a two-stage pipeline, where item tokenization is largely dominated by textual semantics with limited incorporation of collaborative signals and interaction similarity, leading to code assignments that are misaligned with downstream generation. Conversely, the generation stage tends to overlook the original semantic information, as the code sequences are re-embedded based on interaction data. This cross-stage information decoupling limits semantic coherence and recommendation accuracy.
  To address this issue, we propose SCRec, a general framework that enhances cross-stage coherence through bidirectional information supplementation. Specifically, we introduce (i) collaborative-enhanced tokenization to explicitly inject textualized collaborative signals into semantic tokenization, without introducing additional alignment task, (ii) semantic-guided generation to dynamically recalibrate semantic priors with learnable code embeddings in generation stage, and (iii) manifold alignment to reconcile the geometric mismatch between the embedding space of discrete codebook indices and the dense continuous semantic space. These interrelated components form a general framework that aligns semantic and collaborative signals and enhances cross-stage information coherence, with minimal additional training and inference costs. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness, robustness, and generalizability of our proposed framework.

</details>


### [7] [Odds-Shift Slippage in One-vs-Rest Rankers: Diagnosing and Repairing Reweighting-Induced Top-K Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13810)
*Akifumi Goto*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文将"用尺度正类权重补偿不平衡"的常见做法量化拆解为"理想对数几率平移"与"未实现部分"（odds-shift slippage），在LightGBM与MLP配对实验、MULAN基准上系统测量，并给出按标签做等渗回归的修复方案（oddslip）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: One-vs-rest排序器为极稀疏标签排序时常用 per-label 权重 scale_pos_weight = n_-/n_+ 来抵消类别不平衡。依据 Elkan 恒等式，该权重应等价于把标签 j 的对数几率平移 ln w_j，从而按加权几率排序；但这样排序并非 precision@K 下贝叶斯最优的边际排序，理论上可事后反演平移。然而当权重高达数千时，有限学习器实际产生多少平移、失效后又该如何修复，尚缺乏实测。作者因此提出并测量"权重承诺平移与实际平移之间的差距"，即 odds-shift slippage。

Method: 构造"仅在权重上不同"的LightGBM与MLP配对模型（同数据、同结构、同随机性），比较有/无 per-label 权重（如 scale_pos_weight = n_-/n_+）的排序性能，用 Elkan 恒等式给出的理想平移预测解释损失。对带叶步长上限 c、学习率 η 的booster，推导 T 轮内可实现平移上界 Tηc（nat），并以上限扫描验证。分析无上限时饱和单元会出现取值恰为 1.0 的并列，任何可分映射都无法区分。修复方案为 per-label 等渗回归（isotonic regression）：先用解析反演，观察到其只在平移确实被实现且无饱和时有效，故改用按标签的等渗校准；对无校准正例的标签映射为先验而非直通；并提出交叉验证规则来在正例稀缺时择用校准。

Result: 在 Santander 上，加入权重使 MAP@7 从 0.808 降至 0.117；对boosting配对，理想对数几率平移仅解释该损失的 23%（Instacart 为 32%；同一批行上 MLP 配对达 98%），其余归因于 slippage。叶步长上限扫描证实 Tηc 上界；无上限时饱和单元并列于 1.0。解析反演仅在平移被实现且无饱和时可获益；per-label 等渗回归将 Santander 模型恢复到 0.784（校准器在验证期拟合时为 0.780），前提是无校准正例的标签被映射为先验而非直通。在 11 个公开 MULAN 基准与 5 类学习器上，55 个格中有 8 个加权模型损失超过一半 MAP@K；在 delicious 与 Corel5k 上，同一修复使性能回到未加权水平；per-label 校准在正例稀缺处有害，而交叉验证规则可消除该损害。代码以 oddslip 发布。

Conclusion: per-label 正类权重并不会按 Elkan 恒等式实现预期的对数几率平移，尤其在树模型叶步长受限时会出现严重的 odds-shift slippage，使排序显著退化且部分单元饱和不可分。理想的解析反演仅适用于平移被真实实现的情形；稳健的修复是按标签等渗回归，并须对无正例标签回退到先验、以交叉验证规则规避正例稀缺时的校准反噬。该结论在 MULAN 多基准与多学习器上具有一致性。

Abstract: One-vs-rest rankers that show each user the top-$K$ of many rare labels usually counter imbalance with a per-label positive-class weight, scale_pos_weight $= n_-/n_+$. Elkan's identity says such a weight shifts label $j$'s log-odds by $\ln w_j$, so the model ranks by weighted odds rather than by the marginal that is Bayes-optimal for precision@$K$, and suggests inverting the shift afterwards; what a finite learner does with a weight in the thousands, and which repair then works, has not been measured. We call the gap between the promised and the realized shift odds-shift slippage and measure it on matched pairs of LightGBM and MLP models that differ only in the weights. On Santander the weight takes MAP@7 from 0.808 to 0.117; for the boosted pairs the ideal odds shift accounts for 23% of that loss (32% on Instacart; 98% for an MLP pair on the same rows) and slippage for the rest. We prove that a booster whose leaf steps are capped at $c$ realizes at most $Tηc$ nat of shift in $T$ rounds at rate $η$, which a cap sweep confirms, and show that without a cap saturated cells tie at exactly 1.0, beyond the reach of any separable map. The analytic inversion therefore pays only where the shift was realized and nothing saturated, whereas per-label isotonic regression returns the Santander model to 0.784 (0.780 with the calibrator fitted on the validation period), but only if labels without calibration positives are mapped to their prior rather than passed through. On 11 public MULAN benchmarks and 5 learners the weighted model loses more than half of its MAP@$K$ in 8 of 55 cells, and on delicious and Corel5k the same repair returns it to the unweighted level; per-label calibration hurts where positives are scarce, a harm that a cross-validated rule removes. The recipe is released as oddslip.

</details>


### [8] [P3Rec: Distilling Prior--Posterior Preference Reasoning for LLM-based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13993)
*Jinfei Chen,Weihai Lu,Jiawei Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出P³Rec框架，从用户与物品双视角联合抽取先验与后验偏好推理知识，并逐步内化到行为表征中，再以兴趣熵自适应校准用户表征后进行对比检索优化，实现更完整且高效的推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-as-Enhancer方法将LLM偏好知识蒸馏到轻量推荐器中以避免昂贵的在线推理，但蒸馏知识往往仅来自单一视角：先验偏好刻画用户稳定一致的兴趣，对当前决策指导有限；后验偏好揭示目标相关的细粒度兴趣，却可能过度依赖目标线索。二者各有局限，需要互补融合。

Method: P³Rec首先从用户侧推导目标无关的先验偏好与目标条件的后验偏好，同时从物品语义与前驱交互中提取以物品为中心的偏好表征；随后通过先验偏好吸收与后验引导的偏好蒸馏，将先验与后验知识逐步内化到行为表征中；由于综合偏好表征未必总能提供同等明确的检索方向，进一步用兴趣熵刻画历史兴趣分散度，在对比检索优化前对用户表征进行自适应校准。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上的大量实验验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: P³Rec通过联合建模并内化互补的先验与后验偏好知识，结合兴趣熵驱动的表征校准与对比检索优化，在保持推荐效率的同时实现了更完整的偏好推理。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong semantic understanding and preference reasoning capabilities, offering new opportunities for user modeling in recommender systems. Existing LLM-as-Enhancer methods typically distill LLM-derived preference knowledge into lightweight recommenders to avoid costly online LLM inference. However, they often construct distillation knowledge from only one perspective. Prior preference captures users' stable and consistent interests but provides limited guidance for the current decision, whereas posterior preference reveals target-relevant fine-grained interests but may rely excessively on target clues. To address these limitations, we propose P$^3$Rec, a framework that jointly extracts and internalizes complementary prior and posterior preference reasoning knowledge. Specifically, P$^3$Rec first derives target-agnostic prior preferences and target-conditioned posterior preferences from the user side, while further extracting item-centric preference representations from item semantics and predecessor interactions. It then progressively internalizes prior and posterior knowledge into behavioral representations through prior preference absorption and posterior-guided preference distillation. Since the resulting comprehensive preference representation may not always provide an equally decisive retrieval direction, P$^3$Rec further characterizes historical interest dispersion with interest entropy and adaptively calibrates the user representation before contrastive retrieval optimization. In this way, P$^3$Rec achieves more complete preference reasoning while preserving efficient recommendation. Extensive experiments on multiple public datasets demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [9] [TF-IDF and BM25 Are Exact KL Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14016)
*Ivan Silajev*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文为 TF-IDF 与 BM25 提供了统一的概率推导，证明二者均可精确解释为两个概率模型之间的 KL 散度，从而赋予其统计方法意义上的理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TF-IDF 与 BM25 是查询-文档相关性评分中最广泛使用的方法，但两者都缺乏标准的概率推导，无法在统一框架内被论证为统计方法。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 将 TF-IDF 与 BM25 的评分分别刻画为两个概率模型之间的 Kullback-Leibler 散度。重点处理实践中采用的带 "+1" 修正的 IDF 项的 BM25 变体，并同时讨论不带该修正的原始 BM25 形式。

Result: 两种评分方法均获得精确的 KL 散度解释，即其得分可等价表示为相应概率模型间的 KL 散度，从而建立起共同的理论基础。

Conclusion: 该框架为 TF-IDF 与 BM25 提供了统一的理论依据，明确了它们所度量的内容，并使二者能够从理论层面而非仅实验层面与其他信息检索方法进行比较。

Abstract: TF-IDF and BM25 are two of the most widely used methods for scoring query-document relevance, yet neither has a standard probabilistic derivation that justifies it as a statistical method within a unified framework. We address this gap by showing that both scoring methods admit an exact interpretation as Kullback-Leibler divergences between two probability models. We treat the BM25 variant that includes the plus 1 correction in the IDF term, which is the one used in practice, and also discuss the original BM25 formulation without that correction. The resulting framework provides a common theoretical basis for TF-IDF and BM25, clarifies what they measure, and allows them to be compared theoretically with other information retrieval methods rather than only experimentally.

</details>


### [10] [VARG: Value-Aware and Ranking-Aligned Generative Retrieval for Dynamic E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14493)
*Xiaopeng Chu,Jianbo Zhu,Mingmin Jin,Jing Wang,Xing Fang,Wenyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出VARG——面向天猫App搜索的生成式检索系统，将生成的候选商品直接输入现有精排模型，通过RQ-VAE语义前缀、三阶段SFT与Prefix-GRPO等手段融合相关性、个性化与商业价值，离线验证标识稳定性与召回质量提升，14天20%流量A/B实验中GMV提升1.45%、人均IPV提升0.22%、PCTR提升0.31%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商搜索中召回与粗排需在精排前同时兼顾相关性、个性化与商业价值，传统候选生成难以直接对接精排目标，因此需要一种能直接向精排输出可用候选项、并内嵌商业价值先验的生成式检索方案。

Method: 1）VARG-ID用RQ-VAE构建语义前缀，通过双向query-item对比学习增强相关性，并将前缀与按价值排序的第三个token结合，形成细粒度商品地址与商业价值先验；2）三阶段监督微调依次学习item-to-identifier映射、query语义检索、个性化检索；3）个性化训练结合价值感知与层级对齐监督，扩展用户上下文，并用局部序监督（LO-SFT）学习第三token编码的簇内排序；4）Prefix-GRPO将输出合法性、用户行为、精排优势与搜索相关性构成的门控奖励，与前缀感知token加权结合，对齐候选生成与商业价值及排序目标；5）每日协同的商品与模型更新在保留已有商品地址的同时纳入新品与行为反馈。

Result: 千万级商品离线实验验证了标识稳定性，并显示SFT策略与Prefix-GRPO在检索质量和头部价值召回上相对各自基线均有提升；14天覆盖20%搜索流量的在线A/B实验中，VARG将生成候选直接送入精排，GMV提升1.45%、人均IPV提升0.22%、PCTR提升0.31%；在线导购类query评测中，VARG以更小的候选配额保持有竞争力的相关性。

Conclusion: VARG表明生成式检索可在电商搜索中直接对接精排，通过语义前缀、价值先验、分阶段监督与强化学习对齐，在不牺牲相关性的前提下提升商业价值指标，并通过可协调的每日更新维持标识稳定性和新品覆盖。

Abstract: Integrating recall and pre-ranking in e-commerce search requires candidate generation to account for relevance, personalization, and business value before final ranking. To this end, we present VARG, a generative retrieval system for Tmall App search that directly admits generated item candidates to the existing final ranker. VARG-ID constructs semantic prefixes using RQ-VAE, enhances search relevance through bidirectional query-item contrastive learning, and combines these prefixes with a value-ordered third token to provide fine-grained item addresses and a business-value prior. Three-stage supervised fine-tuning progressively learns item-to-identifier mappings, query-semantic retrieval, and personalized retrieval. Personalized model training combines value-aware and hierarchy-aligned supervision with expanded user context, and uses local ordinal supervision (LO-SFT) to learn the local within-cluster ordering encoded by the third token. Prefix-GRPO combines gated rewards based on output legality, user behavior, ranker advantage, and search relevance with prefix-aware token weighting to align candidate generation with business value and ranking objectives. Coordinated daily product and model updates preserve existing item addresses while incorporating new products and behavioral feedback. Offline experiments on tens of millions of products validate identifier stability and demonstrate gains in retrieval quality and head-level value recall from SFT strategies and Prefix-GRPO over their respective baselines. In a 14-day online A/B test covering 20% of search traffic, VARG directly admits generated candidates to the final ranker and improves GMV by 1.45%, per-user IPV by 0.22%, and PCTR by 0.31%. Online shopping-guide query evaluations further show that VARG maintains competitive relevance with a smaller candidate quota.

</details>


### [11] [The Wisdom of the Loudest: A Large-Scale Audit of Generative Search on Reddit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14575)
*Agam Goyal,Wang Claire,Eshwar Chandrasekharan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文审计了Reddit Answers（社区型生成式搜索）在检索与综合过程中的声音选择偏差：用20个求助/支持类社区的1万条查询重复三次生成3万条回答（覆盖1468万条评论），发现差异主要源于检索，回答常跨社区拼接证据，并偏好已高可见的顶层评论；正式、指导性语言更易被呈现，而经验性声音更易在筛选中流失并在综合阶段进一步削弱，第一人称单数语言显著下降。作者据此主张此类系统应同时面向来源可溯、多元性与可读性进行设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线社区的价值不仅在于答案，更在于其经验与视角的多样性；生成式搜索正日益成为访问这些社区话语的中介，但哪些社区声音能在检索与综合中被保留尚不清楚，因此需要审计其选择机制是否中立。

Method: 对Reddit Answers进行审计：选取20个建议与支持类社区的10,000条查询，每条重复三次，得到30,000条回答，覆盖14.68M条评论；并分析跨运行差异来源、跨社区证据组合情况、评论层级偏好以及语言特征（正式/指导性 vs 经验性、第一人称单数）在检索与综合阶段的变化。

Result: 不同运行之间的差异主要由检索驱动；回答经常跨社区组合证据；选择强烈偏向已可见的顶层评论；正式与指导性语言更可能被呈现；经验性声音在筛选中存活率更低，并在综合阶段被进一步削弱，第一人称单数语言显著减少。

Conclusion: 以社区为基础的生成式搜索并非中立的摘要过程；其设计不应只追求相关性与流畅度，还应考虑来源可溯（provenance）、多元性（plurality）与可读/可解释性（legibility）。

Abstract: Online communities are valued not only for answers, but for the diversity of experiences and perspectives they contain. Generative search increasingly mediates access to this discourse, yet little is known about which community voices survive retrieval and synthesis. We audit Reddit Answers using 10,000 queries from 20 advice- and support-seeking communities, repeated three times to produce 30,000 answers over 14.68M comments. We find that differences across runs are driven primarily by retrieval, answers routinely combine evidence across communities, and selection strongly favors already-visible, top-level comments. Formal and directive language is more likely to be surfaced, while experiential voice is less likely to survive selection and is further weakened during synthesis, with first-person singular language declining sharply. These findings show that community-grounded generative search is not neutral summarization, and should be designed not only for relevance and fluency, but also for provenance, plurality, and legibility.

</details>


### [12] [Beyond Benchmark Scores: How Synthetic and Authentic Query Distributions Diverge in RAG Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14579)
*Filip J. Kucia,Barbara M. Gawlik*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文指出仅用合成问题集评测RAG系统会高估其部署效果，并以高校教师信息系统为例，对比1851条合成问题与322条真实用户问题，发现两者在查询长度与来源覆盖上差异显著，导致合成基准上的最优配置在真实查询上性能大幅下降，且优化合成查询会选出高延迟的混合检索器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统通常使用从目标语料生成的合成问题集进行评测，但这类合成基准在分布偏移下可能误导结论、夸大部署成熟度。作者希望揭示合成查询与真实用户查询之间的系统性差距，说明单一依赖合成基准的风险。

Method: 在一个高校教师信息系统上开展对比研究：使用Gemini Notebook生成1,851条合成问题，并通过学生问卷收集322条真实查询；对比两类查询的词汇长度、来源覆盖等分布特征，并比较不同检索配置（含稀疏检索与混合检索）在两类查询上的性能与延迟表现。

Result: 合成与真实查询集差异显著：真实查询平均6.8词，合成查询平均15.7词；真实查询仅来自53个唯一来源，合成查询覆盖165个。合成基准上表现优异的配置在真实查询上出现明显性能下降；在合成查询上优化会选中更高延迟的混合检索器，其中稀疏检索组件对长合成问题有效但对短真实查询无益，延迟最高达最快配置的8倍。

Conclusion: 应将合成查询集与真实查询集视为查询质量谱系的两个互补极端：合成数据用于验证理想条件下系统的最大检索能力，真实查询用于检验系统对用户不精确、欠规范输入的鲁棒性。评测应同时纳入二者，以避免误导性结论。

Abstract: RAG systems are routinely evaluated using synthetic question sets generated from the target document corpus. While this practice provides a useful check on overall retrieval capability, relying exclusively on synthetic benchmarks can mislead under distribution shift and overstate deployment readiness. Synthetic generation spreads questions evenly across the corpus, formulating long, detailed queries; real users put most of their traffic on a few administrative and procedural topics in short queries, while also asking about matters the generator never covers at all. We demonstrate this gap on a university faculty information system, comparing 1,851 synthetic questions generated via Gemini Notebook against 322 authentic queries collected via a student survey. The synthetic and authentic query sets differ significantly: authentic queries average 6.8 words versus 15.7 for the synthetic ones, and draw from only 53 unique sources compared to 165. Consequently, configurations that appear highly effective on synthetic benchmarks experience a substantial performance drop on authentic queries. Importantly, optimizing on synthetic queries selected a higher-latency hybrid retriever. In our setting the sparse retrieval component benefited long synthetic questions but not short authentic ones, costing up to $8\times$ the latency of the fastest configuration we tested. We propose treating synthetic and authentic query sets as complementary extremes of the query-quality spectrum: synthetic data verifies maximum retrieval capacity under idealized conditions, while authentic queries test system robustness to the imprecise, underspecified inputs of real users.

</details>


### [13] [EviQE: Evidence Selection for LLM-Based Query Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14875)
*Hai Son Le,Amin Bigdeli,Shirin Seyedsalehi,Morteza Zihayat,Ebrahim Bagheri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 EviQE，将证据选择与查询扩展生成分离：聚合多重重写器检索到的文档，筛选紧凑证据集，再进行一次有依据的扩展，在 TREC DL 与 BEIR 上取得稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大模型的查询扩展研究多关注"如何生成扩展"，而忽视"模型应阅读哪些文档"。直接扩展、冷启动扩展与单源种子扩展的证据质量受限，且多轮检索-生成迭代的收益不明确。

Method: EviQE 把多个重写器视为互补的检索视角，先合并它们检索到的池化候选文档，再通过相关性筛选选出紧凑证据集，最后用该证据集执行一次有依据的查询扩展（grounded expansion）。文中对比了 LLM-Score 等基于相关性的证据选择策略。

Result: 在 3 个 TREC DL 与 5 个 BEIR 基准上，不同重写器常检索到互不相同的相关文档，池化候选的相关文档覆盖率高于任何单一来源；基于相关性选择证据（LLM-Score）稳定优于直接重写、冷启动扩展和单源种子扩展；在选定强条件证据后，增加检索-生成轮次收益甚微，甚至降低效果。

Conclusion: 证据选择是 LLM 查询扩展的关键环节：将证据选择与生成解耦、并利用多重写器的互补性，比单纯增加生成轮次更有效；未来工作应聚焦证据质量控制而非迭代次数。

Abstract: LLM-based query expansion increasingly conditions reformulation on documents retrieved from the target corpus, yet most work focuses on how to generate expansions rather than which documents the model should read. We propose EviQE, which aggregates documents retrieved by multiple reformulators, selects a compact evidence set, and uses it for one grounded expansion step. This separates evidence selection from generation and treats reformulators as complementary retrieval perspectives. Across three TREC DL and five BEIR benchmarks, reformulators frequently retrieve distinct relevant documents, so pooled candidates provide higher relevant-document coverage than any individual source. The strongest gains come from relevance-based evidence selection: LLM-Score consistently outperforms direct reformulation, cold-start expansion, and single-source seeded expansion. Additional retrieval-generation rounds provide little benefit once strong conditioning evidence has been selected and can reduce effectiveness.

</details>


### [14] [Route Me If You Can: A Benchmark for Query Reformulation Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14885)
*Hai Son Le,Negar Arabzadeh,Amin Bigdeli,Radin Hamidi Rad,Sajad Ebrahimi,Charles L. A. Clarke,Ebrahim Bagheri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 QueryRoute 基准，通过冻结查询变体、多检索器排序列表、检索分数与逐查询 oracle 标签，将"选择哪个重写查询"形式化为可复现的推理时决策问题，覆盖 3,757 查询、11 个候选系统、5 个重写器骨干、3 个检索器，共 619,905 条检索结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一重写策略在不同查询、领域、检索器与模型骨干下均非最优，因而存在推理时决策问题：给定原始查询与候选重写池，应向检索器发出哪一个。既有研究因重写器池、检索器、相关性信号、训练标签与评测指标各异而难以比较。

Method: 构建 QueryRoute 基准，冻结研究该决策所需的高成本中间产物：原始查询、生成变体、多检索器下的排序列表、检索分数与逐查询 oracle 标签；并基准测试监督分类、路由（routing）、查询性能预测（QPP）与 LLM-as-judge 等选择器。

Result: 实验显示相对固定重写器存在显著的 oracle 上界空间，但现有选择器仅能恢复其中一部分；选择器排名随检索器变化，且相近的平均有效性可能掩盖不同的逐查询行为。

Conclusion: 发布的产物与评测框架使后续选择器可在无需重新生成变体、重跑检索或重建评判流程的前提下进行对比。

Abstract: LLM-based query reformulation can improve retrieval, but no single reformulation strategy is consistently optimal across queries, domains, retrievers, or model backbones. This creates an inference-time decision problem: ``Given an original query and a pool of candidate reformulations, which one should be issued to the retriever?''. Existing studies are hard to compare because they use different reformulator pools, retrievers, relevance signals, training labels, and evaluation metrics. We introduce QueryRoute, a benchmark that freezes the expensive artifacts needed to study this decision reproducibly: original queries, generated variants, ranked lists under multiple retrievers, retrieval scores, and per-query oracle labels. The benchmark contains 3,757 queries, 11 candidate systems, five reformulator backbones, and three retrievers across TREC DL, BEIR, and BRIGHT, yielding 619,905 retrieval outcomes. We benchmark supervised classification, routing, QPP, and LLM-as-judge selectors. Results show substantial oracle headroom over fixed reformulators, but current selectors recover only part of it; selector rankings change across retrievers, and similar mean effectiveness can hide different query-level behavior. The released artifacts and evaluation harness allow future selectors to be compared without regenerating variants, rerunning retrieval, or rebuilding judge pipelines. Code and data are available at https://github.com/haisonle001/QueryRoute

</details>


### [15] [LazFormer: Scaling Transformers for Industrial Recommendation via Transferable Generative Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14978)
*Xiaodong Li,Alin Fan,Mingyang Li,Yan Xiao,Shichao Nie,Junfeng Zhang,Shaochuan Lin,Zhanming Ou,Tao Luo,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出LazFormer，一种通过可迁移生成式预训练实现工业推荐扩展的Transformer框架，解决稠密参数负迁移与稀疏参数过拟合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐中Transformer通常从零训练单一排序模型以同时优化稀疏和稠密参数，导致算力消耗大、收敛慢。预训练模型虽能提供良好初始化，但存在两个局限：(1) 预训练与排序的输入特征不一致，直接迁移稠密参数会造成负迁移；(2) 排序阶段多轮训练易使稀疏参数过拟合，而冻结稀疏参数又限制其对排序目标的适应性。

Method: 1) 生成式预训练模块：自回归生成序列特征，为后续排序提供稀疏与稠密参数的有利初始化；2) 可迁移残差适配器：以残差方式向排序注入排序专属特征，缓解稠密参数负迁移；3) 请求感知排序模块：集成长序列压缩、混合稀疏注意力和请求感知范式，高效建模用户长序列；4) 非对称多轮训练策略：跨轮重置稀疏参数并持续累积稠密参数，缓解稀疏参数过拟合。

Result: 摘要未给出具体量化结果，仅声明所提方法在应对稠密参数负迁移、稀疏参数过拟合及长序列建模效率等方面具备有效性。

Conclusion: LazFormer通过可迁移生成式预训练与残差适配、请求感知排序及非对称多轮训练策略，为工业推荐Transformer的可扩展性提供了一套兼顾初始化质量、迁移性与训练稳定性的解决方案。

Abstract: Transformers have shown promising performance in LLMs due to their outstanding scalability, several studies have investigated the scalability of Transformers for industrial recommendation. They typically rely on a single ranking model to optimize both sparse and dense parameters from scratch, resulting in substantial computational resource consumption and slow convergence. Fortunately, the pre-training models offer an effective solution to the above issues by providing favorable initialization of both sparse and dense parameters for the subsequent ranking. However, they still face two major limitations: (1) Since the input features used in pre-training and ranking are usually inconsistent, directly transferring dense parameters from pre-training to ranking may lead to negative transfer. (2) Multi-epoch training during the ranking process may result in the overfitting of sparse parameters, while freezing the sparse parameters limits their adaptability to the ranking objectives. To this end, we propose a Scaling Transformer for Industrial Recommendation via Transferable Generative Pre-training, termed LazFormer. Specifically, we first present a generative pre-training module to autoregressively generate sequential features, providing favorable initialization of both sparse and dense parameters for the subsequent ranking. To solve the negative transfer of dense parameters, we propose a transferable residual adapter that injects additional ranking-specific features into ranking in a residual manner. Moreover, a request-aware ranking module integrates long-sequence compression, hybrid sparse attention, and a request-aware paradigm to efficiently model users' long sequences. Besides, we further propose an asymmetric multi-epoch training strategy that resets sparse parameters while continuously accumulating dense parameters across epochs, alleviating the overfitting of sparse parameters.

</details>


### [16] [Generate to Explore, Select to Exploit: Aligning LLM-based Headline Generation with Personalized Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15094)
*Yi Chen,Rufeng Cheng,Qiang Xie,Tao Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出GESE框架，将推荐标题个性化拆分为"生成式探索+选择性利用"，用LLM生成候选题集再由实时选择器择优，在亿级DAU平台上实现CTR +2.57%、停留时长 +0.87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐流中静态标题难以满足用户群体的多元多模态兴趣，尤其压制长尾受众需求；已有LLM用于内容理解或排序，但直接优化其输出单一最优标题易导致模式坍塌，收敛到满足平均口味的通用模式，遗漏特定潜在意图。

Method: GESE运行于系统展示层，将个性化解耦为生成式探索与选择性利用两个阶段。生成侧把LLM视为概率探索器，采用组序列策略优化（GSPO）配合分层奖励机制，生成最大化潜在用户兴趣语义覆盖度的候选集；选择侧以轻量、实时、反馈感知的选择器作为利用者，基于即时上下文信号从候选池中确定最优实现。

Result: 在日活超1亿的商业平台上大规模部署，显著优于现有最优基线，CTR提升2.57%，停留时长提升0.87%。

Conclusion: 将多样性导向的生成与精确性导向的选择解耦，为生成式AI对齐动态用户效用提供了稳健范式。

Abstract: In industrial recommendation feeds, presenting a static headline for an item often fails to satisfy the diverse, multimodal interests of the user population, particularly suppressing the needs of long-tail audiences. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have been integrated into recommendation for content understanding or ranking, directly optimizing them to output a single best headline typically leads to mode collapse---converging to generic patterns that satisfy average tastes but miss specific latent intents. To bridge this gap, we introduce GESE (Generate to Explore, Select to Exploit), a framework operating at the system's presentation layer that decouples personalization into generative exploration and selective exploitation. First, we treat the LLM as a probabilistic explorer, utilizing Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO) with a hierarchical reward mechanism to generate a candidate set that maximizes the semantic coverage of potential user interests. Subsequently, a lightweight, real-time feedback-aware selector acts as the exploiter, identifying the optimal realization from the candidate pool based on instant contextual signals. Extensive deployment on a commercial platform with over 100 million daily active users demonstrates that GESE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a 2.57% lift in CTR and 0.87% in dwell time. These results validate that decoupling diversity-oriented generation from precision-oriented selection offers a robust blueprint for aligning generative AI with dynamic user utility.

</details>


### [17] [Top-K Is Not a Budget for Hybrid Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15143)
*Chunran Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DiBud将访问预算作为输入，在线增量化认证并返回RRF排名的精确前缀，在预算约束下提升认证输出量并降低访问成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合检索通常融合稠密与稀疏检索器的Top-L结果，但固定截断深度L难以适应变化的查询与语料；精确融合虽不依赖固定深度，但完成指定Top-K仍需可变且可能高昂的访问成本。

Method: 提出DiBud：直接以访问预算为输入，对完整列表上的RRF排名进行增量式认证，返回精确前缀；采用选择性访问（selective access）以在预算内增加已认证输出，并引入预算化停止（budgeted stopping）以限制每次请求的访问次数。

Result: 在五个查询集上观察到完成精确Top-20存在长尾访问成本；预算为2048次访问时，DiBud相比均衡访问使前100位内的平均认证输出提升7.86%。经预算校准以保留95%质量后，留出查询在平均nDCG@20上保留95.05%–97.68%，同时访问次数比完成精确Top-20减少65.92%–99.53%。

Conclusion: DiBud以访问预算为直接控制变量，在有限访问下实现RRF精确前缀的高效认证，兼顾输出覆盖率与检索质量，显著优于固定深度融合与均衡访问策略。

Abstract: Modern hybrid retrieval for RAG typically fuses the Top-$L$ results from dense and sparse retrievers, but a fixed truncation depth may not transfer across changing queries and corpora. Exact fusion removes the dependence on a fixed depth, yet completing a specified Top-$K$ still incurs variable access costs. We present DiBud, which takes an access budget directly as input and incrementally certifies and returns an exact prefix of the RRF ranking over the full lists. Selective access increases certified output within the budget, while budgeted stopping bounds accesses per request. Experiments on five query sets reveal long-tailed costs for completing exact Top-20. At a budget of 2048 accesses, DiBud increases mean certified output within the first 100 positions by 7.86% over balanced access. After budget calibration for 95% quality retention, held-out queries retain 95.05%--97.68% of mean nDCG@20 while using 65.92%--99.53% fewer accesses than completing exact Top-20.

</details>


### [18] [ProLiVis 2.0: Literature-Centric Visualization of Protein--Protein Interaction Networks, with a Citation-Trust Model for Interaction Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15236)
*Melih Sözdinler,Yalçın Doksanbir,Gökhan Akpınar,Ege Aktan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ProLiVis 2.0 是一个基于文献的蛋白质互作可视化系统重写版，提出引文信任模型、确定性 O(n log n) 中心布局与纯浏览器端实现，使互作证据的可信度可见；在 SARS-CoV-2 子集上显示 70% 的互作仅来自单一文献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质互作数据库（如 BioGRID）只记录证据而不加权，一次高通量筛选与多实验室反复验证的互作以同等权重呈现；基于此类数据库的工具将所有报告互作一律绘制为边，无法表达可信程度。

Method: ProLiVis 2.0 是对 arXiv:2111.12794 文献中心可视化系统的重写：（1）引文信任模型，基于七项指标打分，其中通过按共同机构隶属对支撑文献聚类，得到独立实验室数量项，以区分多次验证与同一团队重复发表；（2）中心布局的确定性重构，闭式解且复杂度 O(n log n)，替代原有力导向布局，使已发表图像可由会话清单复现；（3）完全运行于网页浏览器、内置分析数据库的实现，无需安装且不上传数据。

Result: 在限定于 SARS-CoV-2 的 BioGRID 5.0.260 版本上，34,540 条报告互作中有 24,344 条（70%）仅依赖单一文献；将信任阈值提高至 0.2 后仍剩 11,320 条。这表明经人工审编的互作网络大部分未被重复验证，而现有数据库视图无从呈现这一事实。

Conclusion: 该工作通过可信度评分与可复现的确定性布局，将互作证据的权重差异显式可视化，揭示主流互作数据库中普遍存在的不可重复性问题，并提供免安装、隐私友好的浏览器端分析工具。

Abstract: Protein-protein interaction databases record evidence without weighing it. In BioGRID, an interaction asserted once by a single high-throughput screen and one confirmed by twenty laboratories across a dozen assays are the same kind of row in the same file. Tools built on such databases inherit that flattening: they draw every reported interaction as an edge, and the resulting picture states that two proteins interact without stating how much anyone should believe it.
  We present ProLiVis 2.0, a rewrite of the literature-centric visualization system of arXiv:2111.12794. It contributes three things. First, a citation-trust model that scores each interaction from seven terms, including a term for the number of independent laboratories behind the supporting publications, obtained by clustering those publications over shared institutional affiliations; a plain count of publications cannot distinguish five confirmations from one group publishing five times. Second, a deterministic reformulation of the center layout, closed-form and $O(n \log n)$, which replaces the force-directed placement of the original and makes published figures regenerable from a session manifest. Third, an implementation that runs entirely in a web browser, with an embedded analytical database, requiring no installation and uploading no data.
  On BioGRID release 5.0.260 restricted to SARS-CoV-2, 24,344 of 34,540 reported interactions (70%) rest on a single publication, and raising the trust threshold to 0.2 leaves 11,320 of them. That the large majority of a curated interaction network is unreplicated is a fact no existing view of the database makes visible.

</details>


### [19] [Clean Scores, Buried Evidence, and Confident Wrong: A Receipt-Based Audit of Frontier Agentic QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15319)
*Luis M. Sánchez*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 前沿模型在浅层文档/图表阅读任务上表现良好；在受控数据室审计中，将证据转入"埋藏"条件后，准确率下降、被迫声明增多、工具调用与单位正确答案成本上升，且置信度与基准校准无法充分反映错误答案。作者主张智能体评估应转向声明级溯源、条件感知评分与人工对抗验证的"审计纪律"，而非榜单排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅层阅读基准给出高分，容易高估模型在高风险场景中的可靠性。作者关注证据被"埋藏"（buried）时的表现退化，以及这种退化在责任由人承担的场景（金融尽职调查、国防幕僚工作）中的后果，因为模型并不承担后果，签署者才承担。

Method: 采用受控数据室审计（controlled data-room audit）设计：对比浅层条件与"埋藏证据"条件下的模型表现，记录准确率、被迫声明（forced declarations）、工具调用次数与单位正确答案成本；同时检查置信度与基准校准对错误答案的捕捉能力，并复盘一起已记录的生产事故。

Result: 证据被埋藏后，准确率下降、被迫声明增加、工具调用增多、单位正确答案成本上升；置信度与基准校准不能完全识别错误答案；生产事故显示模型可将准确数字表与虚构的结构性论断混在一起。

Conclusion: 智能体评估需具备三项要素：声明级回执（statement-level provenance，而非答案级分数）、条件感知评分、人工对抗验证——应作为一种审计纪律而非排行榜。举证责任应从模型转移到证据链。

Abstract: Frontier models score well on shallow document/chart reading tasks. In a controlled data-room audit, moving evidence into buried conditions reduced accuracy, increased forced declarations, increased tool calls, and increased cost per correct answer. Confidence and benchmark calibration did not fully capture wrong answers; a documented production incident shows fabricated structural claims can be mixed with accurate numeric tables. Agentic evaluations need claim-level receipts (statement-level provenance, not answer-level scores), condition-aware scoring, and human-adversarial verification - an auditing discipline, not a leaderboard. The setting we measure is financial due diligence; the setting we are building toward next is defense staff work, where the same buried-evidence shape appears. In both, the model is not a party to the consequences; the person who signs is. In plain terms: in the documented cases we examine, agents can pair accurate numbers with confident fabricated explanations, and the burden of proof must therefore move from the model to the evidence trail.

</details>


### [20] [Benchmarking Embedding Models for ESG Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15434)
*Motaz Saad,Veronica Cretì,Ivan Gentile,Kianna Kazemi,Antonella Longo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文构建 ESG 领域嵌入模型基准数据集，评测 14 个开源与闭源模型在检索与 RAG 任务上的表现，发现 Qwen3 系列整体最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ESG 数据支撑企业问责、可持续报告与金融决策，嵌入模型是将非结构化 ESG 文本转为数值表示的关键手段，但其在 ESG 特定任务中的有效性缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 构建面向 ESG 领域的基准数据集，选取 14 个开源与闭源嵌入模型，在检索与检索增强生成（RAG）任务上进行对比评测。

Result: 不同模型表现存在差异，Qwen3 系列模型取得最高整体性能。

Conclusion: 该研究为 ESG RAG 任务中嵌入模型的选型提供了实践性参考。

Abstract: The use of Environmental, Social, and Governance (ESG) data is fundamental for modern corporate accountability, sustainability reporting, and financial decision-making. Embedding models have emerged as a powerful approach for transforming unstructured ESG text into numerical representations suitable for downstream natural language processing (NLP) tasks. However, their effectiveness in these ESG-specific tasks has not been systematically studied. In this paper, we construct a benchmark dataset specifically tailored to the ESG domain. We benchmark fourteen models, both open-source and closed-source embedding models, comparing their performance with respect to retrieval, and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). The results demonstrate performance variations across different models, with Qwen3-based models achieving the highest overall performance. This study provides practical insights into which models are better suited for ESG RAG tasks.

</details>


### [21] [Beyond Retrieval: Scaffolding Children's Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15568)
*Diletta Micol Tobia,Hrishita Chakrabarti,Maria Soledad Pera,Monica Landoni*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 将儿童搜索重新定位为"脚手架式学习体验"，而非单纯的检索任务，主张让儿童成为搜索过程的主动参与者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线信息获取系统主要面向主流人群（如成人）设计，儿童对其运作机制理解有限，导致搜索行为缺乏结构且效率低下，进而可能抑制其好奇心与批判性搜索技能的发展。

Method: 基于儿童信息检索（Child-oriented IR）与人机交互（HCI）两个领域的既有文献，进行理论定位与概念框架构建，把儿童视为搜索过程的主动参与者，并以"脚手架式学习"为框架重新刻画儿童的搜索过程。

Result: 未提供具体实验或实证结果；本段为研究定位与框架陈述。

Conclusion: 儿童搜索研究应超越"简化版成人检索"的思路，转向支持学习与技能培养的脚手架式交互设计。

Abstract: Children increasingly turn to online information access systems that are primarily designed for the mainstream population, e.g., adults, but possess a limited understanding of how these systems work, contributing to their unstructured and ineffective search practices. This lack of knowledge can hinder their curiosity and the development of critical search skills. Grounded on the existing literature of both child-oriented Information Retrieval and Human Computer Interaction, our work positions children as active participants in the search process, framing it as a scaffolded learning experience rather than a simple retrieval task.

</details>


### [22] [The Magnitude Mirage: Rethinking Confidence for Reasoning-Intensive Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15578)
*Jamie Holdcroft,Abdelrahman Abdallah,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文揭示RAG检索拒答中基于相似度分数阈值的"量级幻象"问题，并提出六种零成本查询性能预测(QPP)指标进行替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多生产级RAG系统通过阈值化原始相似度分数来实现检索拒答，隐式地将分数绝对值视为置信信号。但当查询需要超出语义匹配的推理时，这一做法会系统性退化：神经检索器常给语义相关但违反约束的文档赋予高分，导致基于量级的阈值在逻辑与时间推理任务上的拒答性能接近随机。

Method: 在11种检索架构、28个数据集上验证问题；针对六种零成本QPP指标开展大规模实证研究，覆盖三个认知层级：语义匹配(BEIR)、逻辑推理(BRIGHT)、时间推理(TEMPO)；对比分数分布信号与分数量级信号的效果差异。

Result: 核心发现是放弃分数量级、转向分数分布信号带来的提升，超过不同分布类替代方案之间差异的5-10倍。具体而言，Score Gap (s1−sk) 与 Score Magnitude and Variance 的实用变体(LSMV)在量级置信几乎无判别力的场景下，将拒答AUROC最多提升0.16。

Conclusion: 基于分布信号的拒答方法无需额外推理、重训练或延迟，是部署中RAG系统替代量级阈值化的实用零成本方案。

Abstract: Many production RAG systems implement retrieval abstention by thresholding raw similarity scores, implicitly treating score magnitude as a confidence signal. We demonstrate that this practice degrades systematically as queries require reasoning beyond semantic matching. Across 11 retrieval architectures and 28 datasets, neural retrievers consistently assign high similarity scores to semantically related but constraint-violating documents, causing magnitude-based thresholds to collapse toward near-random abstention performance on logical and temporal reasoning tasks---a failure we term the Magnitude Mirage. To address this without computationally expensive alternatives, we conduct a large-scale empirical study of six zero-cost Query Performance Prediction (QPP) metrics across three cognitive tiers: semantic matching (BEIR), logical reasoning (BRIGHT), and temporal reasoning (TEMPO). Our central finding is that the key improvement comes from abandoning magnitude in favor of score-distribution signals: the gain from this shift exceeds the differences among distributional alternatives by a factor of 5-10$\times$. In particular, Score Gap ($s_1 - s_k$) and a practical adaptation of Score Magnitude and Variance (LSMV) improve abstention AUROC by up to 0.16 in settings where magnitude-based confidence provides little discriminative power. These methods require no additional inference, retraining, or latency, making them a practical zero-cost replacement for magnitude thresholding in deployed RAG systems.

</details>


### [23] [Self-Evolving Memory for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15598)
*Xinyu Lin,Zhuosong Jiang,Zixiao Suo,Siqin Wang,Hanqing Zeng,Hanchao Yu,Yinglong Xia,Jiang Zhang,Aashu Singh,Fei Liu,Wenjie Wang,Fuli Feng,Yang Song,Qifan Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出自演化记忆范式LION，通过稀疏键值记忆层缓解生成式推荐中的"演化冲突"，在持续演化场景下优于直接参数更新策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐需具备自演化能力以适应用户偏好随时间变化。现有持续训练与蒸馏式适配直接更新共享模型参数，导致不同用户的异质偏好偏移在完全共享的自回归参数空间中被优化，主导行为模式逐渐主导模型演化，而欠代表模式被忽视，形成"演化冲突"。

Method: 提出自演化记忆范式及其三条原则：隔离记忆、强化演化、可扩展应用。据此设计LION框架，核心为稀疏键值（Key-Value）记忆层：稀疏记忆激活隔离不同行为模式的演化，并设计整合损失（consolidation loss）在持续适配中强化对欠代表偏好动态的学习。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上，按周期评估、用户/物品分组评估及演化收敛性分析等多种持续演化设置下，LION均表现出有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将演化能力从共享参数转移到稀疏记忆层并配合整合损失，可在异质行为模式下实现有效且可扩展的自演化生成式推荐，为缓解演化冲突提供了简单有效的方案。

Abstract: Generative recommendation has emerged as a promising end-to-end paradigm for personalized recommendation. However, user preferences continuously evolve over time, making self-evolving an essential capability for generative recommender systems. Existing evolving strategies, such as continual retraining and distillation-based adaptation, directly update the shared model parameters using streaming interactions. Nevertheless, we find that directly applying such strategies to generative recommendation introduces a critical issue, termed evolution conflict. Specifically, heterogeneous preference shifts from different users are optimized within a fully shared autoregressive parameter space, causing dominant behavioral patterns to progressively dominate the model evolution process while underrepresented patterns become increasingly overlooked. To address this issue, we propose a self-evolving memory paradigm for generative recommendation, aiming to enable effective evolution across heterogeneous behavioral patterns. We further identify three key principles for effective self-evolving recommendation systems, including isolated memorization, reinforced evolution, and scalable application. Guided by these principles, we develop LION, a simple yet effective framework centered on a sparse Key-Value memory layer. Specifically, LION introduces sparse memory activation to isolate the evolution of different behavioral patterns, while a consolidation loss is designed to reinforce the learning of underrepresented preference dynamics during continual adaptation. Extensive experiments on diverse real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of LION under various continual evolution settings (e.g., per-period evaluation, user/item group evaluation, and evolution convergence analysis). The codes are released at https://github.com/JazyJiang/Self-Evolving-Memory-for-Generative-Recommendation.

</details>


### [24] [IROH: Insightful Ranking Of Humor using Multi-Stage Hybrid Retrieval with Rationale-Distilled LLM Judges for JOKER 2026 Track Task 1 English](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15618)
*Ana-Maria Luisa Mocanu,Sebastian Mocanu,Ciprian-Octavian Truică,Elena-Simona Apostol*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: VANGUARD团队提出三阶段检索系统IROH（混合稀疏-稠密检索、交叉编码器重排、LoRA微调LLM评审集成），在CLEF 2026 JOKER Task 1英文赛道以0.6347 MAP位列榜首；消融实验表明理性蒸馏评审是排名质量的主要驱动因素，结构化硬负例反而损害泛化，且更轻量、校准更好的模型可媲美甚至超越更大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向CLEF 2026 JOKER Task 1英文检索任务，构建高性能幽默文本检索系统，并系统探究各组件（检索、重排、评审模型与训练数据构造策略）对最终排名质量的实际贡献。

Method: 三阶段流水线：1）混合稀疏-稠密检索作为首阶段；2）交叉编码器重排；3）LoRA适配的LLM评审集成。使用Gemma 4在"通用"与"分型"两种提示策略下生成查询感知理性依据，并构造至多四类结构化硬负例用于训练数据。消融覆盖三种交叉编码器架构、四种稠密嵌入器与八种评审配置。

Result: 在JOKER Task 1英文赛道以0.6347 MAP排名第一。三点发现：(1) 理性蒸馏评审是排名质量的主要驱动因素，而将理性依据附加到首阶段索引的贡献可忽略；(2) 结构化硬负例在几乎所有配置下损害泛化，尽管会抬高本地验证分数；(3) 更轻量、校准更好的模型与更大模型相当或更强——通用理性的Qwen2.5-7B评审达0.6055 MAP，优于所有Gemma-4-31B配置，且通用理性相对分型理性的优势几乎全部集中在较小模型上。

Conclusion: 系统整体有效并取得领先性能；实践中应优先采用理性蒸馏评审，谨慎使用结构化硬负例（易造成本地验证过拟合与泛化下降），并倾向选择更轻量、校准更佳的模型而非单纯放大模型规模。

Abstract: Our team, VANGUARD, presents IROH (Insightful Ranking of Humor), a three-stage retrieval system for JOKER Task 1 English at CLEF 2026, achieving first place on the leaderboard with 0.6347 MAP. Our pipeline combines hybrid sparse-dense retrieval, cross-encoder reranking, and a LoRA-adapted Large Language Model judge ensemble. We employ Gemma 4 to generate query-aware rationales under two prompt strategies, generic and typed, and produce up to four types of structured hard negatives for training data construction. Through an ablation across three cross-encoder architectures, four dense embedders, and eight judge configurations, our key findings are threefold: (1) the rationale-distilled judge is the primary driver of ranking quality, whereas appending rationales to the first-stage index contributes negligibly; (2) structured hard negatives degrade generalisation in nearly all configurations despite inflating local validation scores; and (3) across the components we ablate, the lighter, better-calibrated model is competitive with or stronger than its larger counterpart, with the generic-rationale Qwen2.5-7B judge (0.6055 MAP) outperforming every Gemma-4-31B configuration, and the advantage of generic over typed rationales is concentrated almost entirely in the smaller model.

</details>

### [25] [Scaling Chance-Constrained Correlated Equilibrium Computation for Exclusive Resource-Assignment Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13565)
*Jaehan Im,Ufuk Topcu,David Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 该文针对机会约束相关均衡计算量随智能体数量指数增长的问题，针对共享资源竞争类博弈提出“精确一对一资源分配”限制，将联合动作数从O(2^(nr))降至O(n^r)，并给出非空性的充要条件及保留全部纯纳什均衡的充分条件，实验显示中位计算时间减少99.81%且协调性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机会约束相关均衡为成本不确定、由中央协调者协调的自利智能体提供了稳健协调方法，但其计算需考虑所有联合动作，数量随智能体数目指数增长，导致可扩展性差；尤其当多个智能体竞争共享资源且同时使用不安全或不理想时，直接求解不可行。

Method: 针对共享资源竞争博弈引入“精确一对一资源分配”限制，即每个资源恰好分配给一个智能体，从而将联合动作数从O(2^(nr))降至O(n^r)（n个智能体、r个资源）。在受限联合动作集上求解问题，同时保留所有与单边偏离相关的激励约束；并分析受限问题可行集与机会约束纯纳什均衡凸包的关系，推导非空性充要条件及保留无限制博弈全部机会约束纯纳什均衡的充分条件。

Result: 理论上证明受限问题的可行集恰为精确一对一分配集内机会约束纯纳什均衡的凸包，从而给出非空性的充要条件；并导出使无限制博弈中所有机会约束纯纳什均衡均被该限制保留的充分条件。在垂直起降场离场走廊协调场景的数值实验中，相对无限制模型，中位计算时间减少99.81%，同时取得等价的协调性能。

Conclusion: 精确一对一资源分配限制在保持激励约束与协调性能的同时显著降低机会约束相关均衡的计算复杂度，为共享资源竞争场景提供了可扩展且可证明的协调建模方法，适用于如垂直起降场走廊协调等实际应用。

Abstract: The chance-constrained correlated equilibrium concept provides a robust coordination technique for self-interested agents whose costs are uncertain to a central coordinator, but the number of joint actions considered in its computation grows exponentially with the number of agents. We propose an exact-one resource-assignment restriction for games in which agents compete for shared resources and simultaneous use of a resource is undesirable or unsafe. The restriction allows each resource to be assigned to exactly one agent, reducing the number of joint actions considered from O(2^(nr)) to O(n^r) for n agents and r resources. The resulting problem is solved over the restricted joint action set while retaining all incentive constraints associated with unilateral deviations. We show that the feasible set of the restricted problem is exactly the convex hull of the chance-constrained pure Nash equilibria contained in the exact-one assignment set, yielding a necessary and sufficient condition for nonemptiness. We further derive a sufficient condition under which all chance-constrained pure Nash equilibria of the unrestricted game are retained by the restriction. Numerical experiments in a vertiport departure-corridor coordination scenario demonstrate a 99.81% reduction in median computation time relative to the unrestricted formulation while achieving equivalent coordination performance.

</details>


### [26] [Online Fair Division: Pushing the Frontier of Approximate Proportionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13650)
*Yingjian Du,Ankang Sun*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究确定性在线公平分配中PROP1的可实现保证，分析未来信息（尤其是最大物品价值MIV）对保证的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线公平分配中，物品随时间到达且必须立即不可撤销地分配，未来信息未知。理解在即时决策下可实现何种公平性，是核心问题。本文聚焦PROP1，并探究提前获知未来信息能否提升保证。

Method: 针对n个代理、非负可加估值、商品数未知且对手可自适应决策的确定性在线分配。分别分析无额外信息情形与算法预先知道每个代理最大物品价值（MIV）的情形，并给出算法的保证与相应下界。

Result: 无额外信息时，回答Choo等人的开放问题：给出确定性算法保证Ω(1/log(nm))-PROP1；并证明对任意固定n及充分大的m，任意确定性算法存在实例使其PROP1因子为O(log log m / log m)。在MIV已知情形，给出竞争比为1/2的确定性算法，改进Choo等人的1/n；并证明即使两名代理且MIV精确已知，也无确定性算法能达到任意接近1的竞争比。

Conclusion: 确定性在线公平分配中，无额外信息时可获得对数级近似的PROP1保证且该量级在上下界意义下基本紧；提前获知MIV可将保证提升至常数1/2，但仍无法达到接近完美的竞争比。

Abstract: Online fair division captures allocation problems in which indivisible resources arrive over time and must be assigned before future resources are known. Understanding what fairness remains achievable when allocation decisions are immediate and irrevocable is a fundamental question in this setting. We study deterministic online allocation among $n$ agents with nonnegative additive valuations, where the number of goods is unknown and the adversary can adapt to previous allocation decisions. We focus on proportionality up to one good (PROP1) and examine how advance future information affects the achievable guarantees.
  In the setting without additional information, we answer an open question in Choo et al. that asks whether a nontrivial deterministic approximation for PROP1 can be obtained. In particular, we present a deterministic algorithm that guarantees $Ω(1/\log(nm))$-PROP1, where $m$ is the number of goods. Moreover, we complement this result by showing that, for every fixed $n$ and all sufficiently large $m$, every deterministic algorithm has an instance with $m$ goods on which its PROP1 factor is $O(\log\log m/\log m)$. We also study the setting where the algorithm knows in advance the maximum item value (MIV) for every agent. With MIV information, we give a deterministic algorithm with competitive ratio 1/2, improving the $1/n$ guarantee in Choo et al. We also show that no deterministic algorithm can achieve a competitive ratio arbitrarily close to one, even for two agents with exact MIV information.

</details>


### [27] [Revisiting EFX and fPO Allocations for Binary Chores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13970)
*Zehan Lin,Xiaowei Wu,Shengwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文将二元加性成本下不可分家务的公平高效分配研究扩展到不等权益情形：提出面向家务的词典势函数，证明其最小化解同时满足WEFX与fPO，并给出多项式时间局部搜索算法；同时揭示不等权益下"两全其美"保证的尖锐障碍，但在等权益情形下构造了事前EF+fPO、事后EFX+fPO的多项式时间抽签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元估值在商品分配中已被广泛研究，但其在家务（chores）场景下的对应问题理解仍很有限；已有工作（Tao et al., TCS 2025）仅证明了等权益二元家务中EFX与fPO相容。本文旨在填补不等权益下二元家务的公平与效率共存问题，并探究"两全其美"（best-of-both-worlds）保证在不等权益下是否可行。

Method: 1) 针对家务设计新的词典势函数（lexicographic potential function），证明任何最小化该势函数的分配同时满足WEFX和fPO；2) 以该势函数为引导，设计多项式时间局部搜索算法计算满足WEFX与fPO的分配；3) 对"两全其美"保证进行不可能性分析（两个代理人、相同二元加性成本下的事前WEF与事后WEF1冲突）；4) 在等权益情形下，多项式时间构造事前EF+fPO、且每个实现均EFX+fPO的抽签。

Result: 在不等权益二元家务下，成功计算出同时满足WEFX与fPO的分配，并由新的词典势函数保证，配合多项式时间局部搜索算法实现。在"两全其美"方面，证明不等权益下存在尖锐障碍：即使仅两个代理人且成本函数相同，也不存在同时满足事前WEF与事后WEF1的抽签。相反，在等权益情形下，可在多项式时间构造事前EF+fPO、事后EFX+fPO的抽签。

Conclusion: 二元家务在不等权益下WEFX与fPO可兼容并可在多项式时间内计算，但"两全其美"保证因不等权益而受阻；等权益情形下则同时具备强事前与事后保证，凸显权益结构对公平抽签可行性的关键影响。

Abstract: We study fair and efficient allocations of indivisible chores under binary additive costs, with a focus on unequal entitlements and best-of-both-worlds guarantees. While binary valuations have been extensively studied for goods, their chore counterpart remains much less understood. A notable exception is due to Tao et al. (TCS 2025), who established that EFX and fPO are compatible for binary chores with equal entitlements. We revisit binary chores under unequal entitlements and establish the computation of allocations that satisfy WEFX and fPO. Our key technical contribution is a new lexicographic potential function tailored to chores: any allocation minimizing this potential ensures both WEFX and fPO. This potential further guides a polynomial-time local-search algorithm for computing allocations with such fairness and efficiency guarantees. For best-of-both-worlds guarantees, we show that unequal entitlements create a sharp obstruction: even with two agents and identical binary additive costs, no lottery can simultaneously satisfy ex-ante WEF and ex-post WEF1. In contrast, for equal entitlements, we construct in polynomial time a lottery that is ex-ante EF and fPO, while every realization is both EFX and fPO.

</details>


### [28] [Consistency-Robustness Tradeoffs for Strategyproof Scheduling with Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14088)
*Hau Chan,Jianan Lin,Chenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究带预测的无关机器上的策略防伪调度，提出 EdgeSkip 机制，在一致性-鲁棒性权衡上改进了已有工作并给出匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带预测的调度问题中，如何在保证策略防伪（strategyproofness）的同时，利用预测提升最坏情况（makespan）性能，且明确一致性与鲁棒性之间的最优权衡，是本文的核心动机。此前 Balkanski, Gkatzelis, and Tan 仅给出 (6, 2n) 的保证，存在改进空间且缺乏紧确下界。

Method: 本文提出 EdgeSkip：一种确定性的、属于逐作业加权机制（job-wise weighted mechanisms）的策略防伪机制。该类机制通过依赖预测的权重独立分配每个作业。EdgeSkip 使用由预测计算的多项式时间 2-近似参考调度及标准权衡参数；另研究无计算限制下使用最优参考调度的变体，以及一个容错变体。

Result: EdgeSkip（多项式时间、2-近似参考调度）实现 4-一致、(2n−2)-鲁棒，优于 (6, 2n)。无计算限制且用最优参考调度时，达到 C-一致与 max{n,(n−1)C/(C−1)}-鲁棒（任意 C>1）。对逐作业加权机制证明匹配的信息论下界，从而确定该类机制的精确一致性-鲁棒性权衡；对任意确定性策略防伪机制给出鲁棒性下界 max{n, C/(C−1)}。容错变体改进已有保证，实验显示良好经验性能。

Conclusion: EdgeSkip 在一致性-鲁棒性权衡上改进现有结果，并为逐作业加权机制确定了紧确权衡边界，同时给出更一般的确定性策略防伪机制鲁棒性下界，兼具理论最优性与实际有效性。

Abstract: We study strategyproof scheduling on \(n\) unrelated machines with predictions. Each machine is controlled by an agent with privately known processing times, while the mechanism receives a public prediction of the processing-time matrix before the agents report. The objective is to minimize the makespan subject to strategyproofness. We measure performance by consistency, the approximation guarantee for correct predictions, and robustness, the worst-case guarantee for arbitrary predictions.
  We introduce \textsc{EdgeSkip}, a deterministic strategyproof member of the class of job-wise weighted mechanisms. Such mechanisms allocate each job independently using prediction-dependent weights. Using a polynomial-time \(2\)-approximate reference schedule computed from the prediction and a standard tradeoff parameter, \textsc{EdgeSkip} is \(4\)-consistent and \((2n-2)\)-robust, improving on the \((6,2n)\) guarantee of Balkanski, Gkatzelis, and Tan. Without computational restrictions, \textsc{EdgeSkip} with an optimal reference schedule is \(C\)-consistent and \(\max\{n,(n-1)C/(C-1)\}\)-robust for every \(C>1\). We prove a matching information-theoretic lower bound for all job-wise weighted mechanisms, thereby determining the exact consistency--robustness tradeoff for this class. For arbitrary deterministic strategyproof mechanisms, we establish the robustness lower bound \(\max\{n,C/(C-1)\}\) for every \(C>1\). We also study an error-tolerant variant and improve upon prior guarantees. Experiments demonstrate that our mechanisms achieve good empirical performance.

</details>


### [29] [Approximating Optimal Welfare in Complementary Allocation under Decentralized Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14301)
*Meryem Essaidi*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究多个机构各自只观测个体某一维度需求时，如何分配可分割的互补品。以仅依赖本地信息的最优规则DEC为基准，发现本地观测可导致与m成线性的福利损失；提出阈值转介机制，用一比特报告达到OPT/[4(1+ln(2/OPT))]的近似，并用几何阈值阶梯以Θ(log log(1/OPT))比特实现OPT/8的常数因子近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中互补资源常由不同机构按各自可见的需求维度分配。若某干预需要多种互补资源，单一机构可能知道个体缺少自身资源，却无法判断补齐后是否形成有效组合。作者希望在剥离激励问题、仅保留信息结构的条件下，衡量这种"信息分割"带来的福利代价，并寻找简单报告语言能否挽回损失。

Method: 设定m种可分割互补品，每个机构仅观测基线可及性剖面中的自身坐标。以DEC为宽松的分散式基准，即在合作机构、已知总体分布与容量下仅依据本地信息的最优规则。定义阈值转介：机构报告其价值是否低于公共阈值，清算规则仅使用联合报告。核心工具是剖面级收费证书：若最优效用至少为某阈值两倍，则OPT在每个缺陷坐标上至少支付该阈值。

Result: 本地观测单独作用可使最优协调政策任意低效：损失因子与m成线性（即使容量相等），在三机构下与OPT成反比。单阈值转介以每机构一比特达到至少OPT/[4(1+ln(2/OPT))]，且等收入族表明该对数损失对单阈值是紧的。几何阈值阶梯以Θ(log log(1/OPT))比特恢复OPT/8，并在该类机制内证明该比特数为必要。

Conclusion: 结果表明，简单的报告语言可将本地信息造成的巨大福利损失压缩为常数因子恢复：单阈值给出对数近似，阶梯式阈值以双重对数比特给出常数因子近似。信息分割的代价可通过聚合报告与阈值结构有效缓解。

Abstract: Complementary resources are often allocated by agencies that see different coordinates of an individual's needs. If an intervention requires complementary resources, an agency may know if said person lacks its own resource; yet not know if supplying completes a useful bundle. We study allocation of $m$ divisible complementary goods when each agency observes only its own coordinate of a baseline-access profile.
  To isolate information from incentives, we measure the welfare cost of this split against a generous decentralized benchmark DEC: the best rule that acts on local information alone, with cooperative agencies and known population distribution and capacities. Even so, local observation alone can make optimally coordinated policies arbitrarily inefficient: a factor linear in $m$ (even with equal capacities), and a factor inverse in OPT (with but three agencies).
  Threshold referrals recover much of this loss: an agency reports if its value lies below a public threshold, and a clearing rule uses only the joint reports. Aggregating types by their reports captures a rectangle under the optimal half-utility survival curve. Our main tool is a profile-level charging certificate: if optimal utility is at least twice a threshold, OPT pays at least that on every deficient coordinate. This yields welfare at least ${\rm OPT}/[4(1+\ln(2/{\rm OPT}))]$ with one bit per agency; and an equal-revenue family shows this log-loss tight for one threshold. A geometric threshold ladder improves this to a constant fraction of OPT with doubly-logarithmic messages: a staircase recovers ${\rm OPT}/8$ with $Θ(\log\log(1/{\rm OPT}))$ bits, and this is necessary within the class.
  The results isolate how a simple reporting language turns large local-info losses into constant-factor recovery. In short: One threshold gets a log-approximation. A staircase, with log-of-log bits, a constant-factor.

</details>


### [30] [Envy-Free Chore Allocation with Unit Subsidies under Negative Dichotomous Valuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14465)
*Mainak Sarkar,Soumyarup Sadhukhan*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究负二分估值（每件杂务边际负效用为0或1）下不可分杂务的补贴分配，证明每名代理人至多1单位补贴、总补贴不超过n-1即可实现无嫉妒，且分配本身满足EF1，界限紧，可在多项式时间内计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Barman等（2022）针对二分物品模型证明了每名代理人至多1单位补贴足以实现无嫉妒，本文探究该结论在杂务（负估值）对应情形下是否成立，并考察在无额外结构假设下能否保持同样的最优补贴保证。

Method: 从Tao等（2025）算法产生的无嫉妒部分分配出发，将剩余杂务分配给终端相等图中尾部强连通分量的不同代理人，并通过相关相等图中的后向闭包确定受补贴的代理人。

Result: 对任意负二分估值实例，存在完备分配A和补贴向量p∈{0,1}^n且∑p_i≤n-1，使(A,p)无嫉妒；且A在补贴前即满足EF1。该界是紧的，分配与补贴可在值预言模型中多项式时间计算。若同时要求帕累托最优，则一般无法维持单位补贴保证。

Conclusion: 杂务情形下单位补贴保证与物品模型同样最优，且无需超出二值边际的结构假设；但该保证与帕累托最优性一般不相容。

Abstract: We study the allocation of indivisible chores with subsidies under negative dichotomous valuations, where the marginal disutility of every chore is either zero or one. This is the chore analogue of the dichotomous goods model of Barman et al. (2022), for which a subsidy of at most one unit per agent is known to suffice for envy-freeness.
  We show that the same optimal guarantee holds for chores, under no structural assumption beyond binary marginals. For every instance with negative dichotomous valuations, there exists a complete allocation $A$ and a subsidy vector $p\in\{0,1\}^n$ with $\sum_{i\in N} p_i\le n-1$ such that $(A,p)$ is envy-free. Moreover, the allocation $A$ is EF1 even before subsidies are paid. The bound is tight, and the allocation and subsidies can be computed in polynomial time in the value-oracle model. We further show that the unit-subsidy guarantee cannot, in general, be maintained if Pareto optimality is also required.
  Our algorithm starts from an envy-free partial allocation produced by the algorithm of Tao et al. (2025), assigns the remaining chores to distinct agents in a tail strongly connected component of the terminal equality graph, and determines the subsidised agents through a backward closure in the associated equality graph.

</details>

*Qixuan Zai,Randall Berry*
