TL;DR: 研究可拥堵资源市场中，企业通过定价竞争、消费者基于价格与拥堵程度选择，在未知逆需求曲线下用MARL学习定价，发现独立学习智能体可能形成默契合谋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献已发现独立学习的MARL智能体可产生默契合谋，但尚不清楚在资源可拥堵、消费者同时关注价格与拥堵的市场中这种合谋如何形成，以及学习动态、状态表示与策略互动如何共同影响竞争。

Method: 构建Bertrand竞争模型：企业公布价格，消费者依据价格与拥堵程度选择企业；价格、拥堵与愿意接受服务的消费者数量之间的关系由未知逆需求曲线决定，企业须通过经验学习该曲线。每个企业建模为自利学习智能体，以最大化利润为目标选择价格，采用MARL方法进行独立学习。

Result: 在可拥堵资源市场中，独立学习的定价智能体会发展出默契合谋行为；学习动态、状态表示与策略互动共同塑造了市场竞争格局。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了可拥堵资源市场中定价学习的合谋机制，对经济学习理论及具备学习能力的市场机制设计均具有启示意义。

Abstract: This paper investigates multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) in settings where firms compete for customers using congestible resources. We consider Bertrand competition in which firms compete by announcing prices and customers choose among firms based on both price and congestion. The relationship between price, congestion and the quantity of customers willing to accept service is governed by an unknown inverse demand curve, which firms must learn through experience. Each firm is modeled as a self-interested learning agent that chooses its price to maximize profit. A growing literature has shown that independently learning MARL agents can develop tacitly collusive behavior. We examine how such behavior emerges in markets with congestible resources. Our results provide insight into how learning dynamics, state representation, and strategic interaction jointly shape competition, with implications for both economic learning and the design of learning-enabled markets.

</details>


### [32] [Strategic Decision Focused Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14907)
*Tinashe Handina,Yuehan Diao,Adam Wierman,Eric Mazumdar*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出"策略型决策聚焦学习"（strategic DFL）框架：ML系统预测外生状态，多个策略性agent在观察到预测后进行博弈。研究表明预测准确率与均衡收益间可能呈非单调关系（更准的预测反而降低表现），并提出算法应对该挑战，在野生动物保护和基础设施防护基准上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策聚焦学习（DFL）研究大多假设单一决策者在孤立环境中优化，忽略了预测会通过影响多个策略性agent的行为及其均衡结果而产生反馈。作者希望刻画这种策略互动如何改变学习问题的本质。

Method: 将问题形式化为策略型决策聚焦学习：ML系统预测不被agent行为影响的外生状态，agent在观察（或依据）预测后进行博弈，预测通过改变agent策略进而影响均衡。作者分析准确率—均衡收益景观，并据此提出针对性算法。

Result: 理论证明预测准确率与均衡收益的景观可能非单调，即更准确的预测可能导致绩效下降；并在野生动物保护（如反盗猎巡逻部署）与基础设施防护等基准上验证了所提算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 在设计预测器时必须纳入策略性交互的考量，策略因素从根本上改变了DFL问题，忽略agent的策略反应会导致次优甚至有害的预测。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) predictions are increasingly being used to guide decision-making, giving rise to the problem of decision-focused learning (DFL) where predictors are optimized for downstream decision quality rather than accuracy alone. However, most existing work assumes a single decision-maker optimizing in isolation. This paper formalizes strategic decision-focused learning, where an ML system predicts an exogenous state that some agents observe before playing a game. For example, a park ranger may predict wildlife locations to allocate anti-poaching patrols against strategic poachers. While the exogenous state is unaffected by agent actions, predictions influence agents' strategies and the resulting equilibrium. We find that strategic considerations fundamentally change the learning problem. In particular, we show the prediction accuracy-equilibrium payoff landscape can be non-monotonic, i.e., better predictions can degrade performance. We propose algorithmic approaches to address these challenges and validate them across benchmarks in wildlife conservation and infrastructure protection. Our theory and experiments highlight the importance of accounting for strategic interactions when designing predictors.

</details>


### [33] [Improved Impossibility Bounds for Maximin Share Allocations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15085)
*Tomer Ezra,Tamar Garbuz*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 该论文改进了不可分割物品分配中最大最小共享（MMS）的不可行性下界：对货物将不可行缺口从 1/n^4 提升到 1-Ω((log n)^-2)，对家务给出 1+Ω((log n)^-2) 的乘性下界，并改进了四代理情形的通用不可行界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMS 是分配不可分割物品的核心公平基准，但即使在加性偏好下也未必可同时达到。既有研究多集中于近似可行性保证，而对定量不可行性上界关注较少，因此需要给出更强的不可行下界以刻画 MMS 逼近的极限。

Method: 作者构造加性偏好的货物与家务实例：对任意充分大的代理数 n，设计使任意分配都令某代理至多获得其 MMS 的 1-Ω((log n)^-2) 比例的货物实例，以及对任意分配都令某代理承担至少其 MMS 的 1+Ω((log n)^-2) 倍成本的家务实例；同时显式构造四代理十一物品实例以获得常数不可行界。

Result: 对充分大的 n，货物实例中任意分配都存在代理仅得其 MMS 的 1-Ω((log n)^-2) 比例，把 Feige、Sapir 与 Tauber（2021）的 1/n^4 缺口指数级改进为逆多对数缺口；家务实例中任意分配都存在代理承担至少其 MMS 的 1+Ω((log n)^-2) 倍成本。由此可知，对任意固定 ε>0，货物的 1-O(n^-ε) 保证与家务的 1+O(n^-ε) 保证均不可行。此外，四代理十一物品实例将通用不可行界从货物的 39/40 改进到 20/21，从家务的 44/43 改进到 31/30。

Conclusion: MMS 的不可行性远比以往认知严重：货物与家务的近似保证均无法达到多项式级别的 1±O(n^-ε)，其上界由逆多对数项所主导；常数层面亦可将通用不可行界进一步收紧至 20/21（货物）与 31/30（家务）。

Abstract: The maximin share (MMS) is a central fairness benchmark for allocating indivisible items, but it need not be simultaneously attainable even under additive preferences. While extensive work has developed approximation guarantees, quantitative impossibility bounds have received comparatively little attention. We establish improved asymptotic and constant impossibility bounds for both goods and chores.
  For every sufficiently large number $n$ of agents, we construct additive goods instances in which every allocation gives some agent at most a $1-Ω((\log n)^{-2})$ fraction of her MMS. This strengthens the $1/n^4$ shortfall of Feige, Sapir, and Tauber (2021) to an inverse-polylogarithmic shortfall, an exponential improvement on the logarithmic scale of $n$. For chores, we construct instances in which every allocation gives some agent cost at least a $1+Ω((\log n)^{-2})$ factor of her MMS. Consequently, for every fixed $\varepsilon>0$, guarantees of $1-O(n^{-\varepsilon})$ for goods and $1+O(n^{-\varepsilon})$ for chores are impossible.
  We also give four-agent, eleven-item instances that improve the universal impossibility bounds from $39/40$ to $20/21$ for goods and from $44/43$ to $31/30$ for chores.

</details>


### [34] [Tight Subsidy Bounds for Weighted Proportional Allocation of Mixed Manna](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15208)
*Jugal Garg,Eklavya Sharma,Xiaowei Wu*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 研究混合manna下可分割物品的公平分配，引入货币补贴恢复比例性并最小化补贴总额，证明紧界τ(n)≈n/4，并给出多项式时间算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在混合manna（同一物品对不同主体可能是商品或负担）且权利可能不均等的设定下，比例分配未必存在，因此需借助货币补贴恢复比例性。此前仅对纯商品或纯负担情形有上界n/3-1/6，且下界约为n/4，存在间隙待弥合。

Method: 采用KKM不动点定理的新应用，将KKM框架推广到基于份额（share-based）的公平性概念；针对加权混合manna的一般设定进行构造性证明，并设计多项式时间算法。

Result: 证明补贴总额上界恰好为τ(n)≈n/4（每项(负)效用有界于1），与下界吻合，得到紧界；结果适用于加权混合manna，同时分配满足加权WPROP1；对任意固定主体数可在多项式时间内计算。

Conclusion: 弥合了混合manna比例性补贴的上下界间隙，解决了Wu et al.(2023)与Garg et al.(2026)提出的开放问题，并拓展了KKM方法在公平分配中的适用范围。

Abstract: We study the problem of fairly allocating m indivisible items among n agents with possibly unequal entitlements in the mixed manna setting, where each item may be perceived as a good or a chore by different agents. We focus on the fundamental fairness notion of proportionality. Since proportional allocations need not exist in this setting, we allow monetary subsidies to restore proportionality while minimizing the total subsidy. When each item's (dis)utility is bounded by 1, a total subsidy of at least τ(n) \approx n/4 may be necessary. For goods-only or chores-only instances, the best previously known upper bound was n/3-1/6 due to Wu and Zhou~(2024). We close this gap by proving that a total subsidy of at most τ(n) always suffices, thereby establishing the tight subsidy bound. Our results hold even in the more general setting of weighted mixed manna, resolving an open question posed by~Wu et al. (2023) and Garg et al. (2026). The allocation also satisfies weighted proportionality up to one item (WPROP1). Our proof develops a novel application of the Knaster-Kuratowski-Mazurkiewicz (KKM) fixed-point theorem, extending the KKM framework to share-based fairness notions. Finally, we design a polynomial-time algorithm to compute such allocations for any fixed number of agents.

</details>


### [35] [Reconfiguration in Fair Division Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15358)
*Fabian Frank,Warut Suksompong*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究不可分物品公平分配的重构问题，聚焦EF$k$公平性层级：证明同尺寸向量的EF1分配在固定k下未必存在EF$k$重构路径（即使来自环消除算法或MNW），但递归平衡选取序列（含轮询）产生的分配总存在保持EF2的路径；判定EF1重构路径存在性对任意固定代理数NP难，并进一步研究允许转移的模型获得额外连通性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平分配中常需从一个公平分配迁移到另一个（如因偏好变化或代理动态调整），而逐步交换时中间分配仍需保持公平，因此需刻画不同公平概念下的重构可行性。EF$k$作为EF与EF1之间的层级，其重构边界尚不清楚。

Method: 采用交换（swap）序列的重构模型，分析同尺寸向量的EF1分配之间能否存在中间保持EF$k$的路径；结合环消除算法、最大纳什福利（MNW）、递归平衡选取序列等经典EF1构造方法进行构造性与不可能性证明；并通过NP-hard性归约证明EF1重构路径判定复杂性；另设允许转移（transfer）的更宽松模型进行连通性分析。

Result: 对任意固定k，存在两个同尺寸向量EF1分配（包括来自环消除算法或MNW的解）之间不存在保持EF$k$的重构路径；但递归平衡选取序列（含轮询）产生的分配总可通过保持EF2的路径连通；判定EF1重构路径存在性对任意固定代理数为NP-hard；在允许转移的模型下得到额外的连通性保证。

Conclusion: EF$k$重构在交换模型下存在强不可能性，即使经典EF1算法输出也不保证高阶EF$k$连通；但轮询类分配具备EF2连通性，并给出EF1重构的NP难下界；允许转移可缓解部分连通性限制。这刻画了公平分配重构的可行边界与算法结构依赖。

Abstract: We revisit reconfiguration in the fair allocation of indivisible goods, where the goal is to transform one fair allocation into another through a sequence of exchanges while preserving fairness at every step. Our focus is on the hierarchy of envy-freeness up to $k$ goods (EF$k$). We show that for any fixed $k$, two EF1 allocations with the same size vector need not admit a reconfiguration path whose intermediate allocations satisfy EF$k$. This impossibility persists even when the two allocations arise from standard EF1 approaches: the envy cycle elimination algorithm or the maximum Nash welfare solution. In contrast, we prove that allocations with the same size vector produced by recursively balanced picking sequences, including round-robin, are always connected via a path that maintains EF2. We also show that deciding whether an EF1 reconfiguration path exists is NP-hard for any fixed number of agents. Furthermore, we complement these exchange-based results by studying a more permissive model that also allows transfers, establishing additional connectivity guarantees.

</details>


### [36] [Differentially Private Multicolor Discrepancy and Fair Division of Indivisible Goods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15372)
*Max Dupré la Tour*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究纯差分隐私下不可分物品的公平分配问题，在增益性、多项式时间与下界三方面改进了Manurangsi和Suksompong的已有工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续Manurangsi与Suksompong开启的"差分隐私+不可分物品公平分配"研究方向：在保护每个参与者效用信息（entry privacy）的前提下，实现尽可能强的无嫉妒（envy-freeness, EF）保证，并降低对代理人数量n的依赖。

Method: 对n个代理人、m个物品、非负加性效用与固定隐私参数，给出entry-private算法，核心技术是"多色差异（multicolor discrepancy）"的私有算法（可独立使用）；该算法虽可能指数时间，但达到共识无嫉妒（consensus EF）。随后在物品价值取自规模为D的公开字母表这一附加结构下，设计多项式时间的entry-private算法以获得普通无嫉妒。最后对必须移除的物品数给出Ω(log n)下界证明（即使效用为二值）。

Result: (1) 指数时间算法以高概率实现 consensus EF up to O(√n + log³m)，显著优于此前普通EF的O(n log m)；(2) 当物品价值取自公开字母表（大小D）时，多项式时间算法以高概率实现普通 EF up to O(polylog(mD))；(3) 证明在entry privacy下，物品数足够多时，即使二值效用，也必须移除Ω(log n)个物品才能实现普通EF。

Conclusion: 该工作把隐私公平分配的效用损失从n的线性依赖降到亚线性（√n及polylog），并通过共识EF与字母表结构分别换取更强保证与多项式时间，同时以Ω(log n)下界刻画了隐私约束下的固有代价。

Abstract: We study the fair division of indivisible goods under pure differential privacy, continuing the line of work initiated by Manurangsi and Suksompong. For $n$ agents with nonnegative additive utilities over $m$ goods and a fixed privacy parameter, we give an entry-private algorithm that, with high probability, achieves consensus envy-freeness up to $O(\sqrt n+\log^3 m)$ goods. This substantially improves the dependence on $n$ over the previous $O(n\log m)$ guarantee for ordinary envy-freeness, while providing the stronger consensus guarantee. A key ingredient is a private algorithm for multicolor discrepancy, which may be of independent interest. Our algorithm may require exponential time.
  We also obtain substantially stronger guarantees under additional structure: when all item values belong to a public alphabet of size $D$, we give a polynomial-time entry-private algorithm achieving ordinary envy-freeness up to $O(\operatorname{polylog}(mD))$ goods with high probability.
  Finally, we prove an $Ω(\log n)$ lower bound on the number of goods that must be removed to achieve ordinary envy-freeness under entry privacy, for sufficiently many goods, even with binary utilities.

</details>


### [37] [Delegating Authorization to Misaligned Agents: Coalitional Alignment and Safe Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15803)
*Natalie Collina,Surbhi Goel,Aaron Roth,Sikata Bela Sengupta*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究长时程AI代理的控制安全问题：将高风险动作的审批委托给多个评审代理构成的小组，提出"k-稳健联盟对齐"（k-robust coalitional alignment）这一比个体对齐更弱的条件，并证明其是阈值投票规则安全的充要条件；该刻画可推广至折扣MDP的序贯控制，并在策略性投票下给出Nash均衡安全性的保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程代理的每一步动作都会改变状态并影响未来动作轨迹，若代理未完全对齐，则必须对重大动作预先审批才能保证安全；但逐步人工审批使人类注意力成为瓶颈。将审批委托给其他AI代理又会引入同样的对齐问题——评审者本身可能未对齐。因此需要寻找一种弱于"个体对齐"、却足以给出安全保证的评审机制条件。

Method: 1) 设定一个评审小组，每个评审代理报告提议者（proposer）的动作提案相对于基线策略是否改善其自身效用。2) 采用容忍k个否决的阈值投票规则。3) 证明安全性等价于：移除任意k个评审者后，委托人的效用可写成剩余评审者效用的非负线性组合，再加上一个在所有可行提案上非负的项——称此为k-稳健联盟对齐。4) 将该刻画推广到序贯控制：在折扣MDP与任意提议者下，逐状态安全是诱导策略不劣于基线策略的充要条件。5) 分析策略性投票情形，考察全小组在奖励函数空间中的覆盖与Nash均衡安全性。6) 用现有评审者模型进行实验验证。

Result: 理论上：k-稳健联盟对齐是阈值规则安全的充要条件；在折扣MDP中逐状态安全等价于诱导策略匹配或优于基线。策略性投票下，奖励函数空间中的全小组覆盖可保证一致同意规则下所有Nash均衡都是安全的；而更宽松的阈值规则即使在评审者个体对齐时也可能允许不安全均衡。实验上：使用现有评审者模型，集体评审即使在没有个体对齐者、且容忍部分否决时仍可保持可靠性（sound）。

Conclusion: 安全委托评审不要求每个评审者自身对齐，而要求评审小组满足k-稳健联盟对齐这一集体性质；该性质是阈值审批规则安全的充要条件，并可自然推广到序贯决策与策略性投票场景，为设计可扩展的AI审批机制提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Long-running AI agents create a control problem: each action they take changes the state, which in turn affects the trajectory of future actions. If the agent is not fully aligned, then guaranteeing safety requires approving consequential actions before allowing them to be executed. But requiring human approval at every step makes attention a bottleneck. Delegating review to other AI agents raises the same alignment problem: the reviewers may themselves be misaligned. We identify a condition on a reviewing panel that is weaker than individual alignment yet necessary and sufficient for a guarantee that the principal fares at least as well in expectation as under a designated baseline policy.
  Each reviewer agent reports whether an action proposal made by a proposer agent improves its own utility relative to the baseline. We show that a threshold rule tolerating $k$ disapprovals is safe exactly when, after any $k$ reviewers are removed, the principal's utility can be written as a nonnegative combination of the remaining reviewers' utilities, plus a term that is nonnegative on every feasible proposal. We call this property $k$-robust coalitional alignment. The characterization lifts to sequential control: in a discounted MDP with an arbitrary proposer agent, safety at every state is both necessary and sufficient for the induced policy to match or improve on the baseline.
  When reviewers vote strategically, full-panel coverage in reward-function space guarantees that every Nash equilibrium is safe under the unanimous approval rule; in contrast, more permissive thresholds can admit unsafe equilibria even when reviewers are individually aligned.
  Experiments with existing reviewer models show that collective review can remain sound without an aligned individual, even when some disapprovals are tolerated.

</details>

*Sushmita Gupta,Sanjay Seetharaman*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMS保证是不可分物品分配的核心公平基准。自Kurokawa等证明精确MMS分配未必存在后，大量工作转向近似MMS的存在性与计算。然而Bouveret与Lemaître十余年前提出的基础复杂度问题——判定精确MMS分配是否存在有多难——始终未解：加性估值仅有Δ_2^P（即P^NP）上界而无硬度结果，2-加性估值虽已知NP-难但与上界存在显著间隙，且加性与k-加性下MMS存在性的精确复杂度被列为公开问题。

Method: 针对加性物品，通过归约证明判定MMS存在性为D^P-难；针对2-加性估值，在单调子模物品实例类上构造归约证明Δ_2^P-完全；进一步分别针对3个代理人与无限制代理人数构造弱/强coNP-难归约；并构造强硬度归约，使最优MMS近似比率出现逆多项式间隙；最后通过保持MMS存在性的多项式时间变换，将物品情形结论迁移至杂务情形。

Result: 得到加性物品下MMS存在性判定的首个硬度结果（D^P-难）；2-加性估值下实现Δ_2^P-完全，闭合复杂度间隙，且据称是此类首个结果；证明3个代理人时弱coNP-难，与两代理人已知存在性保证构成精确二分；无限制代理人数时为强coNP-难；强硬度构造产生最优MMS近似比率的逆多项式间隙，除非P=NP否则不存在FPTAS；上述物品情形结果均通过多项式时间变换扩展至杂务情形。

Conclusion: 本文系统推进了精确MMS存在性判定的复杂度刻画，首次给出加性物品下的D^P-硬度，并以Δ_2^P-完全性闭合2-加性估值的复杂度间隙；同时给出3个代理人与无限制代理人数的coNP-难二分，并排除近似最优MMS比率的FPTAS，最后统一推广至杂务场景。

Abstract: The maximin share (MMS) guarantee is a central fairness benchmark for allocating indivisible items. Since Kurokawa, Procaccia and Wang [EC'14, JACM'18] showed that exact MMS allocations need not exist, much work has studied existence and computation of approximate MMS allocations. In contrast, a basic complexity question posed more than a decade ago by Bouveret and Lemaître [JAAMAS'16] has remained unresolved: how hard is it to decide whether an exact MMS allocation exists?
  For additive valuations, Lonc and Truszczynski [JAIR'20] showed membership in $Δ_2^P$ (also known as $P^{NP}$), but no hardness result was known. For the more general class of 2-additive valuations, Bouveret and Lemaître established NP-hardness, leaving a substantial gap to the $Δ_2^P$ upper bound. Moreover, the (precise) complexity of MMS existence in additive and $k$-additive settings was posed as an open question.
  We make progress on all of these fronts: (1) For additive goods, we prove that deciding MMS existence is $D^P$-hard, giving the first hardness result for this longstanding problem. (2) For 2-additive valuations, we close the complexity gap by proving $Δ_2^P$-completeness on a class of instances of monotone submodular goods. To the best of our knowledge this is the first result of this kind.
  We also prove weak coNP-hardness for three agents, thereby establishing a precise dichotomy with the known existence guarantee for two agents; and strong coNP-hardness when the number of agents is unrestricted. Moreover, the strong hardness construction produces an inverse-polynomial gap in the optimal MMS approximation ratio, ruling out an FPTAS for approximating this ratio unless P=NP. Finally, we show that all these results for goods extend to the chores setting through a polynomial-time transformation that preserves MMS existence.

</details>

Abstract: We study participatory budgeting with additive utilities. We introduce a selection rule based on utility-weighted harmonic entropy and a cost penalty, and prove that it returns budget-feasible outcomes satisfying core-up-to-one.

Abstract: Vision-language models are extending video understanding from offline clip analysis to continuous interactive streaming, but most research still emphasizes model capability rather than deployable low-latency interaction. This paper presents a unified edge-cloud system for real-time video VLM applications. Lightweight phone, smart glasses, PC, and pseudo-replay clients publish video and speech to a server runtime that provides shared ASR/TTS, session orchestration, backend adaptation, response delivery, and archive-backed measurement. The system integrates six representative video VLM backends with streaming or interaction-oriented capabilities and evaluates them across backend runtime, media transport, client-observed latency, and interaction behavior. With suitable backend selection and the WebRTC path, the tested system reaches approximately 0.9 to 1.0 s to first VLM text and 1.3 to 1.5 s to first non-silent TTS audio, while exposing backend adaptation costs and differences in real-time interaction behavior.

</details>

### [41] [NOC NOC, who's there? Clustering systems of tree-child and normal networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13336)
*Anna Lindeberg,Marc Hellmuth*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文引入集合系统的"非重叠覆盖"(NOC)性质，证明其等价于逐成员的包含可见性，并用其刻画正规网络与树孩子网络的聚类系统，给出二次界、多项式识别算法及若干跨领域推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统发育网络的聚类系统可编码网络的结构信息，但此前对正规网络(tree-child网络)聚类系统的组合刻画依赖严格兼容性条件，缺乏统一的、基于重叠关系的简洁表述，也缺少直接的识别算法与簇数上界。

Method: 通过集合系统的重叠关系定义NOC性质，证明NOC等价于包含可见性(inclusion-visibility)，即严格兼容性条件的逐成员形式；据此刻画正规网络为满足NOC的半正则网络；利用Hasse图给出规范正规实现，并基于NOC设计多项式时间识别算法。

Result: 证明聚类系统可由正规网络实现当且仅当其满足NOC，当且仅当每个簇均包含可见；此种系统恰为可由树孩子网络实现的系统；Hasse图提供规范正规实现；得到树孩子与正规网络不同簇数的紧二次上界及直接的识别算法。

Conclusion: NOC视角统一并简化了正规与树孩子网络聚类系统的刻画，给出紧的簇数上界与识别算法，并在系统发育之外产生若干推论，包括正规网络计数、包含可见性的序论解释、NOC集合系统的结构性质，以及一般NP难的最小集合覆盖问题的一个可解特例。

Abstract: Clustering systems provide a natural way to encode structural information contained in phylogenetic networks. In this note, we study the clustering systems of normal and tree-child networks through an overlap-based property of set systems, called not-overlap-covered (NOC). We show that the NOC property is equivalent to inclusion-visibility, a memberwise formulation of the strict-compatibility condition previously used for tree-child clustering systems.
  We characterize normal networks as precisely the semi-regular networks whose clustering systems satisfy NOC. Consequently, a clustering system is realized by a normal network if and only if it satisfies NOC, or equivalently, if every one of its clusters is inclusion-visible. In this case, the Hasse diagram provides a canonical normal realization. These are exactly the clustering systems realized by tree-child networks.
  The NOC formulation yields a sharp quadratic upper bound on the number of distinct clusters of tree-child and normal networks and a direct polynomial-time recognition algorithm. Finally, we explore several consequences of the NOC perspective beyond the phylogenetic setting. These include connections to the enumeration of normal networks, an order-theoretic interpretation of inclusion-visibility, structural properties of NOC set systems, and a tractable special case of Minimum Set Cover, which is NP-hard in general.

</details>


### [42] [Prefix Sharing Is a Sorting Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13692)
*Rong He*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM服务中KV cache按前缀复用时，可复用片段（检索段落、工具定义、few-shot样例）的排列顺序如何决定可共享计算量，证明单一全局顺序仅在两片段情况下最优，一般情形渐近错误；提出"最小前缀trie代价等于请求集合上二叉层次结构的最小加权代价"的结构定理，给出O(3^m)精确算法、与最小顶点覆盖和归并排序递归的联系，以及1/2近似算法，并在BEIR检索轨迹上将prefill计算降低17–36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部署系统用单一全局约定固定可复用片段（如检索段落、工具定义、few-shot示例）的拼接顺序，以最大化KV cache的精确前缀匹配复用。作者认为这种约定缺乏理论依据，需要刻画顺序选择与前缀共享量之间的真实关系，并量化全局约定在一般情形下的次优程度。

Method: 将片段顺序选择形式化为前缀trie代价最小化问题，并证明结构定理：最小前缀trie代价等于在请求集合的所有二叉层次结构H上最小化 sum_x w(x) t_x(H)，其中t_x(H)是需用chunk x的请求集合在层次H下的规范分解大小。基于该恒等式给出O(3^m)精确算法（m为片段数），将两片段情形归约为最小顶点覆盖，并分析leave-one-out族的最优解对应二叉树的归并排序递归结构。进一步提出按公共交集进行凝聚聚类（agglomerative clustering）作为可实现节省量的紧1/2近似，并在BM25检索轨迹上（三个BEIR语料）与生产级RAG顺序对比prefill计算量，同时考察按层次DFS顺序服务请求时无界缓存的精确最优性及缓存容量与重排窗口的替代关系。

Result: 理论上证明全局顺序仅当请求至多含两个片段时最优，一般情形渐近错误；在leave-one-out族上，全局顺序付出Theta(n^2)代价而真实最优为Theta(n log n)，最优解对应归并排序递归的最小外部路径长度；按公共交集的凝聚聚类给出可实现节省量的紧1/2近似。实验上，在三个BEIR语料的BM25检索轨迹中，所提布局相较生产RAG顺序将prefill计算减少17–36%，且随检索深度增加优势扩大，与理论预测一致。按层次DFS顺序服务请求时，仅容纳单请求上下文的缓存即可精确达到无界缓存最优，表明缓存容量与重排窗口互为替代。

Conclusion: 可复用片段的排列顺序本质上等价于在请求集合上选择一个二叉层次结构，单一全局顺序并非最优；通过求解该层次结构优化问题（精确或1/2近似），可显著降低RAG场景下LLM服务的prefill计算量，并且合理的DFS服务顺序使有限缓存即可达到无界缓存最优，说明顺序设计与缓存调度可以相互替代。

Abstract: LLM serving reuses KV cache by exact prefix match, so when a prompt is assembled from a set of reusable pieces -- retrieved passages, tool definitions, few-shot exemplars -- the order chosen for those pieces determines how much computation can be shared. Every deployed system fixes that order by a single global convention. We prove this is optimal only when requests contain at most two pieces, and asymptotically wrong in general. Our main result is a structure theorem: the minimum prefix-trie cost equals min_H sum_x w(x) t_x(H) over binary hierarchies H on the requests, where t_x(H) is the canonical decomposition size of the set of requests needing chunk x. Choosing chunk orders is therefore equivalent to choosing one hierarchy over requests. The identity yields an O(3^m) exact algorithm, identifies the two-chunk case as minimum vertex cover, and shows that on the leave-one-out family the optimum is the minimum external path length of a binary tree -- the merge-sort recursion -- so a global order pays Theta(n^2) against a true cost of Theta(n log n). Agglomerative clustering by common intersection is a tight 1/2-approximation for the achievable saving. On BM25 retrieval traces over three BEIR corpora the resulting layout reduces prefill by 17-36% against production RAG ordering, and the margin widens with retrieval depth as the theory predicts. Serving requests in the hierarchy's DFS order finally lets a cache holding one request's context attain the unbounded-cache optimum exactly, so cache capacity and reorder window act as substitutes.

</details>


### [43] [Beyond Kruskal: Polynomial-Time Tensor Decomposition under the Lovitz-Petrov Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13693)
*Shiri Sivan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文为三阶张量分解在Lovitz–Petrov可辨识性条件下给出首个多项式时间算法，并与该条件的多项式时间验证结合，实现端到端的高效唯一性认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kruskal条件是经典且著名的确定性可辨识性判据，但其本身验证为NP-hard，且在其条件下没有已知的多项式时间分解算法。Lovitz–Petrov条件更一般且可多项式时间验证，但此前缺乏相应的多项式时间分解算法，因此需要填补这一算法空白。

Method: 提出一种在Lovitz–Petrov条件下求解三阶（3-way）张量分解的多项式时间算法，并将该算法与Lovitz–Petrov条件的多项式时间验证相结合，构成端到端的认证流程。

Result: 给出了Lovitz–Petrov条件下三阶张量分解的首个多项式时间算法；与条件的多项式时间验证结合后，可在计算出分解后，确定性地在多项式时间内证明该分解唯一，从而也是最小秩分解。

Conclusion: 该工作弥补了Lovitz–Petrov可辨识性条件在算法层面的缺失，实现了高效端到端唯一性认证。相比任意张量分解只能给出张量秩的上界（而确定张量秩一般为NP-hard），本方法能够确定性地认证唯一性与最小秩。

Abstract: Identifiability criteria certify that a given tensor decomposition is a unique rank decomposition. Kruskal's classical condition is one of the best-known deterministic criteria for identifiability. However, no polynomial-time decomposition algorithm is known under the Kruskal condition, and verifying the condition itself is NP-hard. Lovitz and Petrov introduced a strictly more general identifiability condition which, in contrast, is polynomial-time verifiable, but no polynomial-time decomposition algorithm was previously known under this condition.
  We give a polynomial-time algorithm for 3-way tensor decomposition under the Lovitz--Petrov condition. Moreover, combining our algorithm with polynomial-time verification of the Lovitz--Petrov condition yields an efficient end-to-end certification procedure: after computing a decomposition, one can deterministically certify in polynomial time that it is unique and therefore of minimum rank. This contrasts with an arbitrary tensor decomposition, which certifies only an upper bound on the tensor rank, while determining tensor rank is NP-hard in general.

</details>


### [44] [Tight Hardness for Temporal Path Covers at Vertex-Cover Number Two](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13877)
*Alp Par*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间路径覆盖（TPC）与时间不相交路径覆盖（TDPC）在底层无向图顶点覆盖数为2时的计算复杂性，并对时间有向星形图上的TDPC边界情形给出算法性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cioni 等人已证明 TPC 与 TDPC 在底层无向图顶点覆盖数为3的时间DAG上NP难，但顶点覆盖数为2的情形仍悬而未决。本文旨在填补这一空白，并进一步刻画 TDPC 在时间有向星形图这一边界结构上的可解性。

Method: 通过从 Distinct Numerical Matching with Target Sums 出发的多项式时间归约，证明 TPC 与 TDPC 在底层无向图顶点覆盖数恰为2时NP完全。针对时间有向星形图上的 TDPC，利用重用归一化（reuse-normalization）论证将最优解约化为叶子唯一的两边合并；并引入跨度（span）参数刻画可行合并实现。

Result: TPC 与 TDPC 在底层无向图顶点覆盖数为2时均为NP完全；对 TPC 得到精确阈值：顶点覆盖数不超过1时多项式可解，为2时NP完全。对 TDPC，若入边或出边一侧为单标签，即使对侧为多标签，最优解仍多项式时间可计算；并证明任意k个可行合并的族强制实现跨度至少为2k-2，从而 TDPC 在时间有向星形图上以最大跨度为参数属于 XP，且对每个固定跨度界多项式可解。无限制多标签星形图情形仍未解决。

Conclusion: 本文解决了顶点覆盖数为2的开放问题，给出了 TPC 的精确顶点覆盖阈值，并部分刻画了 TDPC 在时间有向星形图上的可解性边界（单标签侧可解、按跨度参数化属XP），无限制多标签星形图情形仍开放。

Abstract: Temporal Path Cover (TPC) and Temporally Disjoint Path Cover (TDPC) ask for minimum-cardinality covers of a temporal digraph by temporal paths, with TDPC additionally requiring pairwise temporal disjointness. Cioni et al. proved both problems NP-hard on temporal DAGs whose underlying undirected graph has vertex-cover number three and left the vertex-cover-two case open. We resolve this question affirmatively for both problems. Using polynomial-time reductions from Distinct Numerical Matching with Target Sums, we show that TPC and TDPC are NP-complete already when the underlying undirected graph has vertex-cover number exactly two. For TPC, this yields an exact vertex-cover threshold: polynomial-time solvability at vertex-cover number at most one and NP-completeness at two. For TDPC, we further study the boundary case of temporal oriented stars. A reuse-normalization argument reduces optimum solutions to leaf-unique two-edge merges. If either the incoming or outgoing side is single-label, the optimum remains polynomial-time computable even when the opposite side is multi-label. We also introduce a span parameter for feasible merge realizations and prove that any feasible family of k merges forces a realization of span at least 2k-2. Hence TDPC on temporal oriented stars is in XP parameterized by maximum span, and is polynomial-time solvable for every fixed span bound. The unrestricted multi-label star case remains open.

</details>

*Songhua He*

TL;DR: 本文重新审视 Yao 全表模型下的隐式成员问题，首次在 45 年后定量改进了 Yao 的 Ramsey 界：将两次探测的界从塔型降至多项式 $G_2(n)=O(n^2(\log n)^2)$，并对每个固定 $q\ge3$ 证明 $G_q(n)$ 至多为高度 $q-1$、顶部为 $n^{1+o(1)}$ 的塔，特别地 $G_3(n)\le\exp(n^{1+o(1)})$。此外证明隐式成员与隐式搜索在多项式损失下等价：$H_q(n)\le G_q(n)\le n^q(H_q(n)+1)^{q+1}$。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yao 于 1981 年精确确定了一次探测情形 $G_1(n)=2n-2$，但 $q\ge2$ 的行为长期未解；Fiat 和 Naor 仅构造出宇宙大小为 $\exp(n^c)$ 的方案。本文旨在显著改进 $q\ge2$ 的界，并揭示成员与搜索问题之间的定量联系。

Abstract: We revisit the implicit membership problem in Yao's full-table model [Yao, 1981] and obtain, to our knowledge, the first quantitative improvements to his 45-year-old Ramsey bounds, most notably reducing the two-probe bound from tower-type to polynomial. In this model, an $n$-set $S\subseteq\{1,\ldots,m\}$ is stored as a permutation in an $n$-cell table, and queries decide whether $x\in S$. Let $G_q(n)$ be the largest universe size admitting a $q$-probe membership scheme for all $n$-sets. Yao determined the one-probe case exactly, proving $G_1(n)=2n-2$ for $n>2$, but the behavior for $q\ge2$ remained wide open. Fiat and Naor [1993] constructed schemes for universes of size $\exp(n^c)$ for some constant $c>0$ and sufficiently large constant $q$. For the first adaptive case, $q=2$, we prove $G_2(n)=O(n^2(\log n)^2)$. For every fixed integer $q\ge3$, we show that $G_q(n)$ is at most a tower of height $q-1$ with top $n^{1+o(1)}$; in particular, $G_3(n)\le\exp(n^{1+o(1)})$. The two-probe proof avoids Ramsey theory altogether; for larger fixed $q$, we use Ramsey theory only to make the first $q-1$ probes follow a fixed pattern, and then handle the last probe by the same non-Ramsey argument. Somewhat surprisingly, for each fixed $q$, we also show that implicit membership is as hard as implicit search up to a polynomial loss in universe size. Implicit search must return the cell containing $x$ when present and reject otherwise. For the analogous search threshold $H_q(n)$, we prove $H_q(n)\le G_q(n)\le n^q(H_q(n)+1)^{q+1}$ for every $q,n$. Thus, for every fixed $q$, one threshold is at most $\exp(n^{O(1)})$ if and only if the other is. The proofs were first generated by ChatGPT 5.5 Pro without mathematical hints; the membership-search equivalence emerged while pursuing an improved four-probe bound. The authors have validated and edited the proofs and assume responsibility for all content.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文将"匹配-匹配谜题"(Matching-Match)形式化为图的着色问题：给定固定图及其边应实现的规定彩色点对多重集，研究其计算复杂度与颜色数、宿主图结构的关系。主要结果：两色、最大度三且无预着色时NP完全；蜘蛛图上按颜色数参数化为W[1]-难；腿长≤2的蜘蛛图给出FPT算法；腿长恰为3且允许预着色时NP完全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络模体与彩色局部交互模式是描述复杂网络结构的有用工具。受逆实现视角启发，作者研究如何为固定图的顶点着色，使其边实现规定的彩色点对交互多重集，以刻画给定交互模式能否由某网络结构实现。该问题由Iburi与Uehara提出的Matching-Match谜题形式化，本文旨在厘清其计算复杂度与颜色数目及宿主图结构之间的依赖关系。

Method: 采用计算复杂性分析与参数化复杂性理论的方法。首先通过归约证明两色、最大度三、无预着色情形下的NP完全性；随后聚焦蜘蛛图这一结构化宿主图，利用参数化归约证明按颜色数参数化的W[1]-难性；针对腿长受限的蜘蛛图设计固定参数可解(FPT)算法，并分析预着色对复杂度的影响。

Result: (1) 仅用两种颜色，即使无顶点预着色且图最大度为三，Matching-Match仍为NP完全。(2) 在蜘蛛图上，即使仅躯干预着色，按颜色数参数化问题为W[1]-难。(3) 对于腿长至多为二的蜘蛛图，给出按颜色数参数化的FPT算法，且允许任意预着色。(4) 对于腿长恰为三且允许预着色的蜘蛛图，证明其为NP完全。

Conclusion: Matching-Match问题的复杂度对颜色数和宿主图结构高度敏感：两色已足以导致NP完全，蜘蛛图上的参数化难度与腿长和预着色密切相关，腿长为二与三之间存在复杂度相变，呈现出从FPT到NP完全的分界。

Abstract: Network motifs and colored local interaction patterns provide a useful way to describe the structure of complex networks. Motivated by an inverse realization perspective, we study the problem of assigning colors to the vertices of a fixed graph so that its edges realize a prescribed multiset of colored pairwise interactions. This problem is formalized by the Matching-Match Puzzle, introduced by Iburi and Uehara.
  We investigate how its computational complexity depends on the number of colors and on the structure of the host graph. We first prove that Matching-Match is NP-complete with only two colors, even when no vertex is precolored and the graph has maximum degree three. We then focus on spiders. We show that the problem is W[1]-hard parameterized by the number of colors even on spiders with only the body precolored. In contrast, for spiders whose legs have length at most two, we give a fixed-parameter tractable algorithm parameterized by the number of colors, allowing arbitrary precoloring. Finally, we prove NP-completeness for spiders whose legs all have length exactly three when precoloring is allowed.

</details>


### [47] [Maximum Matching on Regular Nonbipartite Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14126)
*Varsha Dani,Thomas P. Hayes,Seth Pettie*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明：任何基于寻找最短增广路的阻塞流型最大匹配算法，在d-正则二部图和非二部图上均以O(n²)时间运行。这一结果无需新算法，经典匹配算法即自动优于Yus13和DH25。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阻塞流型最大匹配算法一般运行时间为O(m√n)，但若对输入图加以约束（如d-正则图），该上界可被改进。已有工作在d-正则二部图上实现了近线性确定性或亚线性（高概率）算法；在d-正则非二部图上，DH25给出O(n²)（高概率）算法，略快于Yus13的O(n² log n)确定性算法。作者希望刻画经典阻塞流算法在正则图上的精确时间复杂度，并给出更优的确定性界。

Method: 作者对阻塞流型（blocking flow-type）最大匹配算法进行理论分析，这类算法基于反复寻找最短增广路集合。分析对象包括二部图与非二部图上的 d-正则图，并推广到度数落在区间[d, Δ]内的“近似正则”图。

Result: 证明任何基于最短增广路的阻塞流型最大匹配算法在d-正则图上（二部与非二部）均运行于O(n²)时间。对非二部图而言，这是相对于Yus13的O(n² log n)的渐近改进；当d = ω(√n)时也优于O(m√n)。同时将DH25的O(n²)高概率界提升为确定性界。

Conclusion: 主要结论是不需要设计新算法：经典匹配算法在d-正则及近似正则图上自动优于Yus13和DH25。结果还扩展至度数在[d, Δ]范围内的近似正则图。

Abstract: Blocking flow-type maximum matching algorithms are based on finding maximal sets of shortest augmenting paths. They run in $O(m\sqrt{n})$ time, on both bipartite [HK73, Din70, Kar73a, Kar73a] and nonbipartite graphs [GT91, Gab17, Vaz24], but this time bound can be improved if the input is constrained. In this paper we consider $d$-regular bipartite and nonbipartite graphs. Previous algorithms show that a perfect matching in $d$-regular bipartite graphs can be computed in near-linear time deterministically [COS01] or sublinear time with high probability [GKK13]. On $d$-regular non-bipartite graphs, a $(1-1/(d+1))$-approximation can be computed in sublinear time $O(n \log n)$ with high probability [DH25], and hence a maximum matching can be computed in $O(n^2)$ time, w.h.p., which is slightly faster than the best deterministic algorithm for regular graphs [Yus13], running in O(n^2 log n) time. We prove that any blocking flow-type maximum matching algorithm based on finding shortest augmenting paths runs in $O(n^2)$ time on d-regular graphs, both bipartite and nonbipartite. On nonbipartite graphs this is an asymptotic improvement over $O(n^2 \log n)$ [Yus13] and an improvement over $O(m\sqrt{n})$ [GT91, Gab17, Vaz24] when $d = ω(\sqrt{n})$. It also improves [DH25] by making its $O(n^2)$ bound deterministic. However, the main take-away message is that no new algorithms are needed: the "classic" matching algorithms automatically outperform [Yus13, DH25]. We also consider extensions of our results to graphs that are only "nearly regular," meaning that their degrees all lie within a specified range, $[d, Δ]$.

</details>


### [48] [A $(p+q)^{O(pq)}$-approximation for $(p, q)$-Flexible Graph Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14243)
*Karthekeyan Chandrasekaran,Raymond Jiang,Krishna Kalathur*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究 (p,q)-Flexible Graph Connectivity 问题，给出 (p+q)^{O(pq)} 近似算法，对任意固定常数 p,q 实现常数近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: (p,q)-Flexible Graph Connectivity 是网络设计中的鲁棒连通模型，本文旨在为其寻找最小成本边集。

Method: 将问题归约为从 (p,q-1)-Flex-Connected 到 (p,q)-Flex-Connected 的增广问题；利用结构结果，将所有缺陷割表示为一组大小为 (p+q)^{pq} 的有向图中的最小根割。

Result: 得到 (p+q)^{O(pq)} 近似算法，对固定常数 p,q 为常数近似；增广问题也达到同阶近似。

Conclusion: 通过增广算法与结构刻画，成功为 (p,q)-Flexible Graph Connectivity 提供统一近似框架。

Abstract: In the $(p,q)$-Flexible Graph Connectivity problem, the input consists of non-negative integers $p$ and $q$ and a graph $G=(V, E)$ whose edges are classified into safe and unsafe edges with non-negative edge costs. A subgraph H of G is $(p,q)$-Flex-Connected if every non-empty proper subset of vertices has either at least $p$ safe edges or at least $p+q$ total edges crossing it. The goal is to find a minimum cost subset $F\subseteq E$ of edges such that the subgraph $(V, F)$ is $(p,q)$-Flex-Connected. We give a $(p+q)^{O(pq)}$-approximation for this problem, which in particular implies a constant approximation for every fixed constants $p$ and $q$. We achieve this by designing a $(p+q)^{O(pq)}$-approximation for the augmentation problem of finding a minimum cost subset of edges to add to make a (p,q-1)-Flex-Connected graph into a (p,q)-Flex-Connected graph. Underlying the augmentation algorithm is a structural result showing that all deficient cuts can be represented by min rooted-cuts in a $(p+q)^{pq}$-sized collection of digraphs. This structural result was discovered by ChatGPT Astra.

</details>


### [49] [Private Graph Property Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14394)
*Hendrik Fichtenberger,Abigail Gentle,Tamalika Mukherjee,Sayantan Sen*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究差分隐私图性质测试，在稠密图与有界度图模型中设计了高效且具形式化隐私保证的测试算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图性质测试通常只检查随机抽样的少量子结构，理论上与差分隐私及子采样隐私放大天然契合，但两个领域此前联系甚少。作者希望弥补这一空白，建立隐私图性质测试的系统性研究。

Method: 针对诱导子图采样、随机游走与 k-disc 采样等常用图采样过程，作者发展出新的隐私放大定理，并利用这些技术构造隐私测试算法：稠密图模型下的隐私典范测试器、稠密与有界度模型下的隐私二部性测试器及子图无包含测试器；同时基于 k-disc 采样的隐私放大定理证明超有限图的所有性质均可隐私测试。

Result: 成功设计了多种隐私图性质测试器，并证明超有限图性质类可隐私测试；所得隐私测试器的查询复杂度与非隐私对应算法相当。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了差分隐私与图性质测试之间的理论桥梁，表明在稠密图与有界度图模型下隐私测试可在不显著增加查询复杂度的前提下实现。

Abstract: Graph property testing asks whether a massive graph satisfies a given property, or is far from doing so, using only a sublinear number of queries to the graph. Since property testers typically inspect only a small, randomly sampled portion of the input, they appear naturally compatible with differential privacy and privacy amplification by subsampling. Despite this, few results link these two fields. We initiate a systematic study of differentially private graph property testing with the goal of designing efficient testers with formal privacy guarantees in the dense and bounded-degree graph models. We develop new privacy amplification theorems for several widely used graph-sampling procedures such as induced subgraph sampling, random walks and k-disc sampling. We then leverage these privacy amplification techniques to design a private canonical tester in the dense graph model, as well as private bipartiteness testers and subgraph freeness testers in the dense and bounded-degree graph models. Finally, using the new privacy amplification theorem for k-disc sampling, we prove that every property of hyperfinite graphs is privately testable. The resulting query complexities of our private testers are comparable to those of their non-private counterparts.

</details>


### [50] [Scalable Triangle Counting: The Threshold Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15848)
*Asaf Etgar,Anna Gilbert,Quanquan C. Liu,Andrew McGregor*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 提出一种极简的一遍流式三角形计数算法：读取随机顺序边流至前缀中出现 Q 个三角形后停止，输出 Q(m/S)^3；在 η≤T^{2/3} 条件下以 O(ε^{-2}log(1/δ)·m/T^{1/3}) 内存实现 (1±ε) 近似，无需预设 T 或内存预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于采样率的流式三角形计数方法（如 McGregor & Vorotnikova PODS 2020、Tsourakakis et al. KDD 2009）需要预先知道三角形总数 T 或指定内存预算，这在真实场景中往往不可得或难以先验估计。本文旨在设计一种无需这些先验信息、且可提前停止的一遍流式算法。

Method: 算法：从随机顺序边流中持续读取，直到前缀内观察到 Q 个三角形，停止长度为 S，输出估计值 Q·(m/S)^3，其中 m 为总边数。证明基于 Schudy–Sviridenko 关于独立边采样估计量的集中不等式，并将其与算法产生的无放回前缀相耦合。条件为任意边所关联的最大三角形数满足 η≤T^{2/3}。停止规则自行选择前缀长度，无需预设 T，也无需规定内存预算，可在读完整条流之前返回估计。

Result: 在六条真实时间流上，停止前缀遵循理论预测的三次根缩放规律，在 10% 前缀处误差不超过 6%，且不使用 T。在固定存储边预算下，方差缩减蓄水池采样器通常更精确，但必须读完整条流。在一个约 1.8×10^9 条边的更大图上，阈值算法仅读取 0.46% 的流即返回 3.8% 误差，而最强的蓄水池基线在墙钟时间上限内未能完成一遍扫描。

Conclusion: 该阈值停止算法简单高效，无需 T 的先验估计和内存预算，可提前终止并给出高精度估计，在大规模图流上显著优于需完整扫描的蓄水池采样基线；代价是对 η 有一定条件要求，且在相同内存预算下精度可能弱于方差缩减采样器。

Abstract: We study one-pass triangle counting on random-order edge streams. We present a remarkably simple algorithm---read edges from the stream until $Q$ triangles are observed in the prefix, then output $Q\,(m/S)^3$ where $S$ is the stopping length---and prove that, when the maximum number of triangles incident to any edge satisfies $η\le T^{2/3}$, this is a $(1\pm\varepsilon)$-approximation of $T$ with probability $1-δ$ using $O(\varepsilon^{-2}\log(1/δ)\, m/T^{1/3})$ memory. Crucially, the algorithm does not need any a priori estimate of $T$, in sharp contrast with state-of-the-art sampling-rate based algorithms (McGregor and Vorotnikova, PODS 2020; Tsourakakis et al., KDD 2009). It also does not need a prescribed memory budget: the stopping rule self-selects the prefix length and can return an estimate before reading the entire stream.
  The proof rests on a Schudy--Sviridenko concentration argument for an independent-edge-sampling estimator, coupled to the without-replacement prefix produced by the algorithm. On six real temporal streams, the algorithm's stopping prefix follows the predicted cube-root scaling and achieves at most $6\%$ error at a $10\%$ prefix, without using $T$. At a fixed stored-edge budget, variance-reduced reservoir samplers are often more accurate, but only after reading the entire stream. On a separate, much larger, $1.8\times10^9$-edge graph, the threshold algorithm reads $0.46\%$ of the stream and returns $3.8\%$ error, while the strongest reservoir baselines do not finish a pass within the wall-clock cap.

</details>


### [51] [Toward Optimal Time-Space Tradeoffs for Set Reconciliation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14442)
*Rui Xu,Kangyang Zhou,Jiachen Xu,Jiarui Guo,Boyu Xian,Kaicheng Yang,Tong Yang,Yong Cui*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出 XYZ-Sketch，在集合协调（set reconciliation）问题中同时实现近最小通信空间与 O(1) 插入时间更新，并给出固定支撑规范模型下的渐近最优性论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集合协调中时间（计算开销）与空间（通信开销）是两个核心指标，此前工作多牺牲一方优化另一方，如何在两者间同时逼近最优是该问题的关键挑战。

Method: 提出 XYZ-Sketch 数据结构，用于两方集合差异识别；并建立一个宽泛的固定支撑规范模型，在该模型下分析其渐近最优性（依赖于一个开放极值猜想）。

Result: 对足够大的差异规模 d，XYZ-Sketch 仅需 (1+ε)d 元素通信，插入时间为 O(1)，解码时间为 O(d log V)，其中 V 为全集大小；实验验证了其近最优性能。

Conclusion: XYZ-Sketch 可同时实现近最小空间与 O(1) 更新时间，且在固定支撑规范模型下（在开放极值猜想成立时）渐近最优；源代码已公开。

Abstract: Set reconciliation, where two parties each holding a large set of elements aim to identify their set difference, is a fundamental task in many areas. There are two important metrics in this problem: time (computation cost) and space (communication cost). Most previous work focuses on optimizing one metric at the expense of the other. We present XYZ-Sketch, proving that it is possible to achieve near-minimal space and $O(1)$ time updates simultaneously. Specifically, for sufficiently large $d$, XYZ-Sketch reconciles sets with only $(1+\varepsilon)d$ elements for communication, while achieving $O(1)$ insertion time and $O(d\log V)$ decoding time. Here, $d$ and $V$ denote the size of the difference between two sets and the universe size, respectively. We further establish a broad fixed-support canonical model for the problem, showing that, under an open extremality conjecture, XYZ-Sketch is asymptotically optimal within this model. Experiments validate the predicted near-optimal performance of XYZ-Sketch. The source code is available at https://github.com/djwj233/XYZ-Sketch.

</details>


### [52] [Degree-Parameterized Analysis of Sampling-Based Online Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14486)
*Pan Xu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究随机到达顺序下的边加权在线二分匹配问题，以最大离线度 \(d\) 和采样比例 \(\theta\) 为参数，分析了两类基于采样的框架：确定性贪心采样与黑盒采样-匹配，并给出了紧致竞争比、方差界及迁移定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线二分匹配在广告分配、资源调度等场景中具有重要应用，随机到达顺序下的竞争比分析是核心问题。经典 \(1/8\) 与 \(1/e\) 界虽具理论价值，但在有限最大离线度 \(d\) 与采样比例 \(\theta\) 的参数化下缺乏紧致刻画，且采样对匹配数量波动（方差）的影响尚不清晰。本文旨在刻画采样策略在参数 \((d,\theta)\) 空间中的最优行为，并为近似离线求解器提供可迁移的保证。

Method: 1) 确定性贪心采样（Deterministic Greedy Sampling）：从固定规模初始样本计算价格，再应用局部阈值规则；对每个固定 \(d\ge2\) 和 \(\theta\in[0,1]\) 推导显式最坏情况竞争比，并证明其在该策略族内紧致，同时保持线性单到达时间。2) 黑盒采样-匹配（Black-Box Sampling–Matching）：引入前缀相关重加权，后接任意近似离线匹配求解器，证明迁移定理，保证为离线近似比乘以 \(d\) 与 \(\theta\) 的显式函数。3) 方差分析：推导匹配离线代理数量的最坏情况方差界。

Result: 确定性贪心采样的最优采样选择在 \(d=1,2\) 时不采样与采样之间插值，在稠密极限下保证约 \(0.2562\)，优于经典 \(1/8\) 分析，且维持线性单到达时间。方差方面，得到包含 \(\theta\to1_-\) 时阶紧行为以及固定 \(m,d\) 下 \(\theta\to0_+\) 时 \(O(\theta)\) 界的结果。黑盒采样-匹配框架在精确匹配时，于无界度极限恢复经典 \(1/e\) 保证。

Conclusion: 本文在参数化视角下统一并强化了随机到达顺序边加权在线二分匹配的采样框架分析：给出了策略族内紧致的竞争比与最优采样选择，刻画了匹配数量的方差行为，并通过迁移定理将离线近似求解器保证传递到在线情形。结果在稠密极限改进经典 \(1/8\)，在无界度极限恢复 \(1/e\)，兼顾理论紧致性与线性时间效率。

Abstract: We study edge-weighted online bipartite matching under random arrival order, parameterized by the maximum offline degree $d$ and sampling fraction $θ$. We analyze two sampling-based frameworks. For \emph{Deterministic Greedy Sampling}, which computes prices from a fixed-size initial sample and then applies a local threshold rule, we derive an explicit worst-case competitive ratio and prove it tight within this policy family for every fixed $d\ge2$ and $θ\in[0,1]$. The optimal sampling choice interpolates between no sampling for $d=1,2$ and a dense-limit guarantee of approximately $0.2562$, improving on the classical $1/8$ analysis while retaining linear per-arrival time. We also derive worst-case bounds on the variance of the number of matched offline agents, including order-tight behavior as $θ\to1_-$ and an $O(θ)$ bound as $θ\to0_+$ for fixed $m,d$. For \emph{Black-Box Sampling--Matching}, we introduce prefix-dependent reweighting followed by an arbitrary approximate offline matching solver and prove a transfer theorem whose guarantee is the offline approximation ratio times an explicit function of $d$ and $θ$. With exact matching, the framework recovers the classical $1/e$ guarantee in the unbounded-degree limit.

</details>


### [53] [The Matroid Secretary Conjecture is True](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14555)
*Sahil Singla*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了拟阵秘书猜想，给出了一个在线算法，能以至少 1/4 的概率接受离线最优解中的每个元素，且仅需预先知道元素总数与已到达子集的独立性判定 oracle，无需预先获知拟阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟阵秘书问题是秘书问题在拟阵约束下的推广，长期以来"能否达到常数竞争比"是核心悬而未决的猜想。此前研究多依赖对拟阵结构或随机到达顺序的额外假设，且算法通常需预先了解拟阵信息。本文旨在彻底解决该猜想，并在最弱的在线信息条件下实现常数竞争比。

Method: 设计在线算法：元素按随机顺序逐个到达，算法对每个到达元素即时决定是否接受，保持所选集合为独立集。算法仅使用两类信息——元素总数 n，以及对"已到达元素子集"的独立性 oracle（matroid independence oracle）；不要求预先知道拟阵本身。分析上通过概率方法论证离线最优解的每个元素被接受的概率下界。

Result: 证明拟阵秘书猜想成立：所提算法对离线最优解中的每个元素具有至少 1/4 的接受概率，从而保证离线最优值的常数期望近似比（约 1/4）。该结果具有通用性，适用于任意拟阵。

Conclusion: 拟阵秘书猜想被解决，表明在随机到达与独立性 oracle 的最弱假设下，即可对任意拟阵获得常数竞争比的在线算法，为该领域的核心问题画上句号。

Abstract: We resolve the matroid secretary conjecture, giving an online algorithm that accepts each element of the offline optimum with probability at least $1/4$. The algorithm only needs the number of elements in advance and independence-oracle access to subsets of already-arrived elements; it does not need to know the matroid upfront.

</details>


### [54] [Online matching games in bipartite expanders: applications to bitprobes and dictionaries with non-adaptive probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14559)
*Bruno Bauwens,Marius Zimand*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文给出了非自适应探测的静态与动态字典（各用 (1+o(1))K 个单元），首次给出三种操作均非自适应的字典（O(nK) 单元），并提出大小优于以往静态方案的动态 1-bitprobes 方案，其证明基于一种在线匹配博弈策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统字典的查询通常需要自适应探测，即后续探测位置依赖先前读取的内容；而实际中非自适应（可并行）探测能带来并行效率。作者希望在保持接近最优空间（(1+o(1))K 单元）的前提下实现非自适应的静态与动态字典，并进一步构造所有操作都非自适应的字典，同时将 1-bitprobes 存储方案推广到动态场景。

Method: 采用单元比特大小为 n+m+1 的模型（n、m 分别为键与值的比特长度）。构造两类字典：静态字典使用 (1+o(1))K 个单元、查询 ˜O(n²) 次探测；动态字典使用 (1+o(1))K 个单元、查询 ˜O(n² log K) 次探测，二者查询均为非自适应探测。另构造所有三种操作均非自适应、但空间为 O(nK) 单元的字典。构造均为显式的，操作运行时间为 poly(n,m)。对 1-bitprobes 存储方案引入动态版本，支持成员查询，查询运行时间 n^{O(1)}，插入与删除运行时间 n^{O(log n)}。核心技术工具是一种在给定二部图上的在线匹配博弈策略：对手开关左侧节点，策略需在节点被开启时给出不可撤销的匹配；在假设每个节点在多项式轮数后被关闭时给出高效策略，并针对（无损）扩展器给出高效策略。

Result: 得到使用 (1+o(1))K 单元、查询非自适应探测的静态字典（˜O(n²) 探测）与动态字典（˜O(n² log K) 探测）；首次实现三种操作均非自适应的字典（O(nK) 单元）；所有构造显式且操作时间为 poly(n,m)。给出一个动态 1-bitprobes 方案，其大小小于此前所有静态构造，查询运行 n^{O(1)}，插入/删除运行 n^{O(log n)}。

Conclusion: 非自适应探测字典可在接近最优空间下实现，动态与非自适应两者可兼顾（代价是更大空间）；1-bitprobes 可从静态推广到动态并缩小空间。其关键技术贡献是二部图在线匹配博弈的高效策略，对（无损）扩展器有效，这些策略也可为后续工作提供工具。

Abstract: A dictionary is a data structure which stores pairs (key, value). The operation query on input key returns value if the dictionary contains a pair (key, value) and nil otherwise. A dynamic dictionary also supports the insert and delete operations.
  The model with cells of bitsize $n + m + 1$ is used, where $n$ and $m$ are the bitsizes of keys and values.
  We give 2 dictionaries that use $(1+o(1))K$ cells to store $K$ pairs and in which query makes \emph{non-adaptive probes} to the data structure:
  a static one in which query makes $\smash{\widetilde O(n^2)}$ probes, and a dynamic one in which it makes $\smash{\widetilde O(n^2 \log K)}$ probes.
  Also, for the first time, a dictionary is given in which all 3 operations are non-adaptive, but the size is larger: $O(nK)$ cells.
  The constructions are explicit and, consequently, all operations run in time $\poly(n,m)$.
  1-bitprobes storage schemes store a set $S \subseteq \{0,1\}^n$ and answer a membership query by reading a single bit.
  We introduce 1-bitprobes that are {\em dynamic}.
  Such a scheme is given whose size is smaller than in all previous constructions which are static.
  The \textsf{query} operation has runtime $n^{O(1)}$. The operations delete and insert have runtime $n^{O(\log n)}$.
  The proofs rely on a strategy for an online matching game played on a given bipartite graph.
  An opponent switches left nodes {\em on} and {\em off}.
  The strategy needs to assign an irrevocable match to a node when it is turned {\em on}. Such games were introduced in the companion paper~\cite{companion-Hall}.
  The applications in this paper rely on efficient strategies assuming that each node is turned {\em off} after a polynomial number of rounds.
  We present efficient strategies for (lossless) expanders.

</details>


### [55] [Counting Paths and Trees via Exterior Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14682)
*Fahad Panolan,Saket Saurabh,Meirav Zehavi,Jie Xue*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文给出了计数宿主图中k-路径与k-森林的随机近似算法，路径算法达到2^k·poly(n,ε^{-1})的时间复杂度，解决了Koutis-Williams猜想及Lokshtanov-Saurabh-Zehavi开放问题，方法融合外代数与随机矩阵估计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计数k-路径与k-森林是参数化计数中的核心问题。Koutis和Williams [CACM 2016] 提出猜想，Lokshtanov、Saurabh和Zehavi [SODA 2021] 提出是否可实现2^k·poly(n,ε^{-1})时间的近似方案这一开放问题，本文旨在给出可行算法并加以解决。

Method: 本文方法将外代数（exterior algebra）与随机矩阵估计量（random matrix estimators）相结合，并利用Rakhshan和Rabusseau [AISTATS 2020] 的张量列矩界（tensor-train moment bound）。对于森林，进一步引入小分量分离器（small-component separator）以高效计算该估计量。算法引入参数：相对误差ε、失败概率δ。

Result: 1. 路径：计数k个顶点的有向路径，在2^k k^{O(1)}(n+m)ε^{-2}log(2/δ)次算术运算内完成近似。2. 树与森林：对任意固定η>0，在(2+η)^k n^{O_η(1)}ε^{-2}log(2/δ)次算术运算内近似计算给定k顶点森林的非诱导副本数。

Conclusion: 本文提出的路径算法给出了2^k·poly(n,ε^{-1})时间的近似方案，从而验证了Koutis-Williams的猜想，并解答了Lokshtanov-Saurabh-Zehavi的开放问题。方法结合外代数与随机矩阵估计量及张量列矩界，配合小分量分离器处理森林情形，为参数化近似计数提供了有效途径。

Abstract: We give randomized approximation algorithms for counting k-paths and k-forests in a host graph. Here k denotes the number of pattern vertices, n and m denote the numbers of host vertices and edges or arcs, ε is the relative error, and δ is the failure probability. Our main results are:
  1. Paths: We approximate the number of directed paths on $k$ vertices in $2^k k^{O(1)}(n+m)\varepsilon^{-2}\log(2/δ)$ arithmetic operations.
  2. Trees and forests: For every fixed $η>0$, we approximate the number of non-induced copies of a given forest on $k$ vertices in $(2+η)^k n^{O_η(1)}\varepsilon^{-2}\log(2/δ)$ arithmetic operations.
  Our path algorithm resolves a conjecture of Koutis and Williams~[CACM 2016] and answers an open question of Lokshtanov, Saurabh, and Zehavi~[SODA 2021] by giving a $2^k poly(n,\varepsilon^{-1})$-time approximation scheme. Our algorithms combine exterior algebra with random matrix estimators, using the tensor-train moment bound of Rakhshan and Rabusseau~[AISTATS 2020]. For forests, we use a small-component separator to evaluate the estimator efficiently.

</details>


### [56] [Fast and Theoretically-Efficient Batch-Parallel Link-Cut Trees, Euler Tour Trees, and Treaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14746)
*Quinten De Man,Laxman Dhulipala*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出MOJOS框架，通过统一Euler tour树与link-cut树共用的动态序列抽象，构建出理论最优且实用的批量并行树数据结构，并首次实现理论高效的批量并行link-cut树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行批量动态树结构（Euler tour树、UFO树、拓扑树、rake-compress树）在顺序设置下均显著不如link-cut树，而link-cut树已保持顺序最优性能超40年；如何设计高效的批量并行link-cut树一直是重大公开难题。

Method: 提出MOJOS统一框架，利用Euler tour树与link-cut树均依赖支持分裂与合并的公共动态序列抽象这一事实；基于treap设计新型批量动态序列，实现最优工作量与深度；在该框架下开发新的批量并行Euler tour树算法（支持任意批量动态序列作为即插即用替代），并构建首个理论高效的批量并行link-cut树。

Result: 新批量动态序列在批量更新、查询和内存使用上优于现有并行跳表与treap实现；新Euler tour树优于先前支持子树查询的批量动态树实现；link-cut树是首个支持路径查询且在binary-forking模型下批量更新达到O(log n)深度的批量动态数据结构，性能优于所有已知支持路径查询的并行批量动态树结构。

Conclusion: MOJOS弥合了并行批量动态树与顺序link-cut树之间的性能差距，提供了理论最优且实践高效的统一框架，并首次实现理论高效的批量并行link-cut树。

Abstract: Parallel batch-dynamic trees are a fundamental building block in recent theoretical and practical advances in dynamic graph algorithms. However, all existing parallel batch-dynamic tree data structures, including Euler tour trees, UFO trees, topology trees, and rake-compress trees, are all significantly outperformed in the sequential setting by link-cut trees, which have been the sequential state-of-the-art for over 40 years. Despite their excellent performance in the sequential setting, designing efficient batch-parallel link-cut trees has remained a major open problem.
  In this paper, we close this gap by introducing MOJOS, a unified framework for theoretically- and practically-efficient parallel batch-dynamic trees. We exploit the fact that both Euler tour trees and link-cut trees rely on a common dynamic sequence abstraction that supports splitting and joining. We introduce a new batch-dynamic sequence built using treaps that achieves optimal work and depth, and outperforms existing parallel skip list and treap implementations for batch updates, queries, and memory usage.
  With MOJOS, we develop a new batch-parallel Euler tour tree algorithm that outperforms prior batch-dynamic tree implementations supporting subtree queries. Unlike prior batch-parallel Euler tour trees which rely on skip list's ability to represent cyclic sequences, MOJOS allows any batch-dynamic sequence data structure to be used as a drop-in replacement. Finally, we develop the first theoretically-efficient batch-parallel link-cut tree, which is also the first batch-dynamic data structure supporting path queries to achieve $O(\log n)$ depth for batch updates in the binary-forking model. Our link-cut tree implementation outperforms all known parallel batch-dynamic tree data structures supporting path queries.

</details>


### [57] [A rooted tree framework for linear time ultrabubble detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14852)
*Athanasios E. Zisis,Pål Sætrom*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文在前期LCA枚举ultrabubble工作的基础上，提出混合同步评估、BFS树线性枚举以及共享frontier节点互斥性三项改进，组合成六种方法并给出基准测试，将全部ultrabubble识别的时间界提升至O(n+m+K)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全基因组图（pangenome graph）中ultrabubble是重要的变异结构，因其具有有限路径且无死端。前期工作已证明二分biedged图可用LCA方法在O(Kn)时间内枚举ultrabubble，但该复杂度在节点数与snarl数较大时仍有提升空间，需要更实用的方法与更优的理论界。

Method: 1）混合方法：根据snarl规模相对于图中tips和cycle-closing节点数量的比例，在LCA方法与朴素方法之间进行选择；2）借助前期理论框架，通过遍历biedged二分图的BFS树，在O(n+m+K)时间内找出所有ultrabubble；3）证明共享frontier节点的两个候选ultrabubble snarl不可能同为ultrabubble，由此得到的snarl集合是相容的、规模上界为n，并定义互斥的嵌套snarl族。三项结果组合为六种方法。

Result: 提出的六种方法在基准测试中展示了上述改进对识别ultrabubble实际运行时间的影响；理论上将全ultrabubble枚举的时间复杂度由O(Kn)降至O(n+m+K)，并给出了snarl相容性与嵌套族的界n。

Conclusion: 通过混合评估、BFS树遍历与共享frontier节点互斥性三方面改进，显著提升了ultrabubble枚举的理论效率与实用性，为pangenome图的变异结构分析提供了更优算法基础。

Abstract: Pangenomics uses graphs to show genetic differences within or between species. In these graphs, a path can represent one genome, while regions with different paths show genetic variation. Biedged graphs use black edges for sequences and grey edges for links between them. Snarls are minimal subgraphs of a biedged graph that are separated from the rest of the graph by removing two black edges. Ultrabubbles are minimal acyclic and tip-free snarls and thus are important variant structures because they have finite paths and lack dead ends.
  In our previous work, we showed that in linear time every bidirected graph can be transformed to a rooted biedged bipartite one, and that in these graphs, ultrabubbles can be enumerated with a lowest common ancestor (LCA)-based method in $O(Kn)$ time, where $n$ and $K$ are the number of nodes and given snarls, respectively, of the graph.
  Here, we present a series of practical and theoretical improvements to our previous LCA-based approach. First, we present a hybrid method that selects between the LCA-based method and the naive approach for evaluating a snarl, depending on the size of the snarl in relation to the number of tips and cycle-closing nodes in the graph. Second, by using the theoretical framework from our previous paper, we show that all ultrabubbles can be found in $O(n + m + K)$ time, where $m$ is the number of edges, by traversing the breadth-first search (BFS) tree of the biedged bipartite graph. Third, we show that any two snarls that are candidate ultrabubbles and share a frontier node cannot be ultrabubbles; the resulting set of snarls is compatible, bound by $n$, and defines exclusive families of nested snarls.
  We combine these three results into six methods and present benchmarking results that illustrate how the above improvements affect practical run-times for identifying ultrabubbles.

</details>


### [58] [A Strongly Subquadratic $(3+\varepsilon)$-Approximation for Weighted Edit Distance over Arbitrary Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14873)
*Ethan Mader,Borna Tavasoli,Jihan Wang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文首次给出任意度量诱导编辑代价下加权编辑距离的常数因子近似算法，随机化运行时间为 $\widetilde{O}(n^{7/4}/\varepsilon^8)$，达到 $(3+\varepsilon)$-近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权编辑距离在任意度量代价下的近似计算此前仅有 Kuszmaul (2019) 针对等长输入的 $O(n^\delta)$-近似（时间 $\widetilde{O}(n^{2-\delta})$），缺乏强亚二次时间内的常数因子近似结果，且已有工作受限于等长输入与代价比无界等条件。

Method: 提出随机化算法，针对任意度量诱导的编辑代价，在允许输入长度不等、且对编辑代价比值不加任何限制的条件下，于 $\widetilde{O}(n^{7/4}/\varepsilon^8)$ 时间内求解加权编辑距离。

Result: 对任意 $0<\varepsilon\le 1$，算法以至少 $1-n^{-10}$ 的概率返回 $(3+\varepsilon)$-近似解，运行时间为 $\widetilde{O}(n^{7/4}/\varepsilon^8)$。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了首个在强亚二次时间内对任意度量加权编辑距离实现常数因子近似的算法，突破了此前仅能达到 $O(n^\delta)$ 近似且限于等长输入的局限。

Abstract: We study weighted edit distance between two strings of total length $n$, where edit costs are induced by an arbitrary metric. For equal-length inputs, Kuszmaul (2019) gave an $O(n^δ)$-approximation with $\widetilde{O}(n^{2-δ})$ running time for every fixed $0 < δ< 1$. We give the first constant-factor approximation for weighted edit distance over arbitrary metrics in strongly subquadratic running time. For every $0 < \varepsilon \le 1$, our randomized algorithm runs in $\widetilde{O}(n^{7/4}/\varepsilon^8)$ time and returns a $(3+\varepsilon)$-approximation with probability at least $1-n^{-10}$. The algorithm allows unequal input lengths and places no bound on the ratio between edit costs.

</details>


### [59] [Fast Stencil Computations on a Single Arbitrarily Moving Interval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14879)
*Aaron Gregory*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文研究一维、变系数三点模板、单区间自由边界（两端点每步移动任意量且在线揭示）的模板计算，提出工作量为 O((B+N) log T log(N+B))、跨度为 O(T log T log(N+B)) 的调度，并证明结果精确，其中 B 为时间视界加边界轨迹的总变差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模板计算模拟 T 步 N 个单元代价为 Θ(NT)，而基于 FFT 组合多步线性算子的方法依赖预知哪些单元仍服从同一算子；在自由边界问题中该区域由解决定并随演化移动，现有最优边界只能每步移动至多一个单元，限制较强。

Method: 针对一维三点变系数模板与单区间计算区域，允许两端点每步任意移动并在线揭示，定义 B 为时间视界加边界轨迹总变差，设计并行调度并证明计算值精确；核心是任一时间窗口内两端点所触及的范围可归入两个区间，每端点一个。

Result: 调度工作量为 O((B+N) log T log(N+B))，跨度为 O(T log T log(N+B))，且计算结果精确。相比要求边界每步至多移动一个单元的已有界（其 B ≤ 3T，因此本界仍近似线性），此处放宽要求而无损失；宽度 N 的单次跳跃代价为 T+2N。下界方面：p 个区域时界退化 p 倍，当 p=√T 时存在工作量 Θ(T^{3/2}) 而 B+N=Θ(T) 的实例。

Conclusion: 总变差足以刻画代价，且该刻画不可进一步放松；除经典卷积界作为接口导入外，全部结果均在 Lean 4 中机器验证。

Abstract: A stencil computation repeatedly updates every cell of a grid from its neighbours' values at the previous timestep. Simulating T steps on N cells directly costs Theta(NT), and a line of work beginning with Ahmad et al. reduces this by composing many timesteps into one linear operator and applying it with a Fast Fourier Transform. That technique needs to know which cells will still obey the same operator when the composed step ends, and in a free-boundary problem they do not: the region governed by a given rule is determined by the solution and moves as it evolves.
  We study one spatial dimension, a three-point stencil with time-varying coefficients, and a computed region that is a single interval whose two endpoints move by arbitrary amounts at every step, revealed online. Let B be the horizon plus the total variation of the boundary trajectory. We give a schedule whose work is O((B+N) log T log(N+B)) and whose span is O(T log T log(N+B)), and we prove that the values it computes are exact.
  The best existing bound for a region that moves requires its boundary to travel at most one cell per timestep. We drop that requirement and lose nothing by it: a boundary obeying it has B <= 3T, so our bound stays near-linear on every trajectory the earlier result covers. Elsewhere, B grows only by the distance the boundary actually travels -- one jump of width N costs T + 2N.
  The reason total variation suffices is that everything the two endpoints touch over a time window of any length lies in two intervals, one per endpoint. This cannot be relaxed: with p regions the bound degrades by a factor p, and at p = sqrt(T) there is an instance on which the work is Theta(T^{3/2}) while B + N = Theta(T).
  All results are machine-checked in Lean 4, apart from the classical convolution bound, which is imported as an interface.

</details>
