*Baojian Zhou*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文提出确定性局部PageRank算法，在正则化个性化PageRank上达到加性精度ε，局部工作量约Õ(1/(ρ√α))，精度仅以多重对数形式进入；并给出基于支持安全阈值批次的随机算法，期望工作量Õ(V*·min{k*, α^{-1/2}})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部PageRank旨在获得与图规模无关的稀疏近似。现有方法常需图级预处理、已知解支撑集或SDD求解oracle，本文希望去除这些依赖，并区分确定性与随机化路线，特别是实现仅含多重对数开销的确定性局部加速。

Method: 确定性算法结合正则化连续化与受度数缩放箱约束和质量上限约束的加速修正；用同一递推的两个能量分别控制目标收敛与激活坐标的响应；通过选流论证界定累计扫描体量，并用稀疏阈值报告器实现该界；还给出有理输入的有界算术实现。随机算法使用支持安全阈值批次，借助块Cholesky与Chebyshev论证界定批次深度，并用带证书SDD求解获得期望工作量。

Result: 确定性算法在正则化个性化PageRank上以加性精度ε达到Õ(1/(ρ√α))局部工作量，精度仅多重对数进入；随机算法期望工作量为Õ(V*·min{k*, α^{-1/2}})，其中k*与V*分别为最优支撑集基数和度体积。两种方法均蕴含相应的加速度归一化PPR近似；与Cui、Wei、Yang的并发预印本相比，随机工作量尺度相当，而本文主要区别在于确定性局部加速、仅多重对数开销且无需SDD oracle。

Conclusion: 本文给出无需图级预处理或外部解支撑的确定性局部加速PageRank算法，并配套随机化支持安全批次方法；主要贡献是确定性局部加速与多重对数精度开销，且不依赖SDD oracle。

Abstract: Local PageRank algorithms seek sparse approximations with work independent of graph size. We give a deterministic algorithm for regularized personalized PageRank with additive objective accuracy $ε$ in $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/(ρ\sqrtα))$ local work, where $α$ is the lazy teleportation parameter and $ρ$ is the regularizer. Accuracy enters only polylogarithmically. The bound charges discovery, repeated neighborhood scans, numerical updates, certification, and output, without graph-wide preprocessing or a supplied solution support.
  The algorithm combines regularization continuation with accelerated corrections constrained by a degree-scaled box and a mass cap. Two energies for the same recurrence control objective convergence and the response that activates coordinates. A selected-flow argument bounds cumulative scanned volume, and a sparse threshold reporter realizes the bound. We also specify a bounded-arithmetic implementation for rational inputs.
  A second, randomized algorithm uses support-safe threshold batches. A block-Cholesky and Chebyshev argument bounds their depth, and certified SDD solves give expected work $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(V_*\min\{k_*,α^{-1/2}\})$, where $k_*$ and $V_*$ are the optimal support's cardinality and degree volume. Both methods imply the corresponding accelerated degree-normalized PPR approximation. The concurrent September 2026 preprint of Cui, Wei, and Yang also attains the randomized work scale. Our principal distinction is deterministic local acceleration with only polylogarithmic overhead and no SDD oracle.

</details>


### [61] [The Complexity of Weak Partition Connectivity in Hedgegraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14932)
*Yuanhao Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文证明hedgegraph上弱划分连通性的整数阈值决策问题是NP完全的，并给出分数目标的精确公式、若干精确算法及PTAS，同时指出在full-support split systems上不存在FPTAS（除非P=NP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答hedgegraph中弱划分连通性整数阈值决策问题计算复杂度的开放问题，并刻画其精确可解与近似可解的参数范围。

Method: 采用二元矩阵表示，将分数弱划分连通性表达为 m-ρ(A)，其中ρ(A)最大化“所选行数”与“相异投影列数减一”之比；该公式同时用于构造硬度归约和确定性算法。

Result: 整数阈值决策问题NP完全，且在最受限情形（连通无权hedgegraph，每个hedge恰由两个非空、顶点不相交且并集为整个顶点集的超边组成）仍成立；在该类实例上hedge connectivity有简单精确公式。当存在参考列的row supports满足线性交集条件（含最小row-support数s(A)≤2）时可精确计算；对所有full-support split systems，整数与分数目标均存在输出划分的多项式时间近似方案（PTAS）。

Conclusion: 弱划分连通性整数阈值判定在hedgegraph上本质困难，但分数形式具备可精确刻画的结构；在full-support split systems上可实现PTAS，但不具备FPTAS（除非P=NP）。

Abstract: We prove that the integer-threshold decision problem for weak partition connectivity in hedgegraphs is NP-complete, answering an open question about its computational complexity. Hardness holds even for connected unweighted hedgegraphs in which every hedge consists of exactly two nonempty, vertex-disjoint hyperedges whose union is the entire vertex set. On the same class of instances, hedge connectivity has a simple exact formula. Using a binary matrix representation, we express fractional weak partition connectivity as $m-ρ(A)$, where $ρ(A)$ maximizes the ratio of the number of selected rows to one less than the number of distinct projected columns. This formula yields both the hardness reduction and deterministic algorithms: exact computation when some reference column gives row supports satisfying a linear intersection condition, including the case of minimum row-support number $s(A)\le2$, and a partition-output polynomial-time approximation scheme (PTAS) for both the integer and fractional objectives on all full-support split systems. Unless $\mathrm{P}=\mathrm{NP}$, neither objective admits a fully polynomial-time approximation scheme (FPTAS) on this class.

</details>


### [62] [Matrix Spencer: Eight Standard Deviations Suffice and an Almost-Linear Time Algorithm for Dense Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15025)
*Zhao Song,Lichen Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了矩阵 Spencer 猜想：对任意满足 ‖A_i‖≤1 的对称矩阵 A_1,…,A_n，存在符号 ε_i∈{−1,1} 使 ‖∑ε_iA_i‖<8√n；并给出失败概率≤p、算术复杂度为 n^{3+o(1)}polylog(1/p) 的随机算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机符号仅能给出 O(√(n log n)) 的矩阵集中界，而矩阵 Spencer 猜想断言 O(√n)，此前仅在秩、块对角或 Frobenius 范数限制下已知。本文旨在完全证明该猜想，并将常数压到 8 以下，同时给出接近输入规模 n^3 的高效算法。

Method: 存在性证明采用部分染色论证，其核心新估计是谱体 {x:‖∑x_iA_i‖≤R} 的遗传高斯小球界，通过在矩阵加权 Poincaré 不等式下将对数配分函数从对角模型插值到非交换模型得到。为降低常数：用高斯测度到符号的无损编码消除联合界损失，用带认证系数的光滑谱障碍消除小半径损失。算法将高斯点投影到谱体的光滑版本上，其导数为一 Gibbs 矩阵的迹；通过四种依次更廉价地维护该矩阵的方法将运行时间降至 n^{3+o(1)}。

Result: 证明存在差异小于 8√n 的符号配置；给出失败概率至多 p、使用 n^{3+o(1)}polylog(1/p) 次实数算术运算的随机算法，接近稠密输入规模 n^3（至多差亚多项式因子）。

Conclusion: 矩阵 Spencer 猜想成立，常数可压至 8 以下；证明与算法分别通过无损编码、光滑谱障碍和 Gibbs 矩阵高效维护实现。证明猜想与进一步压低常数属不同问题。

Abstract: The Matrix Spencer conjecture asserts that for all symmetric matrices $A_1,\ldots,A_n\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times n}$ with $\|A_i\|\le1$ there are signs $\varepsilon_1,\ldots,\varepsilon_n\in\{-1,1\}$ with $\|\sum_{i=1}^n\varepsilon_iA_i\|=O(\sqrt n)$. Random signs give only the matrix-concentration bound $O(\sqrt{n\log n})$, and the conjecture was known only under rank, block-diagonal, or Frobenius-norm restrictions. We prove it: a signing of discrepancy below $8\sqrt n$ always exists. We also give a randomized algorithm that finds a signing of discrepancy below $12\sqrt n$ with failure probability at most $p$ using $n^{3+o(1)}\operatorname{polylog}(1/p)$ arithmetic operations in the real-arithmetic model, which matches the size $n^3$ of the dense input up to subpolynomial factors.
  The existence proof is a partial-coloring argument with one new estimate: a hereditary Gaussian small-ball bound for the spectral body $\{x\in \mathbb{R}^n:\|\sum_ix_iA_i\|\le R\}$, proved by interpolating a log-partition function from a diagonal model to the noncommutative one under a matrix-weighted Poincaré inequality. Proving the conjecture and bringing the constant below $8$ are different problems. The partial-coloring argument loses a large factor twice, when it turns Gaussian measure into signs through a union bound and when it proves the small-ball estimate at a radius far larger than necessary. We remove the first loss by a lossless coding of Gaussian measure into signs and the second by smooth spectral barriers with certified coefficients. The algorithms project Gaussian points onto a smoothed version of the spectral body, whose derivatives are traces against one Gibbs matrix. Four successively cheaper ways of maintaining that matrix bring the running time down to $n^{3+o(1)}$.

</details>


### [63] [A Deterministic $(2+\varepsilon)$-Approximation for Weighted Feedback Vertex Set in Tournaments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15220)
*Hanqing Li,Zihan Wu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究竞赛图中的带权反馈顶点集问题，对每个固定整数k≥2给出运行时间为n^{2^{O(k)}}的确定性(2+1/k)-近似算法，从而对任意ε>0得到(2+ε)-近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞赛图中带权反馈顶点集问题缺乏兼顾近似比与运行时间的确定性算法；作者希望以更优的近似保证并保持确定性与可行的参数化运行时间来解决该问题。

Method: 算法结合两个要素：当竞赛图的三角形图团数有界时，利用其传递补图的链分解对最大权传递子竞赛图进行精确动态规划；当团数很大时，借助三角形图的结构定理给出常数规模的好代价向量，并用局部比值归约获得近似保证。副产品是用该动态规划在B7-free竞赛图中以O(n^7)精确求解带权反馈顶点集。

Result: 对每个固定k≥2，得到确定性(2+1/k)-近似算法，运行时间n^{2^{O(k)}}（忽略权编码长度的多项式因子）；对每个ε>0，得到确定性(2+ε)-近似，运行时间n^{2^{O(1/ε)}}；在B7-free竞赛图中可O(n^7)精确求解。

Conclusion: 该工作通过三角形图结构性质与动态规划、局部比值归约的结合，在竞赛图带权反馈顶点集问题上实现了确定性的(2+ε)-近似及参数化运行时间，并在特定禁子式类中给出精确算法。

Abstract: We study the weighted feedback vertex set problem in tournaments. For every fixed integer $k\geq 2$, we give a deterministic $(2+1/k)$-approximation algorithm with running time $n^{2^{O(k)}}$, apart from polynomial dependence on the encoding length of the weights. Consequently, for every fixed $\varepsilon>0$, weighted feedback vertex set in tournaments has a deterministic $(2+\varepsilon)$-approximation running in time $n^{2^{O(1/\varepsilon)}}$. The algorithm combines two ingredients. When the triangle graph of the tournament has bounded clique number, a chain decomposition of its transitive complement yields an exact dynamic program for a maximum-weight transitive subtournament. When the clique number is large, a structural theorem for triangle graphs supplies a constant-size strongly good cost vector. A local-ratio reduction with this cost vector gives the claimed guarantee. As a by-product, the dynamic program solves weighted feedback vertex set exactly in $\mathcal B_7$-free tournaments in time $O(n^7)$, where $\mathcal B_7$ is the family of seven-vertex tournaments with feedback vertex set number at least three.

</details>


### [64] [A Faster Undirected Single-Source Shortest Path Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15247)
*Avi Kadria,Liam Roditty*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对非负权无向图的单源最短路径（SSSP）问题，提出了自 Duan 等人 FOCS'23 突破以来首个运行时间的改进算法，达到 $O(m\log^{1/2} n \log\log^{1/4} n \log\log\log^{1/4} n)$，较此前最优结果提升约 $(\frac{\log\log n}{\log\log\log n})^{1/4}$ 倍。核心技术是一个简洁高效的工具，用于计算每个顶点到随机采样集中最近顶点的距离，该工具可能具有独立价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSSP 是算法领域的经典问题。在比较-加法模型中，数十年来的最优界一直是 Dijkstra 算法配合 Fibonacci 堆的 $O(m+n\log n)$。虽然近年来 Duan 等人在加权无向图（FOCS'23）和有向图（STOC'25、ICALP'26）上相继取得次线性对数因子的突破，但无向图情形自 FOCS'23 后未有进一步改进。本文旨在打破这一停滞，进一步降低无向图 SSSP 的运行时间。

Method: 本文的主要技术贡献是一个简洁高效的工具：对每个顶点计算其到随机采样集中最近顶点的距离（即随机采样最近邻距离问题）。基于该工具，设计了新的 SSSP 算法，进一步削减了先前算法中的对数因子开销，从而实现运行时间的改进。

Result: 算法运行时间为 $O(m\log^{1/2} n \log\log^{1/4} n \log\log\log^{1/4} n)$。相比此前无向图最优的 $O(m\log^{1/2} n \log\log^{1/2} n)$，改进因子为 $(\frac{\log\log n}{\log\log\log n})^{1/4}$，这是自 FOCS'23 以来该问题运行时间的首次改进。

Conclusion: 本文在加权无向图 SSSP 问题上取得新进展，刷新了该问题的当前最优运行时间界。所提出的"到随机采样集最近顶点距离"计算工具简洁高效，除服务于本文算法外，可能对相关领域问题具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: The single-source shortest paths (SSSP) problem in graphs with non-negative edge weights is one of the most classic problems in algorithms. For decades, the best known running time in the comparison-addition model was the $O(m+n\log n)$ bound of Dijkstra's algorithm with Fibonacci heaps.
  Recently, Duan, Mao, Shu, and Yin (FOCS'23) gave a randomized $O(m\log^{1/2} n \log\log^{1/2} n)$-time algorithm for SSSP in weighted undirected graphs. For weighted directed graphs, Duan, Mao, Mao, Shu, and Yin (STOC'25) gave an $O(m\log^{2/3} n)$-time algorithm for SSSP. Very recently, Duan, Mao, Shu, and Yin (ICALP'26) obtained an algorithm for directed graphs whose running time matches the $O(m\log^{1/2} n \log\log^{1/2} n)$ time of the undirected case.
  In this paper, we present a faster algorithm for SSSP in weighted undirected graphs, giving the first improvement in running time since the FOCS'23 breakthrough of Duan, Mao, Shu, and Yin. Our algorithm runs in $O(m\log^{1/2} n \log\log^{1/4} n \log\log\log^{1/4} n)$ time, improving the previous running time by a factor of $(\frac{\log\log n}{\log\log\log n})^{1/4}$.
  Our main contribution is a simple and efficient tool that computes, for every vertex, its distance to the nearest vertex in a random sample; this tool may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [65] [Single-Machine Scheduling with Interval Predictions and Costly Preemption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15492)
*Lachlan Bridges*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究处理时间未知但带报告区间的单机总完工时间问题，提出“触发式可恢复几何回退”策略，并给出显式最坏情形系数与精确界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典单机总完工时间问题通常假设处理时间已知；本文关注处理时间未知、仅有报告区间的现实场景，并允许报告出错（区间被违反）时切换策略。由于每次中断未完工任务会产生附加惩罚，需要刻画在惩罚、残差规模与几何深度之间的最坏情形权衡，并为不同信息条件提供可证明的性能保证。

Method: 任务先按报告上界非降序加工；若某任务在该服务量后仍未完工，说明报告上界被违反，策略切换为可恢复几何回退，且每次中断未完工任务附加惩罚κ。针对残差集规模m、已知规模比D=s1/s0、归一化中断惩罚λ=κ/s0和几何深度K，推导显式最坏情形系数Ψ_{m,D,λ}(K)；并针对任意非抢占列表、处理时间位于已知有界区间的情形，推导精确最坏情形系数R_{n,D}。进一步分析最优深度的存在性与选择规则，以及多种信息情形（有效区间、单次区间失败、任意报告、未触发、随机实例）的界。

Result: 当λ>0时，存在有限的最优几何深度，可在触发后根据观察到的未完工任务数选择；最优深度随中断惩罚增大而减小，随残差集规模增大而增大。所得界覆盖有效区间、单区间失败、任意报告、未触发结果以及随机实例；在单失败情形下，还给出证明性回退保证小于有界区间延续保证的精确条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为报告区间可能失效的单机调度提供了可计算的最坏情形性能保证，并明确了中断惩罚与回退深度之间的权衡，以及回退策略相对有界区间延续策略更优的判定条件。

Abstract: We study the single-machine total-completion-time problem $1||\sum C_j$ when processing times are unknown but each job comes with a reported interval. Jobs are initially processed in nondecreasing order of reported upper bound. If a job is still unfinished after receiving that much service, the reported upper bound has been violated and the policy switches to a resumable geometric fallback. Each interruption of an unfinished job incurs an additive penalty $κ$.
  For a residual set of $m$ jobs, known size ratio $D=s_1/s_0$, normalized interruption penalty $λ=κ/s_0$, and geometric depth $K$, we derive an explicit worst-case coefficient $Ψ_{m,D,λ}(K)$. When $λ>0$, a finite minimizing depth exists and can be chosen after the trigger from the observed number of unfinished jobs; the optimal depth decreases with the interruption penalty and increases with the residual set size. We also derive the exact worst-case coefficient $R_{n,D}$ for an arbitrary nonpreemptive list when all processing times lie in a known bounded range. These bounds give guarantees for valid intervals, a single interval failure, arbitrary reports, no-trigger outcomes, and random instances. In the single-failure regime, they also give a precise condition under which the proved fallback guarantee is smaller than the bounded-range continuation guarantee.

</details>


### [66] [Strong and Compact Policies for Submodular Markov Decision Processes via LP-Based Submodular Orienteering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15539)
*Lars Rohwedder,Rico Zenklusen*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究“次模马尔可夫决策过程”（Submodular MDP）及其无随机特例“次模定向问题”（Submodular Orienteering），提出基于LP的Sherali-Adams层级与Round-or-Cut新算法，在多项式时间内实现$O(n^{\varepsilon})$（对Submodular MDP为$O(H^{\varepsilon})$）近似，并揭示近似比与依赖历史顶点数之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MDP的目标函数为可加性奖励，但诸多实际应用（如收益递减、覆盖增益等）更适合用单调次模奖励刻画。Submodular MDP可建模这类问题，而其无随机版本等价于Submodular Orienteering。此前Submodular MDP最好的近似比线性依赖于时间视界H，且Submodular Orienteering在多项式时间内的近似保证尚属空白，因此需要新的算法框架来改进近似比并统一处理两种模型。

Method: 提出一种新颖的LP-based算法，核心思想来自Sherali-Adams层级和Round-or-Cut框架，用以求解Submodular Orienteering；将该方法推广到Submodular MDP场景。通过LP松弛与层级加强约束逐步收紧可行域，并在舍入/切割之间迭代。

Result: 算法的近似保证与已知的Submodular Orienteering准多项式时间对数近似相当，并可扩展到Submodular MDP。在多项式时间范畴内，对任意$\varepsilon>0$达到$O(n^{\varepsilon})$近似（Submodular MDP为$O(H^{\varepsilon})$），n为顶点数；这在Submodular Orienteering中此前未知。相比之前Submodular MDP线性依赖H的近似比有显著改进。此外，方法揭示了近似保证与智能体决策所依赖的历史访问顶点数量之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该工作通过LP-based Sherali-Adams与Round-or-Cut技术，为Submodular Orienteering和Submodular MDP提供了新的多项式时间近似算法，达到$O(n^{\varepsilon})$/$O(H^{\varepsilon})$近似比，明显优于此前线性依赖H的结果；同时揭示近似比与决策所依赖历史顶点数之间的内在权衡。

Abstract: Finding policies for Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) is a central problem in areas such as Reinforcement Learning and Operations Research. Here, we have to repeatedly choose an action that should be performed by an agent. Depending on the action and the current state of the agent, the agent collects a reward and randomly transitions into a new state. The goal is to maximize the reward in expectation over a finite time horizon of length $H$. We consider a recently introduced variant that generalizes the traditionally additive reward function in the model to a monotone submodular one, which allows for capturing a range of interesting applications.
  Without the stochastic component, this problem is equivalent to the Submodular Orienteering problem, where the goal is to find an $s$-$t$ walk in a directed graph maximizing a monotone submodular function under a length constraint. We present a novel LP-based algorithm for Submodular Orienteering using ideas from the Sherali-Adams hierarchy and Round-or-Cut. Our guarantees are comparable to the known quasi-polynomial time logarithmic approximation for Submodular Orienteering, but also extend to the setting of Submodular Markov Decision Processes. In the polynomial time regime, we present an $O(n^{\varepsilon})$-approximation (and $O(H^{\varepsilon})$ for Submodular MDPs) for every $\varepsilon >0$, where $n$ is the number of vertices, which was unknown even for Submodular Orienteering. Prior to our work, the best known approximation guarantee for Submodular MDPs had an approximation ratio linear in $H$.
  Beyond these algorithmic results, our methods reveal a trade-off between the approximation guarantee and the number of previously visited vertices on which an agent conditions its decision.

</details>


### [67] [Breaking the 1/3 Barrier for $\boldsymbol{k}$-Submodular Maximization under Matroid and Knapsack Constraints: A Proportional Top-2 Randomized Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15677)
*Siyuan Chen,Shengminjie Chen,Suixiang Gao,Zheyu Jiang,Chenhao Wang,Wenguo Yang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究非负非单调 k-子模函数在拟阵与背包约束下的最大化问题，提出随机化贪心算法首次突破 1/3 近似比障碍，达到 √2−1≈0.4142。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-子模性推广了子模性，允许每个元素被赋予 k 个标签之一。非单调约束 k-子模最大化的已知近似比长期停留在 1/3 或 1/3−ε，即便在基数、拟阵、背包等基础约束下也是如此。作者质疑这一 1/3 障碍是否为固有限制。

Method: 针对拟阵约束和背包约束，分别设计随机多项式时间算法。核心为简单的随机贪心规则：当某元素被选中时，其标签仅从边际增益最大的两个标签中选取，概率正比于这两个增益的正部。拟阵设定下值预言查询复杂度为 O(n²k)，背包设定下为 O(n³k²)。

Result: 在拟阵与背包约束下均实现了 √2−1≈0.4142 的近似比随机算法。查询复杂度：拟阵 O(n²k)，背包 O(n³k²)。

Conclusion: 证明了 1/3 障碍并非固有，给出了非单调 k-子模最大化在拟阵与背包约束下首个超过 1/3 的近似保证。

Abstract: $k$-submodularity generalizes submodularity by allowing each selected element to be assigned one of $k$ labels, rather than being merely selected or not selected. We study the problem of maximizing a nonnegative non-monotone $k$-submodular function, where $k\ge 2$, under classical support constraints, including a single matroid constraint and a single knapsack constraint. Previously, the best known approximation guarantees for non-monotone constrained $k$-submodular maximization had long remained at $1/3$ or $1/3-\varepsilon$, even in basic settings such as cardinality, matroid, and knapsack constraints. We show that this $1/3$ barrier is not inherent: for both the matroid and knapsack settings considered here, we give randomized polynomial-time algorithms achieving an approximation ratio of $\sqrt{2}-1\approx 0.4142$. The algorithms use a simple randomized greedy rule: once an element is selected, its label is chosen only from the two labels with the largest marginal gains, with probabilities proportional to the positive parts of these two gains. The value-oracle query complexity is $O(n^2k)$ in the matroid setting and $O(n^3k^2)$ in the knapsack setting. These results give the first approximation guarantees exceeding $1/3$ for non-monotone $k$-submodular maximization under matroid and knapsack constraints.

</details>


### [68] [Consistency-Robustness Tradeoffs for Online Bipartite Allocation with Multiple Stages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15837)
*Alexander Lindermayr,Nicole Megow,Lauren Paul*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文首次给出任意 k≥2 阶段顶点加权分数二部匹配在带预测情形下的一致性—鲁棒性权衡，并扩展到经典在线、AdWords 及分数预测场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段情形已有紧确的一致性—鲁棒性权衡，但任意 k 阶段无任何非平凡权衡结果，需填补该空白。

Method: 基于逐阶段凸规划，配以精心校准的顶点相关惩罚项，为每个供给顶点维持动态安全储备，平衡对对抗性未来到达的防护与对预测配置的利用。

Result: 对每个 k≥2 及 R∈[0,R_k]，算法 R-鲁棒且 C_k(R)-一致，其中 R_k=1-(1-1/k)^k，C_k(R)=k(1-R)^{1/k}+R-(k-1)；同时恢复两阶段紧确权衡与无预测最优保证 R_k，且严格优于随机抛硬币基线。经典在线场景一致性至少为 C_∞(R)=1+R+ln(1-R)，R∈[0,1-1/e]，改进了已有最优界。

Conclusion: 框架统一刻画多阶段带预测在线分配的一致性—鲁棒性权衡，并可推广至分数 AdWords 与分数预测。

Abstract: We study learning-augmented online bipartite allocation with multiple stages. In the $k$-stage vertex-weighted fractional bipartite matching problem, demand vertices arrive in $k$ stages, and the algorithm receives possibly inaccurate predictions of the allocation in each stage. While tight consistency-robustness tradeoffs were known for the two-stage case, no nontrivial tradeoff was known for an arbitrary number of stages.
  Our main result is the first consistency-robustness tradeoff for $k$-stage vertex-weighted fractional bipartite matching with predictions, for every $k\ge2$. Let $R_k=1-(1-1/k)^k$. For every $R\in[0,R_k]$, our algorithm is $R$-robust and $C_k(R)$-consistent, where $C_k(R)=k(1-R)^{1/k}+R-(k-1)$. This simultaneously recovers the known tight two-stage tradeoff and the optimal prediction-free $k$-stage competitive guarantee $R_k = C_k(R_k)$, while strictly dominating the natural randomized coin-flip baseline between these endpoints.
  We also present an algorithm for the classical online setting, where demands arrive one by one and the number of demands is unknown in advance. It has a consistency ratio of at least $C_\infty(R)=1+R+\ln(1-R)$ for a given robustness $R\in[0,1-1/e]$, improving the best previously known tradeoff for this problem. Finally, we extend the framework to fractional AdWords and fractional predictions.
  Our algorithms are based on stage-wise convex programs with carefully calibrated vertex-dependent penalties. The penalties maintain a dynamic safety reserve for each supply vertex, balancing protection against adversarial future arrivals with the ability to exploit the predicted allocation.

</details>


### [69] [Thin-shell stability of Gaussian cooling: logconcave sampling with sesteric complexity from a cold start](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15884)
*Yunbum Kook,Santosh S. Vempala*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该文证明高斯冷却路径上的对数凹测度具备薄壳稳定性，改进冷启动下一般对数凹分布采样复杂度至近 n^2.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷启动下对任意对数凹分布进行采样是采样理论的基础问题，已有复杂度为 n^2.75，与抽象 Speedy 行走的复杂度之间存在差距，需要更精细的几何稳定性工具来弥合。

Method: 沿高斯冷却路径研究对数凹概率测度，证明其具有薄壳稳定性，从而推广了经典薄壳定理；并以此几何稳定性结论为基础分析采样算法复杂度。

Result: 对（近）各向同性对数凹分布，采样复杂度由 n^2.75 降至近 n^2.5，达到与抽象 Speedy 行走相同的复杂度量级。

Conclusion: 薄壳稳定性可沿高斯冷却路径成立，这一推广为冷启动对数凹采样提供了更优复杂度界，并揭示了其与抽象 Speedy 行走之间的复杂度联系。

Abstract: We show that logconcave probability measures along the Gaussian cooling path have thin-shell stability, generalizing the thin-shell theorem. This result leads to improved complexity for the fundamental problem of sampling an arbitrary logconcave distribution from a cold start. For (near-)isotropic logconcave distributions, the complexity is nearly $n^{2.5}$, improving the previous bound of $n^{2.75}$, and matching the complexity of the abstract Speedy walk.

</details>


### [70] [The $k$-server conjecture is true](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15979)
*Christian Coester,Elias Koutsoupias,Marek Zbysiński*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了k-服务器猜想：确定性在线算法在每个度量空间上都能达到竞争比k，具体证明了工作函数算法（WFA）满足该竞争比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-服务器猜想长期未决，本文旨在给出其完整证明，并刻画工作函数算法在该猜想中的最优性。

Method: 将工作函数表示为矩阵：矩阵编码所有到达某构型的可行路径；最优代价定义中的min与加法分别对应形式表达式的加法与乘法；每个工作函数值对应矩阵中k列的determinant。请求到达时通过基变换与行替换更新该表示。摊还分析基于一个由更大矩阵定义的势函数，其坐标取原矩阵表示的坐标对。

Result: 证明工作函数算法满足k-服务器猜想，即达到竞争比k。

Conclusion: k-服务器猜想成立，工作函数算法为达到竞争比k的确定性在线算法。

Abstract: The $k$-server conjecture states that a deterministic online algorithm can achieve competitive ratio $k$ on every metric space. We prove the conjecture. Specifically, we show that the work function algorithm satisfies it.
  Our proof uses a natural algebraic representation of the work function as a matrix, which encodes all feasible paths to reach a configuration. In this representation, the minimum and addition operations arising in the definition of optimal costs correspond to addition and multiplication of formal expressions, and each work function value corresponds to the determinant of $k$ columns of the matrix. A request arrival updates the representation via a change of basis and row replacement. The amortized analysis is based on a potential function defined in terms of a larger matrix whose coordinates are pairs of coordinates of the original matrix representation.

</details>
