### [71] [COMPASS: Steering Distributed Vector Search with Scientific Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13452)
*Song Young Oh,Amal Gueroudji,Seth Ockerman,Rob Latham,Orcun Yildiz,Ian Foster,Kyle Chard,Robert Ross*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: COMPASS利用知识图谱结构进行向量数据库的数据分片放置与查询路由，仅检索13-18%语料即可保持广播召回，并在HPC上实现7.9倍吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量数据库常用哈希分片，破坏语义局部性，使查询退化为受最慢分片限制的scatter-gather；向量空间聚类虽有帮助，但科学证据常由与嵌入距离不一致的事实关系连接。

Method: COMPASS以知识图谱（KG）决定数据放置与查询时分片选择：检测社区、拆分超大社区、按主体实体插入嵌入、将查询路由到少量分片。

Result: 在四个生物医学KG上仅搜索13-18%语料，保持broadcast召回，并比基于嵌入的基线多恢复最多2.6倍多跳证据；在15个HPC节点上相比哈希广播实现7.9倍吞吐且尾延迟更低。

Conclusion: KG结构为可扩展向量搜索提供了对嵌入几何的紧凑补充。

Abstract: Vector databases use hashing to partition data across "shards," logical units for distributed execution. This placement, however, destroys semantic locality, forcing each query into scatter-gather limited by the slowest shard. Vector-space clustering can help, but scientific evidence is often connected by factual relations that do not align with embedding distance. We present COMPASS, a framework that uses a knowledge graph (KG) to determine data placement and query-time shard selection. COMPASS detects communities, splits oversized communities, inserts embeddings by subject entity, and routes queries to a small set of shards. Across four biomedical KGs, our method searches only 13-18% of the corpus while preserving broadcast recall and recovering up to 2.6x more multi-hop evidence than an embedding-based baseline. On 15 HPC nodes, COMPASS sustains 7.9x higher throughput with lower tail latency than hash-based broadcast. These results show that KG structure provides a compact complement to embedding geometry for scalable vector search.

</details>


### [72] [Numerical Stability of Linear Algebra Operations over Relational Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13802)
*Andrei Draghici,Yuchen He,Dan Olteanu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究数据库连接（join）矩阵上线性代数运算的数值稳定性问题。作者指出经典后向稳定性在此场景失效（因连接矩阵构成结构化子空间），提出"投影后向稳定性"概念，并引入"数据库条件数"来刻画连接矩阵扰动如何放大为输入数据库扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据库文献虽有大量针对连接矩阵的高效线性代数与机器学习算法，但一直忽视数值稳定性。这是实际隐患：连接矩阵可能远大于输入数据库，其中输入值的重复副本会累积浮点误差。因此有必要对连接矩阵上的线性代数进行正式的数值稳定性研究。

Method: 1）指出标准后向稳定性在此场景失效，因连接矩阵构成环境矩阵空间的结构化子空间，解释计算结果的扰动未必对应任何受扰动的输入数据库，且矩阵-向量乘法这类简单运算即已出现该问题。2）提出"投影后向稳定性"，将后向稳定性从单函数计算推广到两函数复合（连接查询与数值运算），并建立其与经典后向稳定性的联系。3）引入"数据库条件数"，定义为输入数据值进入连接矩阵的最大与最小副本数之比的平方根。

Result: 投影后向稳定性成功克服了经典后向稳定性在连接矩阵场景下的失效问题；数据库条件数能够独立于具体计算方式，量化连接矩阵扰动放大为输入数据库扰动的程度，且与将输入值复制进连接矩阵的"扩展矩阵"的经典条件数一致。

Conclusion: 本文开创性地对数据库连接上的线性代数数值稳定性进行了形式化研究，提出的投影后向稳定性与数据库条件数为分析连接矩阵计算误差提供了新框架，深化了对该场景数值行为（尤其重复副本导致的误差放大）的理解。

Abstract: A large body of work in the database literature develops efficient algorithms for linear algebra and machine learning over matrices defined by relational joins, yet the numerical stability of such computations has so far received no attention. This is a practical concern: a join matrix can be much larger than the input database, and the repeated copies of input values it contains compound the floating-point errors incurred by the numerical operations performed over it.
  This paper initiates a formal investigation of numerical stability for linear algebra over database joins. We first show that backward stability, the standard yardstick of numerical stability, loses its effectiveness in this setting: join matrices form a structured subspace of the ambient matrix space, so a perturbation explaining a computed result need not correspond to any perturbed input database. This failure already occurs for operations as simple as matrix-vector multiplication.
  To overcome this limitation, we introduce projected backward stability, a generalization of backward stability from the computation of one function to that of a composition of two functions, and establish its connection to classical backward stability. In our database setting, the two functions are the join query and the numerical operation.
  We further introduce the database condition number as the square root of the ratio of maximal to minimal number of copies of input data values into the join matrix, and show that it quantifies how a perturbation of the join matrix is amplified into a perturbation of the input database, independently of the computation used. The database condition number coincides with the classical condition number of the expansion matrix that replicates input values into the join matrix.

</details>


### [73] [Towards Anticipatory Databases Through Shared Data and Workload Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14255)
*Farzaneh Zirak,Kasper Overgaard Mortensen,Farhana Choudhury,Renata Borovica-Gajic*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 论文主张将"工作负载语义"作为数据库管理系统中一类新的控制信号，提出语义局部性与语义轨迹概念，并构建跨数据、查询、会话三层的语义上下文框架，在语义预取与语义缓存淘汰两个场景中验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DBMS决策依赖新近性、频率、地址局部性等低层信号，这些信号只刻画"数据如何被访问"，无法反映"正在分析什么内容"以及分析焦点如何随时间演变，因而难以支撑面向动态探索性工作负载的预判式决策。

Method: 提出以工作负载语义作为一等控制信号；定义语义局部性与语义轨迹（刻画相邻查询间的关系及其在会话内的演化）；构建数据层、查询层、会话层的语义上下文表示框架，建模其时间演化并转化为任务特定的效用估计；在语义预取与语义缓存淘汰两个系统中共用同一语义层，分别用语义轨迹预测未来访问、用语义相关性指导块替换。

Result: 两个系统提供了初步证据，表明共享的语义上下文可同时支撑DBMS的多个组件；语义预取能捕获地址局部性无法覆盖的未来访问，语义淘汰可基于语义相关性改进块替换决策。

Conclusion: 工作负载语义可作为跨组件的通用控制信号，该原则有望扩展至其他DBMS决策与数据系统；同时需应对表示、成本、自适应与评估方面的关键挑战。

Abstract: Database management systems increasingly serve dynamic and exploratory workloads, yet many of their decisions still rely on low-level signals such as recency, frequency, and address locality. These signals capture how data was accessed, but not what is being examined or how an analytical focus evolves. We argue for treating workload semantics as a first-class control signal for anticipatory decision making. Central to this view, we introduce semantic locality and semantic trajectories, which capture relationships among nearby queries and how those relationships evolve across a session.
  We propose a framework that represents semantic context at the data, query, and session levels, models its evolution over time, and translates it into task-specific utility estimates. We instantiate this framework in semantic prefetching and semantic cache eviction, which share a semantic layer to make two separate decisions. Prefetching uses semantic trajectories to anticipate future accesses beyond what address-based locality can capture, while eviction uses semantic relevance to inform block replacement. These systems provide initial evidence that shared semantic context can support multiple DBMS components. We further outline how this principle can extend to other decisions and data systems, and discuss key challenges in representation, cost, adaptation, and evaluation.

</details>


### [74] [DiaLSM: Towards Write-Stall-Free Performance via Shard-based LSM-tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14370)
*Hongsu Byun,Safdar Jamil,Honghyeon Yoo,Sungyong Park,Myungcheol Lee,Xubin He,Zhichao Cao,Youngjae Kim*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文用排队模型量化LSM写停顿的发生概率与持续时间，证明单流水线LSM无法根除写停顿，进而提出内部分片架构DiaLSM，在RocksDB上实现最高2.4倍吞吐提升、停顿降低94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LSM-tree虽能提供高写入吞吐，但在持续写入压力下会出现写停顿问题。现有研究多停留在经验性分析，缺乏对写停顿发生概率与持续时间的定量刻画，也未揭示其结构性根因。

Method: 将LSM的写--刷盘--压缩（write--flush--compaction）流水线建模为排队系统，定量推导写停顿的发生概率与平均持续时间；基于模型发现单流水线单体LSM无法消除写停顿，且LSM内部的分片（internal sharding）是根本性缓解途径。据此提出DiaLSM：将写--刷盘--压缩路径拆分为多个独立分片，并采用动态回退重定向（dynamic fallback redirection），使部分分片停顿时写入仍可继续。DiaLSM在RocksDB上实现。

Result: 在db_bench、YCSB与Sysbench OLTP评测中，DiaLSM相比ADOC与Sub-Compaction等最先进方法，吞吐最高提升2.4倍，写停顿减少94%，延迟显著降低。

Conclusion: LSM写停顿源于单流水线的结构性瓶颈，无法通过单体优化消除；内部分片配合动态回退重定向可在部分分片停顿时维持写入进度，从结构上根本缓解写停顿，实验验证其吞吐、停顿与延迟均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Log-Structured Merge-tree (LSM) aims to achieve high write throughput, but is known to experience the write stall problems when subjected to sustained write pressure. We quantify the occurrence probability and average duration of write stalls in LSM using a queuing model in the write--flush--compaction pipeline, moving beyond existing empirical analysis. The proposed model demonstrates that a monolithic LSM with a single pipeline cannot eliminate write stalls, revealing that internal sharding within the LSM offers an opportunity for fundamental write stall mitigation. To break this structural bottleneck, we propose DiaLSM, an internally shard-based LSM architecture. Instead of forcing all writes through one pipeline, DiaLSM splits the write--flush--compaction path into multiple independent shards and employs dynamic fallback redirection, allowing writes to proceed even when some shards stall. Implemented on RocksDB, DiaLSM achieves up to 2.4x higher throughput, 94% lower stalls, and significantly lower latency than state-of-the-art methods ADOC and Sub-Compaction, as demonstrated by db_bench, YCSB, and Sysbench OLTP evaluations.

</details>


### [75] [Natural Language Knowledge Graph Query Execution: Leveraging Controlled Semantics in the LLM Context Window](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14652)
*Blake G. Fitch*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出 NLKGQ 系统：通过 OWL 本体作为"受控语义"迁移机制，将数据模型概念以机器可读词元注入 LLM 上下文，实现零样本 NL→SPARQL 生成，并构建 DBLP-QuAD 3.1 基准以提升评测确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 应用多以非正式方式（提示散文、模式转储、示例）向模型传递领域概念，词汇语义未被机器显式声明，导致数据库查询任务中概念传递效果不稳定、评测结果受快照/端点/问句措辞影响而不可复现。

Method: 以形式化 OWL 本体作为迁移机制，把数据语义浓缩为语义精确的 token；单次 LLM 调用中向上下文注入 SPARQL 系统提示、完整领域 OWL 本体、领域特定提示补充与用户自然语言问句，由模型零样本直接生成 SPARQL。对原生词汇不透明的数据库（或联邦），用包装本体替换为干净术语，并在运行时由重写器还原原生形式。同时提出 DBLP-QuAD 3.1：在保持 2.0 意图前提下使用前沿模型重写自然语言问句、修正参考 SPARQL、使参考结果确定化。

Result: DBLP-QuAD 2.0 确定性重评下 57.6% Match；DBLP-QuAD 3.1 上 1,000 题达 89.9% Match；SemOpenAlex 上 98% Match（同一测试集公开基线为 86%）；神经影像元数据任务达 100%。

Conclusion: 采用带声明式机器可读语义（受控语义）的词汇表示，比非正式提示更能有效向 LLM 传递数据模型概念；OWL 本体 + 运行时重写器的框架可复用，并在多类知识图谱查询基准上取得显著且一致的性能优势。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) applications often transfer domain concepts into the model's context informally, through prompt prose, schema dumps, and examples. We show that for database queries, data model concepts pass to LLMs more effectively through representations whose vocabulary terms carry declared, machine-readable semantics (controlled semantics). NLKGQ is a working system and reusable framework that does this for data modeled in a knowledge graph. A formal OWL ontology serves as the transfer mechanism, concentrating the meaning of the data into semantically precise tokens the model can use directly. In a single LLM call, NLKGQ places in the context a system prompt instructing on SPARQL, the complete domain OWL ontology, and a domain-specific prompt addition, together with the user's natural language query. The model then generates the SPARQL query directly, zero-shot. Where the native vocabulary of an existing database or federation of databases is opaque, a wrapper ontology substitutes clean terms and a runtime rewriter restores the native forms. Evaluating on DBLP-QuAD 2.0 showed that its scores depend on the graph snapshot, the endpoint used, and the wording of its machine-generated questions, so we propose DBLP-QuAD 3.1, which maintains the intent of 2.0 while making reference results deterministic, revising reference SPARQL where needed, and rewriting the natural language questions, with a frontier model, to state each reference query's intent clearly and completely. We evaluate on the DBLP-QuAD 2.0 benchmark (57.6% Match under deterministic re-scoring), DBLP-QuAD 3.1 (89.9% Match on 1,000 questions), SemOpenAlex (98% Match against a published baseline's 86% on the identical test set), and neuroimaging metadata (100%).

</details>


### [76] [Chronos: Efficient Bolt-on Branching Across Data Stores for Stateful Agentic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14889)
*Xinjing Zhou,Jason Mohoney,Samuel Madden,Michael Stonebraker,Lei Cao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Chronos 是一个"bolt-on"系统，通过基于区间的版本化技术与分离式架构，为异构数据存储提供高效的分支与合并能力，显著加速投机执行类应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据密集型应用（尤其工具调用智能体）越来越多地采用投机执行来探索多条候选路径，每条路径都会修改跨异构数据存储的状态。因此应用需要能快速创建分支、隔离状态修改路径、并跨存储一致合并的系统，且不引入过多查询开销。现有系统仅提供部分支持，迫使应用手动协调分支与合并，增加了开销并带来跨存储状态不一致的风险。

Method: Chronos 提出两项核心贡献：（1）紧凑的基于区间的版本化技术，通过简单查询重写实现高效分支与数据共享；（2）bolt-on 架构，将分支管理与各存储内的数据路径分离。结合区间版本化，这种分离为合并提供原子性的跨存储可见性，并在不修改各存储引擎的前提下支持多种数据存储。作者在 PostgreSQL、SQLite、DuckDB、Qdrant 及一个基于 DBMS 的文件系统上实现了 Chronos。

Result: 在跨存储智能体工作流、MCTS 风格探索及单存储基准测试上的评估表明：Chronos 的 MCTS 风格探索比现有方法快至多 16.7 倍，同时在各底层存储上保持实用的查询性能。在并发跨存储工作流下，Chronos 避免了部分可见的合并，并显著优于串行化执行。

Conclusion: Chronos 以 bolt-on 方式为异构数据存储提供高效的分支与合并能力，在不修改存储引擎的前提下实现了原子性的跨存储可见性与一致的合并，同时大幅提升投机执行类应用的性能，并兼顾底层查询效率。

Abstract: Data-centric applications increasingly use speculative execution to explore multiple candidate paths where each path modifies state distributed across heterogeneous data stores. This trend is intensified by the rise of tool-calling agents. Hence, applications need data systems that can create branches quickly, isolate state-modifying paths, and merge changes consistently across stores without imposing substantial query overhead. Existing systems provide only partial support, forcing applications to coordinate branches and merges manually, which increases overhead and risks inconsistent cross-store state.
  To solve this problem, we introduce Chronos, a bolt-on system that provides branching capability across heterogeneous data stores. We make two contributions. First, Chronos introduces a compact interval-based versioning technique that enables efficient branching and data sharing through simple query rewrite. Second, Chronos introduces a bolt-on architecture that separates branch management from data path within each store. Combined with interval-based versioning, this separation provides atomic cross-store visibility for merges and enables Chronos to support diverse data stores without modifying their engines.
  We implement Chronos for PostgreSQL, SQLite, DuckDB, Qdrant, and a DBMS-backed filesystem. We evaluate it using cross-store agent workflows, MCTS-style exploration, and per-store benchmarks. Chronos runs MCTS-style exploration up to 16.7x faster than existing approaches while maintaining practical query performance across the underlying stores. Under concurrent cross-store workflows, Chronos prevents partially visible merges while substantially outperforming serialized execution.

</details>


### [77] [FastPair: GPU-Optimized String Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15034)
*Joseph Isaacs,Francesco Gargiulo,Peter Boncz,Robert Kruszewski,Nicholas Gates,Rossano Venturini,Will Manning,Martin Prammer*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出FastPair，一种GPU字典解码器，通过重组查找顺序并组装解码子串以实现连续输出写，在B300上较DE快2.4–4.2倍，峰值达1.6 TB/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据系统将数据压缩存储、按需解压以节省互连带宽，但该设计在GPU平台上效率低下：传统压缩技术存在串行数据依赖，限制GPU并行性，造成资源闲置。NVIDIA GPU引入解压引擎（DE）加速Deflate、LZ4、Snappy等通用格式解码。近期提出的字符串编解码器用小型训练字典中的定宽码替换高频子串，使各码查找相互独立、可并行执行，但由此产生的离散读取与短输出写仍与GPU硬件偏好连续访存的特性不匹配。

Method: FastPair在现有字典解码流程基础上进行优化：重新组织查找操作，并将解码出的子串组装起来以实现连续输出写入，从而改善内存访问模式与GPU硬件的契合度。

Result: 在B300上，FastPair对十个真实数据列的解码速度比DE快2.4至4.2倍，吞吐量最高达到1.6 TB/s。

Conclusion: 通过针对GPU访存特性重组字典解码过程，可显著提升字符串编解码器的GPU解码性能，优于专用解压引擎。

Abstract: Modern data systems compress data at rest and decompress it only when needed to preserve interconnect bandwidth. This design is often inefficient on GPU-based compute platforms because many conventional compression techniques exhibit serial data dependencies that limit GPU parallelism, leaving resources idle. Recent NVIDIA GPUs address this decoding deficiency through the Decompression Engine (DE), an on-die, fixed-function decompression accelerator for general-purpose compression formats such as Deflate, LZ4, and Snappy. Recent work has proposed string codecs that replace frequent substrings with fixed-width codes from a small, trained dictionary, making each code's lookup independent. While these lookups can run in parallel, the resulting scattered reads and short output writes still do not align well with GPU hardware, which handles contiguous memory accesses more efficiently. We present FastPair, a GPU decoder that optimizes the existing dictionary decoding process by reorganizing lookups and assembling decoded substrings for contiguous output writes. On a B300, FastPair decodes ten real-world columns 2.4 to 4.2x faster than the DE, reaching up to 1.6 TB/s.

</details>


### [78] [Fast Label-Filtering Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search via Progressive Label Set Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15058)
*Ziqi Wang,Jingzhe Zhang,Shuo Shen,Wei Hu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出标签分层相似图（LSSG），用于带标签过滤的近似最近邻搜索（LFANNS），通过增量插入剪枝与MinHash实现跨过滤类型一致且可扩展的高效检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LFANNS索引在不同过滤类型（相等、包含、重叠）下性能不一致，且在标签规模与分布变化时可扩展性下降。

Method: 定义LSSG：边连接标签集落在分层相似度阈值内的近邻向量；设计增量插入算法在向量与标签空间双重剪枝冗余边；利用MinHash结构支持大规模标签；并在显式标签模型下分析逐步概率，解释更严格标签层级为何减少无效的过滤内扩展。

Result: LSSG在相等查询上达到理想最优性；在相同精度下，包含与重叠查询分别比最优对比索引快1.06x-92.9x与1.08x-84.1x，索引大小为0.35x。

Conclusion: LSSG通过分层相似度图与双重空间剪枝，统一提升了多种标签过滤类型下的LFANNS查询效率与可扩展性，同时显著降低索引开销。

Abstract: Approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) retrieves the most similar vectors to a query vector in high-dimensional space. Label-filtering ANNS (LFANNS) extends ANNS with a label filter that the labels of base vectors must satisfy a set relation (e.g., equality, containment, or overlap) with the query labels. Existing LFANNS indices suffer from inconsistent performance across different filter types and degraded scalability under varying label scale and distribution. In this paper, we define label-stratified similarity graph (LSSG), where edges connect neighboring vectors whose label sets fall within stratified similarity thresholds. To implement LSSG efficiently, we design an incremental insertion algorithm to prune redundant edges in both vector and label spaces, and leverage a MinHash structure to ensure scalability for large-scale labels. We analyze stepwise probabilities under explicit label models and explain why stricter label tiers reduce ineffective in-filtering expansions. Benchmark experiments show that LSSG achieves ideal optimality for equality queries, and 1.06x-92.9x and 1.08x-84.1x faster than the best competing index for containment and overlap, respectively, in query speed with identical accuracy and 0.35x index size.

</details>


### [79] [TEAR: Table Extraction with Attribute Recommendation from Texts via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15205)
*Tong Li,Shuye Ding,Jiachuan Wang,Yongqi Zhang,Shuangyin Li,Lei Chen,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出TEAR框架，用于从自然文本（新闻报道、社交媒体）中抽取表格信息并推荐新属性，解决了启发式提示难以界定抽取边界及预定义模式无法覆盖开放属性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的表格抽取方法假设输入为表格描述或专业文档，忽略了新闻报道和社交媒体中常见的"自然文本"。这类文本存在两大挑战：(1) 高变异性与缺乏显式结构线索，使固定启发式提示难以精确界定抽取边界；(2) 人工预定义的模式无法捕捉自然文本中开放、未见过的属性。

Method: 提出TEAR框架，包含两个协同工作流：表格抽取工作流（Table Extraction Workflow），动态自适应调整指令以克服启发式指令的局限；属性推荐工作流（Attribute Recommendation Workflow），从文本中发现新属性以补充启发式模式。TEAR是首个支持自动化文本驱动属性推荐、实现探索式模式设计的框架。

Result: 构建了自然文本上的表格抽取与属性推荐基准，包含两个真实世界数据集、人工标注、合适的评价指标与基线对比。实验表明TEAR在两个任务上均达到state-of-the-art性能，且推荐属性在探索式场景中有效提升抽取性能。

Conclusion: TEAR通过动态指令适配与自动化属性推荐，突破了自然文本表格抽取中边界界定与模式开放性的双重限制，并在新建基准上验证了其有效性与探索式抽取能力。

Abstract: Table extraction from texts is an important task for information systems, and recent approaches that prompt large language models (LLMs) with instructions have drawn great attention for their strong performance. Existing works have assumed the input texts to be table descriptions or specialized documents. However, these efforts have largely overlooked another prevalent category of texts, commonly found in news reports and social media: naturally occurring texts. Extracting tabular information from such texts poses two distinct challenges. First, high variability and the absence of explicit structural cues make fixed heuristic LLM prompts limited in precisely delineating extraction boundaries. Second, manually predefined schemas cannot capture open-ended, unseen attributes in naturally occurring text. In this paper, we propose a framework, TEAR, to address these challenges. It comprises two synergistic workflows: a Table Extraction Workflow that dynamically adapts instructions to overcome the limitation of heuristic instructions, and an Attribute Recommendation Workflow that discovers new attributes from texts to complement the heuristic schema. To our knowledge, TEAR is the first framework that supports automated text-driven attribute recommendation, enabling exploratory schema design for table extraction. To evaluate TEAR, we establish the benchmark for table extraction and attribute recommendation on naturally occurring texts, including two real-world datasets, manual annotations, appropriate metrics, and baseline comparisons. Experiments show that TEAR achieves state-of-the-art performance on both tasks, and the recommended attributes effectively enhance extraction performance in exploratory scenarios.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [80] [When Do Pilots Matter for OFDM Sensing? Pilot-Data Resource Design for ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13329)
*Shengcai Zhou,Luping Xiang,Yi Wang,Kun Yang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文建立了导频-数据（P-D）OFDM感知的解析与优化框架，揭示导频放置与功率分配对随机OFDM通信信号感知旁瓣的联合影响，并提出ROI感知的资源优化设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际OFDM-ISAC信号同时包含确定性导频与随机数据载荷，但二者如何共同影响匹配滤波感知性能尚不清晰，缺乏对导频放置与功率分配的解析刻画与优化设计原则。

Method: 推导闭式均方周期自相关函数（MS P-ACF），分离平均子载波功率谱与数据符号随机性对感知旁瓣的贡献；分析导频放置在不同功率分配下的作用；推导全局EISL最优的两级导频-数据功率分配与峰值旁瓣抑制的导频放置；针对ROI感知联合优化导频放置与子载波功率分配，证明特定条件下严格均匀导频放置全局最优，并提出二分-分支定界算法。

Result: 导频放置在均匀功率分配下不影响归一化旁瓣与归一化全局EISL，在非均匀功率分配下可作为旁瓣重塑自由度；所提设计相比优化均匀导频OFDM将ROI旁瓣降低最多2.19 dB，在-10 dB SNR下测距精度提升84.3%。

Conclusion: 研究明确了导频放置何时以及如何用于调控随机OFDM通信信号的感知行为，为OFDM-ISAC的实际资源设计提供原则。

Abstract: Practical OFDM-based integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) signals contain deterministic pilots and random data payloads, yet how these two types of resources jointly affect matched-filter sensing performance remains insufficiently understood. This paper establishes an analytical and optimization framework for pilot-data (P-D) OFDM sensing. We first derive a closed-form mean-square periodic autocorrelation function (MS P-ACF), which characterizes the respective contributions of the average subcarrier power spectrum and data-symbol randomness to sensing sidelobes. Based on this result, we reveal that pilot placement has no impact on normalized sidelobes under uniform power allocation or on the normalized global expected integrated sidelobe level (EISL), whereas it becomes an effective design degree of freedom for reshaping sidelobes under nonuniform power allocation. We further derive the globally optimal two-level pilot-data power allocation for global EISL minimization and characterize pilot placement for peak sidelobe suppression. For region-of-interest (ROI) sensing, we jointly optimize pilot placement and subcarrier power allocation to minimize the normalized ROI-EISL. A strictly uniform pilot placement is proved globally optimal under specified conditions, while a bisection-and-branch-and-bound algorithm is developed for general resource configurations. Numerical results validate the theoretical analysis and demonstrate that the proposed design reduces the ROI sidelobe level by up to 2.19 dB compared with optimized uniform-pilot OFDM, while improving ranging accuracy at an SNR of -10 dB by 84.3$\%$. These results reveal when and how pilot placement can be exploited to regulate the sensing behavior of random OFDM communication signals, providing practical resource-design principles for OFDM-based ISAC.

</details>


### [81] [A New Upper Bound on the Binary Deletion Channel Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13351)
*Özgür Soysal*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文证明了二元删除信道容量在 $d\ge 13/20$ 时满足 $C(d)\le (1-d)/4$，并通过有限证书与 Lean 形式化验证给出端到端证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二元删除信道容量的上界，特别是大删除概率区域 $13/20\le d<1$ 的紧致界，并希望获得可机器验证的有限证明。

Method: 从右到左描述输出，用六比特上下文分配描述长度；界定增加一个输入比特时“期望描述长度增量减去输出熵”的上界；利用相对熵恒等式将界化归为有限多个线性不等式；对长度 26 的输入窗口构造势函数使不等式 telescoping；再通过删除合成将结果从 $d=13/20$ 推广到更大的删除概率。

Result: 证明 $C(d)\le (1-d)/4$ 对所有 $13/20\le d<1$ 成立；同时给出互信息与译码错误的有限块界，带有显式 $O(\log n/n)$ 修正；有限证书用精确整数算术检验，并在 Lean 中完成端到端形式化。

Conclusion: 该工作为大删除概率下的二元删除信道容量提供了可验证的有限上界证明，并把有限块界与形式化验证结合。

Abstract: We prove that the capacity of the binary deletion channel satisfies $C(d)\le (1-d)/4$ for every $13/20\le d<1$. The proof describes the output from right to left, using a six-bit context to assign a description length. We bound the increase in expected description length minus output entropy when one input bit is added. A relative-entropy identity reduces this bound to finitely many linear inequalities. A potential on input windows of length 26 makes the inequalities telescope, giving a bound for every input word. Deletion composition extends the result from $d=13/20$ to all larger deletion probabilities. We also obtain finite-block bounds on mutual information and decoding error, with explicit $O(\log n/n)$ corrections. The finite certificate is checked using exact integer arithmetic, and the proof is formalized end to end in Lean.

</details>


### [82] [From Priors to Projections: Geometry and simplified MIMO demodulation of probabilistic shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13492)
*Kirill Ivanov,Wei Yang,Mahmoud Taherzadeh Boroujeni,Jing Jiang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明：在MIMO系统中，若成形符号服从Maxwell-Boltzmann分布，则最优MAP检测等价于对均匀星座做一次简单预处理后的检测，从而复用统一处理链并降低接收机复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率成形（PS）在AWGN与Rayleigh衰落信道中已广泛研究，可提升规则QAM性能，但其在MIMO场景下的潜力尚待深入挖掘。

Method: 针对采用Maxwell-Boltzmann分布的成形星座，推导MIMO下最优MAP检测的等价形式，将其转化为均匀星座情形加上一个简单预处理步骤；该等价性同时适用于线性MMSE与非线性（近似）MAP解映射器，并可结合球形译码等自适应方法。

Result: 证明了成形与未成形系统可共用同一条处理链；该方法兼容线性MMSE与非线性（近）MAP解映射；并能降低球形译码等自适应方法的复杂度。

Conclusion: 该等价性为MIMO概率成形提供了简化的接收机架构与更低的检测复杂度，有助于推动PS在MIMO系统中的实际应用。

Abstract: Probabilistic shaping (PS) is a well-known method to achieve improved performance upon a regular quadrature amplitude modulation (QAM) by taking the target constellation and making the distribution of underlying points non-uniform. It has been extensively studied over the years for the additive white Gaussian noise (AWGN) and Rayleigh fading channels. However, the potential of probabilistic shaping in the multiple-input and multiple-output (MIMO) setting needs further investigations.
  In this paper, we prove that if shaped symbols follow Maxwell-Boltzmann distribution, the optimal maximum a posteriori (MAP) detection is equivalent to the case of uniform constellation with a simple preprocessing step. Our approach has multiple benefits. It enables to utilize the same processing chain for both shaped and unshaped system, which simplifies the receiver architecture. This technique can be applied for both linear MMSE and nonlinear (near-)MAP demapper types. In addition, the complexity of adaptive methods such as sphere decoding can be reduced.

</details>


### [83] [Worse Routes, Fresher Information: Preemption Redefines Which Route is Best](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13622)
*Adem Utku Atasayar,Aimin Li,Elif Uysal*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 研究N条异构传输路径下联合采样、路由激活与抢占控制的AoI优化问题，建模为平均代价脉冲控制，并用离散化策略迭代求解两条路径情形，发现抢占可逆转路由偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AoI与采样、传输代价的长期权衡目标下，如何在多条异构路径间进行采样、路由与抢占的联合优化，以及抢占机制对路由选择的影响尚不清晰。

Method: 将问题建模为平均代价脉冲控制模型；在混合状态空间上推导向量形式积分平均代价最优方程；对状态进行结构性约简；针对两条路径情形，采用离散化策略迭代计算最优策略。

Result: 最优策略可能出现抢占逆转路由偏好的现象：仅使用均值时延与方差均更大的路径，而回避看似更优的路径；相比不允许抢占的基线，所提策略将平均AoI最多降低96%。

Conclusion: 抢占控制会显著改变异构多路径系统的最优路由结构，且联合优化采样、激活与抢占可大幅降低平均AoI，说明忽略抢占的基线会严重高估某些路径的优越性。

Abstract: This paper studies joint sampling, route activation, and preemption control over $N$ heterogeneous transmission routes under a long-run objective combining Age of Information (AoI) with sampling and transmission costs. We formulate the problem as an average-cost impulse-control model, derive a vector-form integral average-cost optimality equation over a hybrid state space, and establish structural reductions on the state. We consider a two-route case and compute optimized policies using discretized policy iteration. Surprisingly, the results show that preemption can reverse routing preferences: the optimized policy may exclusively use a route with both a larger mean delay and a larger delay variance while avoiding the seemingly superior route. Relative to baselines that do not allow preemption, the proposed policy reduces average AoI by up to $96\%$.

</details>


### [84] [Weight spectra of some families of GRM codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13653)
*Minjia Shi,Zhaokang Xing,Patrick Sole*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该文完整确定了若干广义Reed-Muller码（GRM）在指定m范围内的重量谱，包括RM₃(2m-i,m) (i=3,4)、RM₄(3m-i,m) (i=2,3)、RM₅(4m-4,m)及RM₇(6m-4,m)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Reed-Muller码的重量谱是编码理论的核心问题，但一般情形的完整重量分布长期未解决。作者针对一系列高余维、特定阶数的GRM码族寻求完整刻画，以填补重量谱在更广参数区间上的空白。

Method: 证明按m进行归纳：利用GRM重量谱之间的和集包含关系（sumset inclusion），并结合低重量码字的结果推进归纳步骤。基例通过Magma计算建立，某些三元情形用Z3进行精确约束求解。

Result: 完全确定了RM₃(2m-i,m)（i=3,4）、RM₄(3m-i,m)（i=2,3）、RM₅(4m-4,m)、RM₇(6m-4,m)在各自所述m范围内的重量谱。

Conclusion: 所提归纳框架（和集包含+低重量码字分析）结合计算辅助的基例验证，可有效确定特定高余维GRM码的重量谱，为更一般的GRM重量谱研究提供了方法范式。

Abstract: Let $\mathrm{RM}_q(r,m)$ denote the generalized Reed--Muller code of order $r$ and length $q^m$ over the finite field $\mathbb{F}_q$.
  We completely determine the weight spectra of $\mathrm{RM}_3(2m-i,m)$ for $i=3,4$, $\mathrm{RM}_4(3m-i,m)$ for $i=2,3$, $\mathrm{RM}_5(4m-4,m)$, and $\mathrm{RM}_7(6m-4,m)$, in the ranges of $m$ specified in the corresponding results.
  The proofs proceed by induction on $m$, using a sumset inclusion for GRM weight spectra together with results on low-weight codewords. The required base cases are established by computations in Magma and, for certain ternary cases, exact constraint solving with Z3.

</details>


### [85] [Adaptive Coding for Two-Way Wiretap Channels with One-Sided Strong Secrecy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13832)
*Masahito Hayashi,Yanling Chen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 研究强单侧保密下离散无记忆双向窃听信道，建立非自适应与密钥辅助自适应内界，证明多轮泄漏定理，消除内部分量速率并刻画自适应增益，对一类二进制信道证明非自适应逆定理并给出自适应与非自适应的操作性分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双向窃听信道中，仅需保护用户1的消息而用户2消息无需保密，这一强单侧保密模型下自适应编码能否带来相对非自适应编码的增益尚不清楚。论文旨在刻画自适应策略的容量优势，并为非自适应编码建立精确的可达区域与逆定理。

Method: 基于选定索引分量的公共可解性估计，构造非自适应与密钥辅助自适应内界；对自适应构造证明多轮泄漏定理，涵盖密钥在使用前已泄露的信息以及编码器使用错误解码密钥的情形；证明中采用理想到实现的耦合、确定性码本选择与递增轮数论证以消除初始化速率损失；精确消除内部分量速率；对开放的二参数二进制信道族证明任意非自适应随机码的逆定理。

Result: 得到非自适应与自适应内界，并在每个固定辅助分布下刻画自适应增益；对某二参数二进制信道族建立任意非自适应随机码的逆定理，给出自适应相对非自适应的操作性分离；对其中一个代表性信道，精确确定完整的非自适应强单侧保密容量区域。

Conclusion: 自适应密钥辅助策略可严格优于非自适应编码，且多轮泄漏分析是刻画该增益的关键工具；所提框架同时给出非自适应强单侧保密容量区域的精确刻画与自适应—非自适应分离。

Abstract: We study a discrete memoryless two-way wiretap channel under strong one-sided secrecy, where User 1's message is confidential and User 2's message is not required to be secret. We develop non-adaptive and key-assisted adaptive inner bounds from a common resolvability estimate for selected index components. For the adaptive construction, we prove a multiround leakage theorem that accounts both for information revealed about a key before its later use and for the possibility that an encoder uses an incorrectly decoded key. The proof includes an ideal-to-implemented coupling, deterministic codebook selection, and a growing-round argument that removes the initialization-rate loss. We eliminate the internal component rates exactly and characterize the adaptive gain at each fixed auxiliary distribution. For an open two-parameter family of binary channels, we also prove a converse for arbitrary non-adaptive stochastic codes and obtain an operational adaptive-versus-non-adaptive separation. For one representative channel, the complete non-adaptive strong one-sided secrecy capacity region is identified exactly.

</details>


### [86] [A Novel Approach to Counterexamples of the Polujan-Pott Conjecture via Set-Partition Permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13968)
*Yansheng Wu,Jiaxin Wang,Jong Yoon Hyun*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文构造了变量数为 $2(2^t-1)$、代数次数为 $t+1$ 的无穷族 Maiorana--McFarland bent 函数 $f_t$，证明了 Polujan 与 Pott 的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Polujan 和 Pott 曾提出关于 Maiorana--McFarland bent 函数代数次数能否达到 $t+1$ 的猜想，此前缺乏显式无穷族的验证。本文旨在通过显式构造完全解决该猜想。

Method: 以最小交换代数 $I_t$ 为基础，由它自然诱导出三角集划分多项式置换 $P_t$；在直和 $I_t \oplus I_t^*$ 中识别初等交换子群，并据此建立显式非线性坐标变换，将 $f_t$ 拉回为规范二次型。

Result: 在奇异群结构下线性化了平移展开 $\operatorname{Dev}(D_{f_t})$，并证明其同构于经典辛设计 $S^\pm(2(2^t-1))$，从而完成了猜想的完整证明。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了满足代数次数 $t+1$ 的显式无穷族 Maiorana--McFarland bent 函数，揭示了其平移展开与经典辛设计之间的同构关系，完全解决了 Polujan--Pott 猜想。

Abstract: In this paper, we settle a conjecture of Polujan and Pott by constructing an explicit, infinite family of Maiorana--McFarland bent functions $f_t$ in $2(2^t-1)$ variables with algebraic degree $°(f_t) = t + 1$ for any integer $t \ge 2$. Our construction builds upon a minimal commutative algebra $I_t$, which naturally induces a triangular set-partition polynomial permutation $P_t$. By identifying an elementary abelian subgroup within the direct sum $ I_t \oplus I_t^*$, we establish an explicit nonlinear coordinate transformation that pulls $f_t$ back to a canonical quadratic form. This linearizes the translation development $\operatorname{Dev}(D_{f_t})$ under an exotic group structure and proves that it is isomorphic to the classical symplectic design $S^\pm(2(2^t-1))$, thereby fully resolving the conjecture.

</details>


### [87] [GNN-Based Polarforming for Multi-User MISO Short-Packet URLLC under Imperfect CSI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14006)
*Zahra Mehrzad,Hamed Aghaei-Karkaj,Rahman Saadat Yeganeh,Kamran Ebrahimi,Mohammad Robat Mili,Symeon Chatzinotas,Ioannis Krikidis*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该文研究不完美CSI下多用户MISO短包URLLC的极化感知传输，联合优化FBL可达和速率与最大解码错误概率，并提出异构图神经网络实现在线波束成形与极化赋形联合决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短包URLLC对时延与可靠性要求严苛，而实际系统中CSI不完美与信道去极化会显著恶化性能；传统固定极化方案难以适应极化状态变化，因此需要利用极化可重构天线进行自适应极化控制以提升可靠性。

Method: 构建基站与用户均配备极化可重构天线的MU-MISO系统，以可控极化系数实现自适应极化状态；建立以最大化FBL可达和速率、最小化最大DEP为目标的多目标优化问题，受发射功率、时延、可靠性与离散极化控制约束；采用归一化加权和效用进行标量化；设计异构图神经网络学习从估计极化CSI到数字波束成形及收发极化赋形向量的联合映射，实现在线低复杂度决策。

Result: 数值结果表明，相比传统固定极化方案，所提基于GNN的极化赋形框架显著提升FBL和速率并降低最大DEP，在信道去极化与不完美CSI条件下增益尤为明显。

Conclusion: 自适应极化控制对可靠低时延传输具有较大潜力，基于异构图神经网络的联合波束成形与极化赋形可实现高效在线决策。

Abstract: This paper investigates polarization-aware transmission for multi-user multiple-input single-output (MU-MISO) short-packet ultra-reliable low-latency communications (URLLC) under imperfect channel state information (CSI). We consider a system in which the base station (BS) and users are equipped with polarization-reconfigurable antennas that enable adaptive polarization states through controllable polarization coefficients. A multi-objective optimization problem is formulated to jointly maximize the finite-blocklength (FBL) achievable sum rate and minimize the maximum decoding error probability (DEP), subject to transmit-power, latency, reliability, and discrete polarization-control constraints. The resulting multi-objective problem is scalarized using a normalized weighted-sum utility. To enable low-complexity online decision-making, a heterogeneous graph neural network (GNN) is developed to learn the joint mapping from estimated polarized CSI to digital beamforming and transmit/receive polarforming vectors (PFVs) while accounting for the system constraints. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed GNN-based polarforming (PF) framework substantially improves the FBL sum rate while reducing the maximum DEP compared with conventional fixed-polarization schemes, particularly under channel depolarization and imperfect CSI. This highlights the potential of adaptive polarization control for reliable low-latency transmission.

</details>


### [88] [Finite-Mask Gaussian-Process Reconstruction on a Periodic Sensor Ring: Mask-Geometry Dependence, Fourier-Mode Coupling, and Posterior Trace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14007)
*Jun Tsuzurugi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 分析周期环上传感器观测掩膜对高斯过程重构的后验协方差、傅里叶模态耦合及归一化后验迹的影响，指出掩膜几何导致相同缺失比例下后验迹不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有完整观测情形下各傅里叶模态后验相互独立，但实际传感器阵列常存在失效、饱和、损坏或丢包等缺失通道，需探讨任意观测掩膜如何破坏模态独立性并影响重构不确定性。

Method: 在旋转平稳先验与均匀独立测量噪声假设下，针对等间距传感器的有限周期环，构造掩膜矩阵 Q_M=F D_M F*，分析其非对角元（掩膜的有限傅里叶分量）对后验精度矩阵的模态耦合作用，并以64通道合成基准验证。

Result: 完整观测时各傅里叶模态后验为独立标量公式；任意掩膜下 Q_M 一般非对角，导致模态耦合，且缺失比例相同的不同掩膜几何会产生不同的归一化后验迹，64通道合成基准展示了该有限矩阵效应。

Conclusion: 该分析是旋转平稳性与共噪声假设下的有限维参考计算，并非非均匀或不等间距仪器化运行管道的性能声明；可为环形麦克风阵列等机械工程场景中通道失效影响提供理论参照。

Abstract: We analyze how an arbitrary observation mask on a finite periodic ring of equally spaced sensors affects the posterior covariance, Fourier-mode coupling, and normalized posterior trace in Gaussian-process reconstruction. Under a rotationally stationary prior and homogeneous independent measurement noise, complete observation gives independent scalar posterior formulas for the Fourier modes. For an arbitrary mask, however, the matrix $Q_M=F D_MF^\ast$ is generally non-diagonal; its off-diagonal entries are finite Fourier components of the realized mask and couple modes in the posterior precision. Consequently, masks with the same unavailable-channel fraction can have different normalized posterior traces because their geometries differ. A dimensionless 64-channel synthetic benchmark illustrates this finite-matrix effect. A circumferential array of equally spaced wall-mounted microphones at a fixed axial station of a circular fan or compressor duct provides one concrete mechanical-engineering interpretation: failed, saturated, corrupted, or dropped-out channels form the observation mask. The analysis is a finite-dimensional reference calculation under the stated rotational-stationarity and common-noise assumptions, not a performance claim for a nonuniform or unequally instrumented operating duct.

</details>


### [89] [Polarforming-Enabled Power-Splitting SWIPT: A GNN-Based Optimization Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14014)
*Hamed Aghaei-Karkaj,Kamran Ebrahimi,Zahra Mehrzad,Mohammad Robat Mili,Symeon Chatzinotas,Ioannis Krikidis*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出在SWIPT系统中引入动态极化控制（polarforming）作为额外自由度，基站与用户端均可调节天线极化，并结合功率分割实现同步信息解码与能量收集；针对高耦合非凸优化问题，采用图神经网络（GNN）学习次优的波束成形、极化及功率分割变量。仿真表明，GNN动态polarforming方案显著优于固定极化方案，尤其在非理想CSI下，且在极化失配与CSI不完美条件下保持鲁棒性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWIPT是未来IoT的关键技术，但此类网络中稳定的功率供给仍是重大挑战。既有方案多依赖传统自由度，难以同时兼顾信息传输与能量收集的效率与鲁棒性，因此需要引入新的自由度并有效求解高维耦合优化问题。

Method: 将动态极化控制（polarforming）作为SWIPT的额外自由度；基站与用户均可调整天线极化，用户设备对入射信号进行功率分割以同时进行ID与EH；将该非凸且变量高度耦合的优化问题交由GNN求解，学习次优的波束成形、极化与功率分割变量。

Result: 仿真结果显示，所提出的基于GNN的动态polarforming优化显著优于固定极化方案，尤其在非理想CSI条件下优势明显；联合polarforming与GNN优化在极化失配和不完美CSI下均能保持稳健的SWIPT性能。

Conclusion: 动态极化控制为SWIPT提供了有效的新自由度，GNN可有效处理其复杂耦合优化，使系统在极化失配与非理想CSI下仍具鲁棒性，验证了该联合设计方案的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Simultaneous wireless information and power transfer (SWIPT) is a critical technology for the future of the Internet of Things (IoT). However, ensuring a stable power supply in such networks remains a significant challenge. This work introduces dynamic polarization control as an additional degree of freedom (DoF) in SWIPT systems. We propose a system where both the base station (BS) and the users can adjust their antenna polarization, a technique known as polarforming. In addition, each user device is capable of splitting the incident signal to perform simultaneous information decoding (ID) and energy harvesting (EH). The resulting non-convex optimization, with many coupled variables, is solved using a graph neural network (GNN) that learns the sub-optimal beamforming, polarization, and power-splitting variables. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed GNN-based dynamic polarforming optimization significantly outperforms fixed-polarization schemes, particularly under imperfect channel state information (CSI). Moreover, joint polarforming and GNN-based optimization maintain robust SWIPT performance under both polarization mismatch and imperfect CSI.

</details>


### [90] [Beam Squint and Aperture--Bandwidth Limitations in Wideband RIS-Assisted THz Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14037)
*Waqas Khalid,Chiew Foong Kwong,David Chieng,Qianyu Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该论文研究波束斜视与有限相位量化下宽带RIS辅助太赫兹链路的遍历速率，通过精确信道矩推导与矩匹配给出闭式速率近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽带太赫兹RIS系统中，频率相关的阵列响应会引起波束斜视，而有限分辨率相位控制会引入量化误差，二者共同破坏频率平坦式RIS控制假设。本文旨在量化这些损伤对系统遍历速率与孔径—带宽限制的影响。

Method: 建立两跳RIS辅助链路模型：两跳经历独立Rayleigh衰落，形成双Rayleigh级联幅度；在信道中纳入频率相关阵列响应与相位量化误差，推导精确的二阶与四阶信道矩。继而采用矩匹配，将每个子载波接收功率近似为Gamma分布，得到宽带遍历速率的可解析闭式近似。同时用首零点带宽近似刻画频率平坦RIS控制的孔径—带宽限制。

Result: 给出了各子载波接收功率的Gamma近似及宽带遍历速率的闭式近似；首零点带宽近似定量表征了孔径—带宽限制；Monte Carlo仿真验证了理论框架的准确性。

Conclusion: 该分析框架能够在波束斜视与相位量化条件下有效评估宽带RIS辅助太赫兹链路的遍历速率，并为频率平坦RIS控制的带宽设计提供理论依据。

Abstract: This paper analyzes the ergodic rate of a wideband reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-assisted terahertz link under beam squint and finite-resolution phase control. Independent Rayleigh fading on the two RIS-assisted hops produces double-Rayleigh cascaded amplitudes. Exact second- and fourth-order channel moments are derived by incorporating the frequency-dependent array response and phase-quantization errors. Moment matching then provides a Gamma approximation for the received power on each subcarrier and a tractable closed-form approximation for the wideband ergodic rate. A first-null bandwidth approximation characterizes the aperture--bandwidth limitation of frequency-flat RIS control. Monte Carlo simulations validate the analytical framework.

</details>


### [91] [Successive Refinement Under Strong-Sense Perfect Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14404)
*Yu Yang,Changhong Liu,Weijie Yuan,Lin Zhou*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文在无限制公共随机性下，研究了多终端有损信源编码中的逐次细化问题，在强意义完美感知约束下推导了率-失真-感知区域，并以伯努利信源为例验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐次细化问题允许两个不同解码器以不同失真水平恢复信源序列，但已有结果忽略了感知质量的影响。本文旨在分析强意义完美感知约束对率-失真-感知区域的影响，从而细化已有结论。

Method: 在存在无限制公共随机性的条件下，对逐次细化问题施加强意义完美感知约束；可达性证明受输出约束有损信源编码启发，逆定理证明改编标准逐次细化问题的证明步骤；并给出伯努利信源的数值算例。

Result: 推导出强意义完美感知约束下的率-失真-感知区域；数值算例表明，伯努利信源在汉明失真下即使在强意义完美感知约束下仍是逐次可细化的。

Conclusion: 感知质量约束会影响逐次细化的可达区域，但伯努利信源在汉明失真下仍保持逐次可细化性，说明强意义完美感知约束与原逐次细化框架可兼容。

Abstract: We revisit a multiterminal lossy source coding problem named successive refinement and derive the rate-distortion-perception region under the strong-sense perfect perception constraint in the presence of unlimited common randomness. Specifically, in successive refinement, one aims to compress a source sequence and allows two distinct decoders to recover the source sequence at different distortion levels. By imposing the strong-sense perfect perception constraint, our results refine the previous result by analyzing the impact of the perceptual quality. Our achievability proof is inspired by output constrained lossy source coding and our converse proof adapts the proof steps of the standard successive refinement problem. Furthermore, we provide a numerical example of the Bernoulli source to illustrate our result and show that the Bernoulli source under Hamming distortion is successively refinable even with the strong-sense perfect perception constraint.

</details>


### [92] [Dependency, Compression, and Synergy: A Unified Information-Theoretic View of Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14421)
*Liangjian Wen,Linjie Li,Jiang Duan,Yong Dai,Jianzhuang Liu,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 这是一篇以信息论视角统一多模态学习的综述，将互信息（MI）、信息瓶颈（IB）与部分信息分解（PID）串为递进的信息处理框架，覆盖170项近期研究，并提出广义多模态信息拉格朗日坐标系统来组织现有范式与指示空白方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态基础模型快速发展，迫切需要理解不同模态如何共享、保持与互补信息。MI、IB、PID虽提供互补视角，但既有研究多将其视为彼此孤立的工具，缺乏统一理解与统一坐标系。

Method: 综述性研究：系统梳理170项近期研究（2018–2026）与12项奠基性工作；以信息论原理为组织主线，围绕跨模态对齐、信息高效融合、交互类型刻画、扩展至多模态基础模型四大挑战展开；不以应用领域为主分类轴，而将医疗、机器人、推荐系统、情感计算、无线通信解释为信息原理的经验验证；提出广义多模态信息拉格朗日（Generalized Multimodal Information Lagrangian）作为统一坐标系统，将现有范式映射到精确或近似参数角点，并用未占据区域命名尚未构建的候选方法族。

Result: 建立了MI—IB—PID的递进式统一视角：MI刻画模态间依赖，IB解释压缩下的任务导向信息保持，PID将保持的信息分解为冗余、独特与协同；提出统一坐标框架定位现有范式并识别空白；讨论新兴多模态基础模型如何在规模上实例化这些原理。

Conclusion: 信息论原理可作为多模态学习的统一组织框架，并指向开放挑战：高维情形下的可扩展信息估计、跨信息论方法的标准化评估、多模态PID的组合复杂性，以及从后验信息分析转向信息感知的多模态学习。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal foundation models have intensified the need to understand how different modalities share, preserve, and complement information. Mutual Information (MI), the Information Bottleneck (IB), and Partial Information Decomposition (PID) provide complementary perspectives, yet existing studies often treat them as isolated tools. This survey presents an information-theoretic perspective connecting these principles as progressively refined views of multimodal information processing: MI characterizes inter-modal dependency, IB explains task-oriented information preservation under compression, and PID decomposes preserved information into redundancy, uniqueness, and synergy. We review 170 recent studies (2018--2026) and 12 foundational works, organizing multimodal learning around four challenges: cross-modal alignment, information-efficient fusion, interaction-type characterization, and scaling to multimodal foundation models. Rather than using application domains as primary taxonomy axes, we interpret healthcare, robotics, recommendation systems, affective computing, and wireless communications as empirical validations of these information principles. Beyond taxonomy, we organize existing multimodal paradigms within a single information-theoretic coordinate system -- the Generalized Multimodal Information Lagrangian -- in which they occupy exact or approximate parameter corners, and whose unoccupied regions name candidate method families the literature has not yet built. We further discuss how emerging multimodal foundation models instantiate these principles at scale and identify open challenges including scalable information estimation in high-dimensional settings, standardized evaluation across information-theoretic methods, combinatorial complexity of multimodal PID, and the transition from post-hoc information analysis toward information-aware multimodal learning.

</details>


### [93] [Hermitian Duality and Syndrome Structure of CSS-Type Codes over the ring $\mathbb{Z}_q[i]$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14434)
*Akanksha Tiwari,Ritumoni Sarma*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究环 R_q=Z_q[i]（q 的所有素因子 ≡1 mod 4）上的主理想码与 CSS 型稳定子构造，给出主理想及其零化子的基数公式、Hermitian 对偶刻画 C^{⊥_H}=Ann(σ(α))，并证明嵌套码下的 X-错误伴随子核为 C_2，进而用商群参数化逻辑 X 算子与物理 X 错误类，构造可纠错的 X 型错误族与基于伴随子的恢复流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该环上构造虽已被用于经典与量子纠错，但主理想基数、Hermitian 对偶、代数陪集与稳定子伴随子之间的精确关系尚缺乏系统性结构分析，需要厘清以支撑稳定子码的设计与解码。

Method: 在 R_q=Z_q[i]（素因子 ≡1 mod 4）上研究主理想码的代数结构：推导主理想与其零化子的基数公式；对长度一主理想码 C=⟨α⟩ 证明其 Hermitian 对偶为 C^{⊥_H}=Ann(σ(α))；对满足 C_2^{⊥_H}⊆C_1⊆C_2 的嵌套码确定对应 CSS 型稳定子，并分析 X-错误伴随子映射 Syn_X 的核与商群结构，据此构造 C_2 在 R_q^n 中的代表元横截集。

Result: 得到主理想与零化子的基数公式；证明 C^{⊥_H}=Ann(σ(α))；证明 ker(Syn_X)=C_2；指出 C_2/C_1 参数化逻辑 X 算子类、R_q^n/C_2 参数化物理 X 错误伴随子类；利用伴随子商群构造出元素伴随子两两不同的 C_2 横截集，该 X 型错误族可由稳定子伴随子测量纠正，并给出与稳定子结构一致的基于伴随子的恢复流程；通过显式例子展示对偶、基数与伴随子结构。

Conclusion: 在 R_q=Z_q[i]（q 素因子 ≡1 mod 4）框架下，主理想的 Hermitian 对偶、基数与零化子关系得到精确刻画，CSS 型稳定子的 X-错误伴随子核恰为 C_2，逻辑与物理 X 类可由商群清晰参数化，从而为构造可纠错 X 型错误族及基于伴随子的恢复方案提供了代数基础。

Abstract: We study the algebraic structure of principal ideal codes and CSS-type stabilizer constructions over the ring $
R_q=\mathbb Z_q[i],$ where every prime divisor of $q$ is congruent to $1$ modulo $4$. Although such ring-based constructions have recently been considered in the context of classical and quantum error correction, the precise relationship among principal ideal cardinalities, Hermitian duality, algebraic cosets, and stabilizer syndromes requires further structural analysis.
  We obtain cardinality formulas for principal ideals and their annihilators. For a length-one principal ideal code $C=\langleα\rangle$, we show that its Hermitian dual is $
C^{\perp_H}=\operatorname{Ann}(σ(α)). $ For nested codes satisfying $
C_2^{\perp_H}\subseteq C_1\subseteq C_2, $
we determine the corresponding CSS-type stabilizer and prove that the kernel of the physical $X$-error syndrome map is $
\ker(\operatorname{Syn}_X)=C_2. $
Consequently, $C_2/C_1$ parametrizes logical $X$-operator classes and $R_q^n/C_2$ parametrizes physical $X$-error syndrome classes. Using this syndrome quotient, we construct a transversal of $C_2$ in $R_q^n$ whose elements have pairwise distinct physical $X$-error syndromes. The corresponding $X$-type error family is correctable by stabilizer syndrome measurement, yielding a syndrome-based recovery procedure that is consistent with the stabilizer structure. Explicit examples illustrate the resulting duality, cardinality, and syndrome structure.

</details>


### [94] [Channel-Aware Selection of Folded Bloom Filters for Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14458)
*John Cartmell,Mihaela Cardei,Ionut Cardei*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究在通信受限的分布式系统中，如何根据信道条件在满足最大假阳性率约束的前提下，对OR折叠式Bloom filter表示进行自适应选择，以提升传输效率与接收端新鲜度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性传输Bloom filter会带来显著的通信开销。无损压缩虽保持成员行为，但只能提供单一传输尺寸；而OR折叠可获得更小的表示并提高假阳性率（FPR），同时保持无假阴性特性。现有折叠方法主要由基数和假阳性约束驱动，未考虑时变通信条件，因此需要研究信道感知的折叠表示选择机制。

Method: 发送端保留不变的规范filter，构建满足最大FPR的候选目录；在每个上报机会，根据当时可用通信资源，从满足FPR约束的表示中选择保留长度最大的版本进行传输。该选择过程由时变通信条件驱动，而非仅由基数与假阳性约束决定。采用两个钓鱼URL数据集，并在五状态马尔可夫容量、Gilbert–Elliott突发错误和Rayleigh块衰落三种信道模型下评估该框架。

Result: 当通信机会变化显著时，信道感知折叠在通信效率和接收端新鲜度上优于完整filter与无损压缩基线；在条件更优的Gilbert–Elliott模型下，它在保持最高接收端新鲜度的同时，效率仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当较新的低保真度更新优于延迟传输更大表示时，满足FPR约束的折叠视图可提供有用的传输工作点，为通信受限环境下的Bloom filter传输提供了一种信道自适应的折中方案。

Abstract: Periodic Bloom-filter transmission can impose substantial overhead in communication-constrained distributed systems. Lossless compression preserves membership behavior but provides a single transmission size, whereas established OR folding produces smaller representations with higher false-positive rates (FPRs) while preserving the no-false-negative property.
  This paper investigates channel-aware selection among OR-folded representations. The sender retains an unchanged canonical filter, constructs a catalog satisfying a maximum FPR, and selects the FPR-qualified representation with the largest retained length supported by the communication resources available at each reporting opportunity. Unlike folding driven principally by cardinality and false-positive constraints, selection is driven by time-varying communication conditions.
  Using two phishing URL datasets, the framework is evaluated under Five-State Markov Capacity, Gilbert--Elliott burst-error, and Rayleigh block-fading models. Channel-aware folding improves communication efficiency and receiver freshness relative to complete-filter and lossless-compression baselines when communication opportunities vary substantially. Under the more favorable Gilbert--Elliott model, it remains competitive in efficiency while maintaining the freshest receiver state. These results show that FPR-qualified folded views provide useful transmission operating points when a recent lower-fidelity update is preferable to delaying a larger representation.

</details>


### [95] [On the Generalized Packing and Covering Radii of Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14477)
*Wenjun Yu,Moshe Schwartz*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了广义填充半径不超过同阶广义覆盖半径这一猜想在二阶情形成立，在码率不超过3/5时对所有阶成立，并证明对任意码率(0,1)下足够长的码该猜想对所有阶成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小距离与覆盖半径是码的两个基本性质，分别被推广为广义Hamming重量层级和广义覆盖半径层级；几何上最小距离决定填充半径且填充半径不超过覆盖半径，据此猜想该关系在所有阶上成立，即同阶广义填充半径不超过广义覆盖半径。论文旨在求证该猜想。

Method: 从几何视角出发，基于码的广义覆盖半径层级与广义填充半径层级，对二阶半径进行证明；并对码率不超过3/5的情形证明所有阶成立；进一步结合渐近分析，对任意码率(0,1)下足够长的码证明所有阶成立。

Result: 猜想在二阶半径情形下成立；当码率至多为3/5时对所有阶成立；对任意码率在(0,1)区间内、所有足够长的码，猜想对所有阶成立。

Conclusion: 该工作证实了广义填充半径与广义覆盖半径之间猜想的多种情形，支持了这一层级关系在一般码参数下的普遍性。

Abstract: The minimum distance and the covering radius are two fundamental properties of the code. Both have been extended: the former to the generalized Hamming weights hierarchy, and the latter to the generalized covering radii hierarchy. In both cases, the lowest level of the hierarchies corresponds to the classical minimum distance and covering radius, respectively. From a geometric point of view, the minimum distance of the code determines the packing radius, which is upper bounded by the covering radius. It was conjectured this relation extends to all other orders of the hierarchy, namely, that the generalized packing radii are upper bounded by the generalized covering radii of the same order.
  In this paper we prove this conjecture is true for the second order radii. We also prove the conjecture holds for all orders when the code rate is at most $3/5$. Finally, we show that for any code rate in $(0,1)$, for all sufficiently long codes the conjecture holds for all orders.

</details>


### [96] [Universal Estimation of the Fisher Information for Processes with Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14582)
*Yibo Shi,Cristian R. Rojas*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅依赖模拟器输出、无需已知记忆长度的费舍尔信息率估计方法，基于FIM与KL散度局部曲率的恒等关系，用CTW估计方向性散度率并以最小二乘恢复矩阵，证明了强一致性与有限样本界（平均误差阶n^{-1/3}），实验验证了预测指数、深度无关的精度稳定性及采样预算规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费舍尔信息矩阵（FIM）决定估计问题的可达精度，但对有记忆的参数过程，仅有窄类模型存在闭式解；当模型仅以模拟器形式可得时无法直接计算。因此需要一种仅从模拟器输出估计费舍尔信息率（FIM的每观测极限）且无需知道记忆长度的方法。

Method: 利用FIM与KL散度局部曲率之间的恒等关系：用上下文树加权（CTW）估计方向性散度率，再通过最小二乘从这些方向性散度率恢复FIM矩阵。整个过程不依赖记忆长度，仅使用模拟器输出。

Result: 在迭代极限下建立了强一致性，并给出对所有不小于真实记忆长度的工作深度一致成立的有限样本界，平均误差阶为n^{-1/3}。分析还给出基于CTW的KL散度率估计的有限样本速率。实验验证了预测的指数，表明精度不随工作深度增大而退化，并可用估计值规划达到指定精度的采样预算；估计器在无有限记忆长度的隐状态源上仍保持准确。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种从模拟器输出估计费舍尔信息率的可行途径，兼具理论保证（强一致性与有限样本界）与实用价值（采样预算规划），且对超出有限记忆假设的隐状态源具有一定鲁棒性。

Abstract: The Fisher information matrix (FIM) determines the accuracy attainable in an estimation problem. For a parametric process with memory, it has a closed form only for narrow classes of models, and it cannot be computed when the model is available only as a simulator. This paper estimates the Fisher information rate of such a process, the per-observation limit of its FIM, from simulator output alone and without knowledge of the memory length. The construction uses the identity between the FIM and the local curvature of the Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence. Directional divergence rates are estimated by context-tree weighting (CTW), and the matrix is recovered from them by least squares. We establish strong consistency in an iterated limit and a finite-sample bound, uniform over every working depth at least as large as the true memory length, which gives a mean error of order $n^{-1/3}$. The analysis also delivers a finite-sample rate for CTW-based estimation of the KL divergence rate. Experiments confirm the predicted exponents, show that the accuracy does not degrade as the working depth grows, and use the estimate to plan a sampling budget that attains a prescribed precision. The estimator stays accurate on a hidden-state source that has no finite memory length.

</details>


### [97] [Fast-Slow Communication with Endogenous Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14680)
*Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了快慢双流通信架构中，快通道在消耗共享资源的同时改变慢通道传输规律的问题，刻画了容量区域并证明了互补性与竞争的判定准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多生物和工程系统中，紧急信息通过快速物理流传输，详细信息通过较慢的物质流传输，但快速过程往往会改变慢速流的传输规律。作者希望在共享资源约束下理解这种耦合架构中两流之间的相互作用规律。

Method: 在严格递增凹的快通道容量-成本函数和带截止时间的慢速分子通道模型下，先刻画分离消息路由与消息无关工作点调度下的容量区域，给出互补与竞争的边际判据；对一维漂移扩散证明截止时间前到达概率关于Péclet数严格对数凹；对可区分令牌截止擦除通道，在任意递增凹传输驱动律下证明精确单交叉定理；并分析正基线漂移和强耦合下的临界归一化截止时间，以及短/长截止时间极限；最后用有限帧LTI-Poisson慢通道数值验证。

Result: 证明了到达概率关于Péclet数严格对数凹；给出单交叉定理：互补存在当且仅当初始传输辅助弹性大于1，且该转变唯一，凸化后递减分配分支仍是Pareto边界；在正基线漂移且耦合足够强时，存在唯一临界归一化截止时间决定互补消失的时机；短、长截止时间极限刻画了相应时间区域；数值实验显示有限帧LTI-Poisson慢通道呈现类似分配行为及计数噪声与符号间干扰。

Conclusion: 快慢流之间的互补而非竞争关系由初始传输辅助弹性是否超过1决定，且该转变具有唯一性；临界归一化截止时间刻画了互补性消失的条件。该框架为共享资源约束下耦合双流通信系统的资源分配提供了理论判据。

Abstract: A communication system may convey urgent information through a fast physical stream and more specific information through a slower material stream. In several biological and engineered settings, however, the fast process also changes the transport law of the slow one. We study this architecture under a shared resource constraint, with a strictly increasing concave fast-channel capacity--cost function and a deadline-constrained slow molecular channel. We first characterize the capacity region under separated message routing and message-independent operating-point schedules, and identify the marginal criterion for complementarity rather than competition between the streams. For one-dimensional drift diffusion, we prove that arrival probability before a deadline is strictly log-concave in Péclet number. For a distinguishable-token deadline-erasure channel, any increasing concave transport-actuation law then yields an exact single-crossing theorem: complementarity exists if and only if an initial transport-assistance elasticity exceeds one, the transition is unique when it exists, and the decreasing allocation branch remains the Pareto boundary after convexification. For positive baseline drift and sufficiently strong coupling, a unique critical normalized deadline determines when complementarity disappears. Short- and long-deadline limits clarify the associated temporal regimes. Numerical examples for a finite-frame LTI-Poisson slow channel exhibit analogous allocation behavior with counting noise and intersymbol interference.

</details>


### [98] [Task-Aware Beamforming for Semantic Wireless Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14801)
*Mohammad Mahdi Mojahedian,Alireza Pourafzal,Musa Furkan Keskin,Henk Wymeersch*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于CRB的框架，利用已知语义地图的局部几何来指导上行接收波束成形。以局部边界穿越作为语义误分类的代理，推导出法向信息最大化（NIM）准则，在单径LOS模型下将最优接收码本限制在匹配导向矢量及其角导数张成的子空间内，并在多区域地图与不确定先验位置下给出推广。数值结果显示NIM可显著降低语义误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多语义定位任务关注的是判定上下文中有意义的区域或状态，而非最小化完整笛卡尔位置估计的误差；因此总体定位精度与语义决策精度未必一致，传统的平方位置误差界（SPEB）设计并不针对语义判定优化，需要用任务相关的边界几何来指导测量资源分配。

Method: 以已知语义地图的局部几何为基础构建CRB框架；用局部边界穿越近似语义误分类，推导法向信息最大化（NIM），最小化沿局部语义边界法向的位置分量CRB。在单径LOS模型下，证明最优接收码本可限制在匹配导向矢量与其角导数张成的子空间，将设计简化为匹配模与导数模之间的测量资源分配，并对一般光滑边界导出分配方案（地理围栏与入侵检测为其径向、切向极限情形）。进一步通过距离归一化极小极大准则扩展到多区域语义地图，并通过最坏情况与先验加权鲁棒形式处理不确定先验位置；用有限时隙实现与基于profile ML估计的观测级蒙特卡洛仿真验证。

Result: 数值结果表明，NIM按任务相关的边界几何分配测量资源，在所研究场景中相比经典SPEB设计可大幅降低语义误差；经验结果与局部CRB的边界穿越近似在所研究工作区间内吻合良好。

Conclusion: 将波束成形/资源分配从总体定位精度导向转向语义决策导向是可行且有效的：以边界法向CRB为优化目标可在单径LOS下获得低维子空间内的显式最优分配，并可推广到多区域与先验不确定场景，为语义定位提供了新的设计准则。

Abstract: Many semantic localization tasks require determining a contextually meaningful region or state rather than minimizing the error of a full Cartesian position estimate. In such settings, aggregate localization accuracy need not align with the accuracy of the semantic decision. This paper develops a Cramér--Rao bound (CRB)-based framework that uses the local geometry of a known semantic map to guide uplink receive beamforming. Using local boundary crossing as a surrogate for semantic misclassification, we derive normal information maximization (NIM), which minimizes the CRB of the position component along the local semantic boundary normal. Under the considered single-path line-of-sight model, an optimal receive codebook can be restricted to the subspace spanned by the matched steering vector and its angular derivative, reducing the design to an allocation of measurement resources between matched and derivative spatial modes. We derive the resulting allocation for a general smooth boundary, with geofencing and intrusion detection arising as radial and tangential limiting cases. The framework is further extended to multi-region semantic maps through a distance-normalized minimax criterion and to uncertain prior locations through worst-case and prior-weighted robust formulations. Numerical results with finite-slot implementations and observation-level Monte Carlo simulations using profile maximum-likelihood (ML) estimation show that NIM allocates measurements according to the task-relevant boundary geometry and can substantially reduce semantic error relative to the classic squared position error bound (SPEB) design in the considered scenarios. The empirical results also closely follow the local CRB-based boundary-crossing approximation in the studied operating regime.

</details>


### [99] [Zero-SNR Analyticity of the Scalar MMSE Is Equivalent to Gaussianity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15048)
*Yixing Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 在平方指数矩条件下，标量MMSE在零信噪比处的解析性当且仅当输入为高斯分布；非高斯输入会导致形式展开式Gevrey-1但发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画高斯信道中最小均方误差（MMSE）在零信噪比处的光滑/解析性质与输入分布的关系，判断解析性是否可作为"高斯性"的判别准则，并推广到互信息等度量。

Method: 将高斯信道估计问题转化为作用于矩生成函数 M(z)=E[e^{zX}] 的逆向热流；在平方指数矩条件下证明非高斯输入使 M 产生非零复零点。通过局部化Borel变换分析零点簇在作用量 ξ=z0²/2 处产生的有限奇点：单零点给出 n^{-1/2} 前因子，m≥2 重零点按Hermite多项式根分裂并给出 n^{-m/2}e^{r_m√(2n)} 前因子；再用有限圆盘局部化与相对环延拓论证奇点在完整Borel变换中存活。

Result: 证明了 mmse_X(s) 在零信噪比处解析当且仅当 X 为高斯（含退化为常数的情形）；对所述类中任意非高斯输入，零信噪比形式展开为 Gevrey-1 但发散。作为推论得到 MMSE 的有理刚性，以及互信息对应的解析性判据。

Conclusion: 零信噪比处MMSE的解析性成为高斯输入的严格刻画；非高斯性表现为形式展开的发散（Gevrey-1），其机制由矩生成函数的复零点经Borel变换产生的奇点所控制，并推广至互信息。

Abstract: Let $Y_s=\sqrt{s}X+Z$, where $Z$ is standard Gaussian and independent of the real random variable $X$. We prove that, under the square-exponential moment condition $\mathbb{E}e^{βX^2}<\infty$ for some $β>0$, the scalar minimum mean-square error $\operatorname{mmse}_X(s)$ is analytic at zero signal-to-noise ratio if and only if $X$ is Gaussian, with constant random variables included as degenerate Gaussians.
  The proof converts estimation in the Gaussian channel into a backward heat flow acting on the moment-generating function $M(z)=\mathbb{E}e^{zX}$. Under the stated tail condition, every non-Gaussian input forces $M$ to have a nonzero complex zero. We show that each zero cluster produces a finite singularity in its localized Borel transform at the action $ξ=z_0^2/2$. After removing the action scale, the Borel coefficients have a nonzero $n^{-1/2}$ prefactor for a simple zero. A zero of multiplicity $m\geq 2$ splits according to the roots of a Hermite polynomial and instead contributes a prefactor $n^{-m/2}e^{r_m\sqrt{2n}}$. A finite-disc localization and relative-cycle continuation argument then show that at least one such singularity survives in the full Borel transform. Thus, for every non-Gaussian input in the stated class, the formal zero-SNR expansion is Gevrey-1 but divergent. Rational-MMSE rigidity and the analogous analyticity criterion for mutual information follow as corollaries.

</details>


### [100] [Free and projective LCD codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15423)
*Dominik Krasula*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 研究交换链环上LCD码的自由性问题，证明双边LCD码均为投射模；并将有限交换Frobenius环的刻画推广到非交换情形，进而证明群码为LCD当且仅当由中心幂等元生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交换链环上LCD码已知是自由的，但当环非不可分解时，存在投射但非自由的LCD理想。作者希望厘清LCD码与投射性的关系，并把交换情形下基于码与其对偶码大小条件的Frobenius环刻画推广到非交换环，以应用于群码的LCD判定。

Method: 先证明所有双边LCD码都是投射模；再借助码与其对偶码的大小条件，将有限交换Frobenius环的刻画推广至非交换环；最后将该结果用于群码，分析其与中心幂等元生成之间的关系。

Result: 证明了所有双边LCD码均为投射码；给出了非交换情形下有限Frobenius环基于码与对偶码大小条件的刻画；并证明群码是LCD当且仅当它由中心幂等元生成。

Conclusion: LCD码的投射性在双边情形普遍成立，突破了交换链环中自由性的限制；Frobenius环的大小条件刻画可自然推广到非交换环，并为群码的LCD性质提供了简洁的代数判别标准。

Abstract: LCD codes over finite commutative chain rings are known to be free. However, if the ring is not indecomposable, there always exists an LCD ideal that is projective but not free. We prove that all two-sided LCD codes are projective.
  We generalise the characterisation of finite commutative Frobenius rings by means of the size condition of a code and its dual to the noncommutative setting. This result is applied to prove that group codes are LCD if and only if they are generated by a central idempotent.

</details>


### [101] [Differential-linear profiles over finite fields of arbitrary characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15445)
*Kirpa Garg,Constanza Riera,Pantelimon Stănică*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该论文引入 p 元（素数特征有限域）差分-线性剖面（level-resolved p-ary differential-linear profile），将其与加法自相关联系起来，并研究其能量、反演、等价性等性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 DLCT 仅在二进制（p=2）情形下与加法自相关有简单对应，缺乏对任意素数特征有限域的系统刻画。作者希望把该关系推广到一般素数特征，并用新剖面刻画 DDT 行、差分均匀性乃至平面性等密码学性质。

Method: 对向量布尔函数与一般有限域函数，定义 p 元差分-线性剖面：其条目是某导数分量取指定迹值时的中心化输入个数；利用该剖面的离散傅里叶变换获得一族加法自相关（输出掩码乘 F_p 非零元）。给出由所有非零输出掩码剖面精确恢复 DDT 行的显式反演公式；建立二阶矩恒等式；分析单项式 x^{p^k+1} 与逆单项式；分析 EA-等价与 CCZ-等价下剖面的变换行为。

Result: 剖面的一阶傅里叶变换恢复经典加法自相关（p=2 回到二进制恒等式）；对固定输入差，所有非零输出掩码剖面可精确确定对应 DDT 行并给出反演公式；能量恒等式表明某导数方向的总剖面能量等于该 DDT 行与平衡行欧氏距离平方的常数倍，由完整行差分谱决定；方向内所有剖面消失当且仅当导数平衡，对奇数特征平方映射可刻画平面性；求出了 x^{p^k+1} 的完整剖面和逆单项式的精确 Kloosterman 和公式；证明 EA-等价重标号掩码并平移迹层次，一般 CCZ 等价可混合多个导数方向，但平方映射的全局非平凡剖面能量是 CCZ-不变量。

Conclusion: 该工作将差分-线性连通表与加法自相关的关系推广到任意素数特征，提出 p 元差分-线性剖面作为更精细的不变量；由此建立剖面、DDT 行、能量和平面性之间的联系，并给出具体函数的精确公式，同时厘清等价变换（EA 与 CCZ）对剖面的影响，表明平方映射的全局非平凡剖面能量具有 CCZ 不变性。

Abstract: The (binary) differential-linear connectivity table (DLCT) measures the dependence between an input difference and a linear mask applied to the corresponding output difference. For vectorial Boolean functions, each DLCT entry is one half of an additive autocorrelation value. We extend this relation to functions over finite fields of arbitrary prime characteristic by introducing a level-resolved p-ary differential-linear profile. Its entries are the centered numbers of inputs for which a derivative component has each prescribed trace value in $\mathbb{F}_p$. The discrete Fourier transform of this profile is the family of additive autocorrelations obtained by multiplying the output mask by the nonzero elements of $\mathbb{F}_p$; when p=2, the usual binary identity is recovered. For a fixed input difference, we show that the profiles over all nonzero output masks determine the corresponding DDT row exactly, and we give an explicit inversion formula. We establish a second-moment identity: the total profile energy in one derivative direction is a constant multiple of the squared Euclidean distance between that DDT row and the balanced row. Thus this energy is determined by the full row differential spectrum, not by differential uniformity alone. It follows that all profiles in a direction vanish exactly when the derivative is balanced; for square maps in odd characteristic, this gives a characterization of planarity. As concrete odd-characteristic examples, we determine the complete profile of the monomial $x^{p^k+1}$ and derive an exact Kloosterman-sum formula for the inverse monomial. Finally, we determine the behavior of the profiles under equivalence. EA-equivalence reindexes the input and output masks and translates the trace level, whereas a general CCZ equivalence may mix several derivative directions. Nevertheless, for square maps the global nontrivial profile energy is CCZ-invariant.

</details>


### [102] [Infinite families of 3-designs from linear and nonlinear codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15525)
*Shiyan Xiong,Xiaoqiang Wang,Dabin Zheng,Qinqin Ji*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 研究 $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$ 上一类线性码 $\mathcal{C}$，证明 $\mathcal{C}$ 中各非零重量码字及 $\mathcal{C}^{\perp}$ 中重量 4 码字的支撑构成 3-设计，相关非线性码 $\mathcal{C}_2$ 亦然，并给出量子码与局部可修复码应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码理论与组合 $t$-设计的联系是编码理论与组合数学交叉的重要课题；非线性码支撑 3-设计的例子相对稀少，需要进一步挖掘新的线性与非线性码类。

Method: 设 $q=p^m$（$p$ 奇素数，$m\geq 2$），在 $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$ 上构造线性码 $\mathcal{C}$，分析 $\mathcal{C}$ 与 $\mathcal{C}^{\perp}$ 的结构性质；并引入与 $\mathcal{C}$ 关联的非线性码 $\mathcal{C}_2$，利用支撑与设计的对应关系进行论证。

Result: 证明 $\mathcal{C}$ 中每个非零重量码字的支撑以及 $\mathcal{C}^{\perp}$ 中重量 4 码字的支撑均构成 3-设计；$\mathcal{C}_2$ 中每个非零 Hamming 重量码字的支撑也构成 3-设计。由 $\mathcal{C}^{\perp}$ 构造出参数为 $[[q+1,q-3,4;4]]_q$ 的纠缠辅助量子纠错码；$\mathcal{C}$ 是局域性为 3 的全符号局部可修复码，并达到局部可修复码的 Singleton 型界，在某些情形下是最优的。

Conclusion: 该工作识别出更多支撑组合 3-设计的线性与非线性码类，尤其为非线性码支撑 3-设计补充了实例，并给出量子纠错码与最优局部可修复码的应用。

Abstract: The connection between coding theory and combinatorial $t$-designs is an important research topic at the intersection of coding theory and combinatorics. Let $q=p^m$, where $p$ is an odd prime and $m\geq 2$. In this paper, we investigate a class of linear codes $\mathcal{C}$ over $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$ and their connection with combinatorial $3$-designs. By analyzing the relevant structural properties of $\mathcal{C}$ and $\mathcal{C}^{\perp}$, we show that the supports of the codewords of every nonzero weight in $\mathcal{C}$ and {the supports of the codewords of weight $4$ in $\mathcal{C}^{\perp}$} form $3$-designs. We further investigate a class of nonlinear codes $\mathcal{C}_2$ associated with $\mathcal{C}$, and prove that the supports of the codewords of every nonzero Hamming weight in $\mathcal{C}_2$ also form $3$-designs. These results identify further classes of linear and nonlinear codes whose codewords support combinatorial $3$-designs. In particular, the nonlinear case provides additional examples of codes supporting $3$-designs, a topic for which relatively few results are currently available. As applications, we construct from $\mathcal{C}^{\perp}$ an entanglement-assisted quantum error-correcting code with parameters $[[q+1,q-3,4;4]]_q$. We also prove that $\mathcal{C}$ is an all-symbol locally repairable code with locality $3$. Furthermore, we show that the code $\mathcal{C}$ {meets the Singleton-type bound for locally repairable codes} and hence is optimal in some cases.

</details>


### [103] [Spatially Coupled MacKay-Neal Codes Achieve Capacity on BMS Channels at Fixed Degrees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15526)
*Kenta Kasai*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文对二元输入无记忆对称信道上的空间耦合MacKay-Neal码建立了两个依赖度数的结论：r=g=3时在任意ℓ≥4、容量大于R=r/ℓ的信道下可实现速率趋于R且误码趋于零；r=g=2时部分BSC信道容量大于R却在终止密度进化下保持正误码。两者均由同一密度值势函数在非耦合不动点处的符号决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间耦合码的阈值饱和现象中，度数与容量、误码行为之间的关系尚缺严格刻画，特别是低度数（r=g=2）与较高度数（r=g=3）情形是否存在本质差异、以及势函数符号能否统一解释这两种行为，需要明确的理论结果。

Method: 采用均匀随机平滑、并在活动链外对两类变量进行缩短构造码集。度数三情形：结合ℓ≥33的解析界与4≤ℓ≤32的精确区间证书，再通过阈值饱和及实际速率的投影论证。度数二情形：解析构造一个势为负的不动点，并控制两端边界贡献。核心依据是同一密度值势在非耦合不动点处的符号，并将该势及度数二分条件联系到早期统计力学预测。有限证书与验证软件见带版本补充材料。

Result: 对r=g=3，任意整数ℓ≥4、任意容量大于R=r/ℓ的信道，存在码实现序列使其实际传输速率趋于R且平均和积比特误码趋于零。对r=g=2及ℓ∈{3,4,5}，存在一类BSC信道其容量大于R，但在终止密度进化下当链长相对耦合宽度增长时仍保持正传输比特误码。

Conclusion: 势函数在非耦合不动点处的符号可统一判定空间耦合MacKay-Neal码的阈值饱和是否发生，揭示了度数三与度数二情形的本质差异，并与既有统计力学预测一致；结果配有可复现的有限证书与验证软件。

Abstract: We establish two degree-dependent results for spatially coupled MacKay-Neal codes on binary-input memoryless symmetric channels. The ensembles use uniform random smoothing and shorten both variable types outside the active chain. For $r=g=3$, every integer $\ell\geq4$, and each channel of capacity greater than $R=r/\ell$, there is a sequence of code realizations whose actual transmitted rate tends to $R$ and whose average sum-product bit error tends to zero. For $r=g=2$ and each $\ell\in\{3,4,5\}$, an interval of binary symmetric channels has capacity greater than $R$ but retains positive transmitted-bit error under terminated density evolution as chain length grows relative to coupling width. Both results follow from the signs of the same density-valued potential at uncoupled fixed points. The degree-three proof combines analytic bounds for $\ell\geq33$ with exact interval certificates for $4\leq\ell\leq32$, followed by threshold saturation and a projection argument for the actual rate. The degree-two proof analytically constructs a fixed point with negative potential and controls both boundary contributions. We relate the potential and the degree-two bifurcation condition to earlier statistical-mechanical predictions. The finite certificates and verification software are available in a versioned supplement.

</details>


### [104] [An Overview of Rate-Distortion-Perception Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15580)
*Jun Chen,Ashish Khisti*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文综述了率-失真-感知（RDP）框架，梳理其从数学理论到实际部署的发展脉络，涵盖Blau–Michaeli函数的数学定义与编码定理、计算评估方法、替代性表述，以及面向现代图像与视频编码的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感知质量与传统失真度量（如MSE）之间存在不匹配，仅优化率-失真会损害感知质量，因此需要将感知因素纳入压缩理论框架，为新一代感知压缩系统提供坚实基础。

Method: 采用综述性研究方法：回顾Blau–Michaeli函数及相关编码定理的数学定义，考察其计算评估方法，讨论该框架的替代性表述，并归纳操作性原理与现代图像/视频编码的设计准则。

Result: 系统整理了RDP框架的理论定义、编码定理、计算方法与替代表述，并提炼出现代图像与视频编码的操作原理和设计指南。

Conclusion: RDP框架为开发下一代感知压缩系统提供了严格的理论基础，能够统一率、失真与感知质量三者的权衡。

Abstract: This paper provides a comprehensive overview of the rate-distortion-perception framework, tracing its evolution from mathematical theory to practical deployment. We review the mathematical definition of the the mathematical definition of the Blau--Michaeli function and the associated coding theorems, examine computational methods for its evaluation, and discuss alternative formulations of the framework. Operational principles and design guidelines for modern image and video coding are also presented, highlighting the rate-distortion-perception framework as a rigorous foundation for developing next-generation perceptual compression systems.

</details>


### [105] [The Capacity of the Relay Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15709)
*Jonathan Ponniah*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该文刻画了中继信道容量，通过三种方案（解码转发、协同压缩转发、非协同压缩转发）给出可达性与逆定理，证明完全基于典型序列性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中继信道容量长期缺乏统一、完整的刻画；不同转发策略各自给出部分可达速率，且缺乏基于典型序列的简洁证明框架。

Method: 提出三种编码方案：解码转发（decode-forward）、协同压缩转发（coordinated compress-forward）、非协同压缩转发（uncoordinated compress-forward）；利用典型序列（typical sequences）的联合典型性、条件典型性与覆盖引理等性质完成可达性与逆定理证明。

Result: 上述三种方案共同刻画了中继信道容量；所有结论均可由典型序列性质严格推出，无需额外的信息不等式工具。

Conclusion: 中继信道容量可由解码转发与两类压缩转发方案统一表征，典型序列方法是证明核心。

Abstract: The capacity of the relay channel is characterized by three schemes: decode-forward, coordinated compress-forward, and uncoordinated compress-forward. The proof relies entirely on properties of typical sequences.

</details>


### [106] [Private Information Retrieval With Arbitrary Privacy Requirements: Introduction and Capacity Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15875)
*Mohamed Nomeir,Shreya Meel,Sennur Ulukus*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出图存储系统下任意隐私需求的隐私信息检索（PIR）问题，统一并推广了经典PIR与局部PIR（LPIR），针对一般图、路径图、环图及新提出的金字塔存储图，给出了PIR容量的上下界与部分精确容量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机源于服务器存储受限、数据（消息）规模庞大以及数据隐私需求异构。经典PIR要求消息索引对所有服务器保密，LPIR仅要求对存储该消息的服务器保密，二者均为极端情形，缺乏对"仅需对指定子集服务器保密"这一普遍需求的刻画，故需建立更一般的隐私需求框架。

Method: 在图存储系统中定义任意隐私需求下的PIR：每条消息需从预先指定的服务器子集（必包含存储该消息的服务器）中私密检索，每个服务器对应一个隐私集（预先规定的、应向其私密检索的消息索引集合）。该框架将经典PIR（隐私集为全部消息索引）与LPIR（隐私集恰为所存消息索引集合）统一为特例。随后推导一般图在特定隐私需求下的PIR容量上下界，并针对路径图与环图（低度图便于深入分析且可容纳更多隐私需求设置）在若干细粒度设置下求出部分精确容量、建立其余情形上下界；最后引入金字塔存储图建模服务器存储与消息复制模式。

Result: 得到一般图在相应隐私需求下PIR容量的通用下界与上界，且该界同时体现LPIR与经典PIR的本质特征；针对路径图和环图，在所述设置及更细粒度设置下，部分情形给出精确容量结果，其余情形建立容量下界与上界；提出金字塔存储图，其具备良好的对称消息存储与复制结构，此前未在任何PIR文献中被研究。

Conclusion: 任意隐私需求下的图存储PIR构成了经典PIR与LPIR之间的桥梁，为异构隐私需求提供了统一建模与分析框架；路径图、环图等低度图适合深入理解新隐私公式，金字塔存储图则为服务器存储建模提供了具对称性的新结构，为后续容量刻画研究奠定基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the problem of private information retrieval (PIR) under arbitrary privacy requirements, in a graph-based storage system. This formulation is motivated by the server storage limitations, abundance of data (messages) and heterogeneous data privacy requirements. Under the arbitrary privacy requirement, each message has to be retrieved privately from a pre-specified subset of servers, where the subset always includes the servers storing it. Thus, each server is associated with a privacy set, which pre-specifies the message indices that should be privately retrieved from it. This setting is a generalization of the classical PIR setting, where the required message index needs to be kept private from all servers, i.e., there, the privacy set of each server comprises all message indices. Our setting is also a bridge between the newly formulated local PIR (LPIR) setting and the classical PIR setting, where in the former, the privacy set is exactly the set of stored message indices. In this paper, we derive general lower and upper bounds on the PIR capacity for general graphs, under certain privacy requirements, that capture the essence of both LPIR and classical PIR. Then, we focus on path and cyclic storage graphs under these and more fine-grained settings, for which we derive capacity results for certain cases, and establish lower and upper bounds for others. Their low degree allows for a more in-depth understanding of the new privacy formulation and admits more privacy requirement settings compared to other simple graphs. Finally, we introduce a new graph structure, the pyramid storage graph, to model server storage. Although this graph has never been investigated in the literature in any PIR context, it enjoys a nice symmetric structure for message storage and replication patterns.

</details>


### [107] [A Hardy-Space Proof of the Filter-Only Gaussian Feedback-Capacity Formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15977)
*Jun Su,Guangyue Han*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文在容量上确界层面证明了Kim提出的功率受限加性平稳高斯噪声信道反馈容量公式中独立平稳分量可被省略，从而闭合Derpich与Østergaard指出的原证明漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kim曾将功率受限加性平稳高斯噪声信道的反馈容量表述为独立平稳高斯分量频谱与严格因果反馈滤波器的无穷维优化，并断言独立分量可省略；但Derpich与Østergaard指出原证明存在缺陷，使仅含滤波器的容量公式推导不完整。本文旨在补全这一归约。

Method: 在噪声频谱有正下界时，采用规范谱分解将独立分量刻画为Schur函数偏离内函数的缺陷；构造在原点取值不变的有穷Blaschke乘积，得到输出谱完全相同且输入功率收敛的滤波器；再用功率回退论证满足原功率约束；最后以交错AR(1)例子将该归约与可达容量的SK(2)构造相联系。

Result: 成功在容量上确界层面建立该归约，证明独立平稳分量可从优化中省略而不改变容量上确界；交错AR(1)例子表明该归约与SK(2)构造相关。

Conclusion: 该一般逼近定理不要求也不保证由单一滤波器达到容量，即归约成立但可达性未被建立，仅针对上确界。

Abstract: The feedback capacity of power-constrained channels with additive stationary Gaussian noise was formulated by Kim in \cite{kim2010feedback} as an infinite-dimensional optimization over the spectrum of an independent stationary Gaussian component and a strictly causal feedback filter. Kim further asserted that the independent stationary component can be omitted. However, Derpich and Østergaard \cite{derpich2022comments} identified a gap in the original proof, leaving the original derivation of the filter-only capacity formula incomplete. In this paper, we establish this reduction at the level of capacity suprema. For the noise spectrum bounded away from zero, canonical spectral factorization represents the independent component as the defect of a Schur function from innerness. Finite Blaschke products that preserve its value at the origin produce filters with exactly the same output spectrum and convergent input powers. A power-backoff argument then enforces the original power constraint. Finally, an interleaved AR(1) example relates the reduction to the capacity-achieving SK(2) construction. The general approximation theorem does not require or establish attainment by a single filter.

</details>
